### [1] [Bayesian optimization of longitudinally aligned CNT targets for 800 nm laser wakefield acceleration in the bubble regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38810)
*B. S. Nunes, P. Martín-Luna, J. Rodríguez-Pérez, A. Sytov, B. Lei, G. Xia, J. Resta-López, M. S. Alva-Sánchez, N. D. Vieira, R. E. Samad, A. Bonatto*

分类： physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文用三维粒子网格模拟结合贝叶斯优化，研究方形截面三角晶格排布、可选中心孔径的纵向排列碳纳米管靶，在固定100 mJ、少周期800 nm激光下优化固态激光尾场加速的电子束能量转换效率；带孔径最优靶效率24.6%，无孔径靶最高25.8%，并论证无孔径靶更易开展原理验证实验。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 固态密度等离子体中的激光尾场加速有望获得TeV/m量级加速梯度，但需使驱动激光与等离子体匹配；纳米结构固体可通过调节结构间距降低有效密度，使红外脉冲激发气泡区尾场。已有研究在同心圆柱壳上验证碳纳米管束方案，本文进一步研究更简单、实验上更易实现的纵向排列碳纳米管靶，以降低靶制备难度并推动固态激光尾场加速的原理验证。

方法： 采用三维粒子网格模拟与贝叶斯优化相结合，针对固定的100 mJ、少周期、800 nm激光脉冲，优化方形截面内三角晶格排布的纵向排列碳纳米管束靶；优化变量包括有效密度、靶长度、管束半径和中心孔径半径，目标是最大化能量高于20 MeV电子的激光到束流能量转换效率。研究分别考察孔径半径不小于0.5 μm和孔径可消失两类情况，并与相同条件下同心壳层靶比较，同时检验优化靶对保持有效密度的随机管束位移的稳健性。

结果： 孔径半径约束为至少0.5 μm时，最优靶处于气泡区，转换效率达24.6%，产生673 pC束团，平均能量36.6 MeV，准单能峰约46 MeV；相同条件下同心壳层靶效率为19%。允许孔径消失时，优化趋向更高密度和更短靶，效率峰值25.8%，最佳配置电荷更高，但峰值能量较低、发散度约增大50%；无孔径靶的平均效率为24.1%。优化靶对保持有效密度的随机管束位移具有稳健性。

结论： 纵向排列碳纳米管靶可在模拟中实现高效固态激光尾场加速，带孔径最优靶效率24.6%，无孔径优化靶效率峰值25.8%且平均效率24.1%。由于无需加工微米尺度开口，无孔径靶可简化靶制备，并可能促进固态激光尾场加速的原理验证实验；摘要未给出实验验证结果。

原始摘要： Laser wakefield acceleration (LWFA) in solid-density plasmas can reach accelerating gradients in the TeV/m range, well above those of gaseous media, provided the driver is matched to the plasma. In nanostructured solids, this matching can be achieved by controlling the spacing between the nanostructures to reduce the effective density, enabling infrared pulses to excite wakefields in the bubble regime. Following numerical demonstrations with carbon nanotube (CNT) bundles on concentric cylindrical shells, a simpler, experimentally more accessible longitudinally aligned CNT (LA-CNT) target is investigated here, with the bundles on a triangular lattice within a square cross section and an optional central aperture. Three-dimensional particle-in-cell simulations are coupled with Bayesian optimization to vary the effective density, target length, bundle radius and aperture radius for a fixed 100 mJ, few-cycle, 800 nm laser pulse, maximizing the laser-to-beam energy conversion efficiency for electrons above 20 MeV. With the aperture radius constrained to at least 0.5 $μ$m, the best target operates in the bubble regime and reaches an efficiency of 24.6%, delivering a 673 pC bunch with a mean energy of 36.6 MeV and a quasi-monoenergetic peak near 46 MeV, against 19% for the concentric-shell target under identical conditions. When the aperture is allowed to vanish, the optimization converges to denser and shorter targets, the efficiency peaks at 25.8%, and the best configurations deliver a higher charge at the cost of a lower peak energy and about 50% larger divergence; aperture-free targets among them retain a mean efficiency of 24.1%. The optimized targets are robust to random bundle displacements that preserve the effective density. By eliminating the need to pattern a micrometre-scale opening, aperture-free targets may facilitate proof-of-principle experiments on solid-state LWFA.

</details>


### [2] [NEOPIC: A Neural Operator Framework for Particle-based Kinetic Plasma Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40528)
*Sriramkrishnan Muralikrishnan, Chelsea Maria John, Jeffrey Kelling, Michael Bussmann, Robert Speck, Paul Gibbon*

分类： physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: NEOPIC 是一种用于粒子型动理学等离子体模拟的免网格、免树神经算子场求解器：它直接作用于粒子的经验测度并在粒子位置求值，可即插即用地替代场求解，理论上保持蒙特卡洛粒子方法的误差结构，并在多种静电基准上验证了稳定性与泛化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 粒子模拟中的场求解通常依赖网格或树结构；网格型神经算子需要将粒子沉积到网格，易使主导输入的粒子噪声发生混叠。论文旨在学习粒子型方法中的场算子，避免网格、树和沉积步骤，同时保持与现有粒子方法及时间积分器的兼容性。

方法： 提出 NEOPIC 框架，其核心是作用于粒子经验测度、直接在粒子位置求值的神经算子，无需网格或树，也不进行网格沉积。它仅替代场求解，可作为即插即用场求解器，训练数据可来自 particle-in-cell、particle-in-Fourier 或 tree 代码，并可结合任意时间积分器，包括隐式格式。论文证明任意粒子数下离散求值与连续算子一致的表示等价性，给出分离采样误差、学习误差和时间步进误差的分析，并分析训练数据中粒子噪声作为隐式正则化的作用及 O(N_p) 复杂度。

结果： 理论结果表明：在扣除学习误差后，NEOPIC 具有标准蒙特卡洛粒子方法的误差结构；其复杂度为 O(N_p)，常数仅由架构决定，与网格或树无关；训练数据中的粒子噪声可作为隐式正则化，为训练粒子数与推理粒子数的关系提供指导。二维和三维静电基准实验——包括 Landau damping、two-stream、bump-on-tail、cyclotron mini-app 和 Penning trap——确认了理论发现；一个在由不同参考方法生成的多个 mini-app 上训练的单一模型能够覆盖所有这些情形，并在训练时间窗的两倍范围内保持稳定。摘要未给出具体定量误差、加速比或计算成本数值。

结论： NEOPIC 可作为粒子型动理学等离子体模拟的免网格、即插即用神经场求解器，在理论上保持蒙特卡洛粒子方法的误差结构并具备 O(N_p) 复杂度；实验结果支持其稳定性、跨 mini-app 泛化能力及面向粒子方法场求解基础模型的发展方向。

原始摘要： We introduce NEOPIC, a framework for learning the field operator in particle-based methods applied to kinetic plasma simulations. Its key novelty is a neural operator that acts directly on the empirical measure of the particles, rather than on a grid, and is evaluated at the particle positions. No mesh or tree is needed and, unlike grid-based neural operators, no deposition onto a mesh takes place, so the particle noise that dominates the inputs of particle simulations is not aliased. Since it replaces only the field solve, it serves as a plug-and-play field solver for particle-based methods: it can be trained on data from particle-in-cell, particle-in-Fourier or tree codes, and combined with any time integrator, including implicit schemes. We prove that the discrete evaluation coincides with the continuous operator at any number of particles (representation equivalence) and derive an error analysis that separates sampling, learning and time-stepping errors: up to the learning error, NEOPIC has the error structure of standard Monte Carlo particle methods. We show that the particle noise in the training data acts as an implicit regularizer, which gives guidance on the number of training particles in relation to the particle numbers used at inference. A complexity analysis shows that NEOPIC is an $O(N_p)$ method whose constants are set by the architecture alone, independently of any mesh or tree. Numerical experiments on electrostatic benchmarks in two and three dimensions, including Landau damping, two-stream and bump-on-tail instabilities, a cyclotron mini-app and a Penning trap, confirm the theoretical findings. A single model trained on several mini-apps generated by different reference methods captures all of them and remains stable up to twice the training time window, which points towards foundation models for fields in particle-based methods.

</details>


### [3] [A semi-implicit unified gas-kinetic wave-particle method with energy-conserving electromagnetic coupling for multiscale plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40206)
*Yixiao Wang, Zhigang Pu, Xing Ji, Kun Xu*

分类： physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种半隐式统一气体动理论波粒（UGKWP）方法，用于含BGK碰撞的多组分Vlasov-Maxwell系统；时间步不受回旋周期、等离子体周期和光跨时间限制，并通过多种基准验证其精度、守恒性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 多尺度等离子体中，动理学区域与碰撞主导、强磁化且准中性的区域共存；碰撞时间、回旋周期和等离子体周期远短于流动时间尺度。显式处理碰撞与场耦合时，时间步必须小于这些时间及单元光跨时间，计算效率受限。

方法： 采用半隐式UGKWP方法求解含BGK碰撞的多组分Vlasov-Maxwell系统。电场由重写后的Maxwell方程给出，形成具有唯一解的单线性系统，且对任意时间步均适用；电流响应由隐式中点速度映射构造，并同时推进粒子和流体波。场步精确守恒离散总能量，且无需解析回旋周期、等离子体周期或光跨时间即可稳定，时间步由流动与声速决定。Gauss定律修正调整电场以及粒子和波的联合电荷，同时守恒每种带电组分的质量、动量和能量。

结果： 方法复现了哨声波和电子等离子体波，涵盖从Landau阻尼到碰撞主导振荡的情形；在弱到强碰撞性与磁化程度的Brio-Wu问题、Orszag-Tang涡和無碰撞磁重联中，与粒子网格、双流体和磁流体参考解一致。其CPU时间远少于采用完美双曲Maxwell方程显式求解器的UGKWP方法或确定性多尺度动理学方法；在碰撞等离子体中，也比采用相同碰撞算子的隐式粒子网格方法更省CPU时间，且碰撞越强优势越明显。

结论： 该半隐式统一波粒方法能够在无需解析短回旋、等离子体和光跨时间尺度的情况下稳定推进多尺度等离子体，并保持场步离散能量守恒及Gauss修正下的组分质量、动量和能量守恒；多基准结果支持其准确性、跨区域适用性和计算效率。摘要未给出具体加速比或定量误差指标。

原始摘要： In multiscale plasmas, kinetic regions coexist with collision-dominated, strongly magnetized and quasi-neutral ones. In these regions, the collision time, the cyclotron period and the plasma period, respectively, are far shorter than the flow time scales. Schemes that treat the collisions and the field coupling explicitly must take time steps below these times and below the light-crossing time of a cell. This paper presents a semi-implicit unified gas-kinetic wave-particle (UGKWP) method for the multispecies Vlasov-Maxwell system with Bhatnagar-Gross-Krook collisions. The electric field follows from a reformulated Maxwell equation, a single linear system with a unique solution for any time step. Its current response is built from the implicit-midpoint velocity map, which then pushes the particles and the hydrodynamic wave alike. The field step conserves the discrete total energy exactly and remains stable without resolving the cyclotron period, the plasma period or the light-crossing time, so that the time step follows from the flow and sound speeds. A Gauss's law correction adjusts the electric field and the joint charge of particles and wave while conserving the mass, momentum and energy of every charged species. The method reproduces whistler waves and electron plasma waves from Landau damping to collision-dominated oscillations. It agrees with particle-in-cell, two-fluid and magnetohydrodynamic references in a Brio-Wu problem from weak to strong collisionality and magnetization, the Orszag-Tang vortex and collisionless magnetic reconnection. It needs far less CPU time than either the UGKWP method with an explicit solver for the perfectly hyperbolic Maxwell equations or a deterministic multiscale kinetic scheme. In collisional plasmas, it also needs less CPU time than an implicit particle-in-cell method with the same collision operator, increasingly so as collisions strengthen.

</details>


### [4] [Evolutionary tracks and isochrones of the inverse Hall cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40092)
*Axel Brandenburg, Vinay Kumar*

分类： physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文检验磁流体湍流逆级联速度的电阻率依赖性是否源于磁重联；通过无动量方程的霍尔级联模拟发现逆转移速度仍依赖电阻率，而对照数值重联实验未见此依赖，因而认为两者未必相关。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 衰减磁流体湍流可表现出逆级联，亚惯性区磁能谱高度随时间增加，但已有证据显示逆转移速度存在电阻率依赖；由于磁重联也依赖电阻率，作者希望厘清逆转移速度的电阻率依赖是否由磁重联导致。

方法： 采用霍尔级联设定，其中不求解动量方程，并把感应项替换为对应磁流体力学中负洛伦兹力的项；同时开展类似的数值重联实验作为对照，比较逆转移速度对电阻率的依赖情况。

结果： 在霍尔级联中仍观察到逆转移速度具有电阻率依赖；但在类似的数值重联实验中没有发现这种电阻率依赖。

结论： 逆转移速度的电阻率依赖不一定与磁重联有关；摘要未提供更多定量参数或具体机制细节。

原始摘要： Decaying MHD turbulence can exhibit an inverse cascade, which means that the height of the spectrum of magnetic energy in the subinertial range increases with time. However, there is evidence for a resistive dependence of the inverse transfer speed. It is unclear whether this is caused by reconnection, which is known to depend on resistivity. Here, we show that in the Hall cascade, where no momentum equation is solved, and the induction term is replaced by what corresponds to the negative Lorentz force in MHD, there is still a resistive dependence of the inverse transfer speed, but no such dependence is found for an analogous numerical reconnection experiment. This suggests that the resistive dependence of the inverse transfer speed is not necessarily related to reconnection.

</details>


### [5] [Electromagnetic emission at the Langmuir frequency from laser interaction with an unmagnetized plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39864)
*J. -R. Marquès, F. Pérez, P. Loiseau, L. Lancia, C. Briand, S. Depierreux, M. Grech, C. Riconda*

分类： physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在欠密、无磁化等离子体的激光-等离子体相互作用中实验观测到朗缪尔频率 ω_p 的电磁辐射，并同时观测到 2ω_p 辐射；该辐射经空间分辨与偏振分析，来源于拉曼不稳定性激发的静电波，并可沿等离子体密度下降方向逃逸。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 论文旨在实验观测激光-等离子体相互作用中朗缪尔频率附近的电磁辐射，并利用该实验平台研究某些射电太阳暴起源中的波耦合过程。

方法： 采用激光与欠密、无磁化等离子体相互作用；对辐射进行空间分辨观测和偏振分析，并分析其与拉曼不稳定性所激发静电波的关系。摘要未提供激光参数、等离子体密度标长、诊断设备等具体实验细节。

结果： 首次观测到 ω_p 处的电磁辐射；其频率虽非常接近等离子体截止频率 ω_{pe}，但辐射可沿等离子体密度分布下降的斜率逃逸；同时观测到 2ω_p 辐射。该ω_p辐射起源于拉曼不稳定性激发的静电波。

结论： 该实验平台可作为研究某些射电太阳暴起源中波耦合过程的有力工具；摘要未给出定量结论或具体应用验证。

原始摘要： We present the first experimental observation of electromagnetic emission at the Langmuir frequency, $ω_p$, resulting from laser-plasma interaction in an underdense and unmagnetised plasma. The emission is spatially resolved and analyzed in polarization. It originates from an electrostatic wave excited by the Raman instability. Despite its frequency being very close to the plasma's cutoff frequency, $ω_{pe}$, this radiation can escape the plasma by following the decreasing slope of the plasma density profile. Radiation at $2ω_p$, is also observed. This experimental platform offers a powerful tool to explore the processes of wave coupling at the origin of some radio solar bursts.

</details>


### [6] [Magnetic shocks generated by two counterstreaming plasma jets induced by an intense femtosecond laser pulse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39808)
*R. S. Zemskov, A. V. Kotov, S. E. Perevalov, A. A. Murzanev, A. I. Korytin, P. A. Chuvakin, A. V. Romashkin, V. N. Ginzburg, A. A. Kochetkov, S. E. Stukachev, I. V. Yakovlev, I. A. Shaikin, A. A. Kuzmin, K. F. Burdonov, A. A. Soloviev, A. A. Shaykin, A. N. Stepanov, M. V. Barkov, E. A. Khazanov, M. V. Starodubtsev*

分类： physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该工作延续对激光产生反向流等离子体喷流磁控的研究，在横向14 T均匀磁场下利用PEARL装置60–80 fs、10–15 J脉冲开展实验，观测到强磁场放大和陡峭密度梯度的窄稳态层，并用时间分辨诊断与混合PIC–流体模拟解释为外场被汇聚等离子体冲压压缩，形成减速并横向偏转流动的高场屏障。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 扩展Zemskov等（2026）关于反向流激光等离子体喷流磁控制的研究，重点考察横向均匀14 T外磁场对喷流相互作用的影响，并探索弱环境磁场被汇聚流压缩放大的实验室模拟途径及其天体物理意义。

方法： 在PEARL装置上用60–80 fs、10–15 J激光脉冲产生等离子体流，施加垂直于喷流传播方向的14 T均匀磁场；采用时间分辨干涉测量、阴影成像和法拉第偏振测量，并结合混合PIC–流体模拟分析相互作用区的高场层、密度梯度和流动偏转。

结果： 实验观测到相互作用区形成窄而稳定的层，伴随强磁场放大和陡峭等离子体密度梯度；诊断与模拟重现了高场屏障的形成，该屏障使等离子体流减速并横向偏转，同时产生多个密度跳变。作者将其解释为外磁场被汇聚喷流的冲压平流和压缩，直至磁压与不断降低的喷流冲压平衡。摘要未给出具体磁场放大倍数等定量数值。

结论： 横向外场可被汇聚等离子体流有效压缩并放大，形成高场屏障并改变流动结构；这提供了一种非湍流的磁场放大通道，可使星际磁场具有动力学重要性，从而利于超新星遗迹中的扩散激波加速。实验建立了连接外部磁化与自磁化体系的实验室平台，并为高能天体物理激波中的磁场放大提供补充途径。

原始摘要： This work extends the investigation of magnetic control of counterstreaming laser-produced plasma jets reported in Zemskov et al. (2026), focusing on the experiments performed in the presence of a uniform 14 T magnetic field oriented transverse to the jet propagation. When generating plasma flows using 60-80 fs, 10-15 J pulses at the PEARL facility, we observe the formation of narrow, stationary layers with strong magnetic-field amplification and sharp plasma-density gradients in the interaction region. We interpret these features as the result of compressing the externally applied transverse field by the converging plasma ram pressure. The external magnetic field is advected and compressed until its magnetic pressure balances the decreasing ram pressure of the jets. Time-resolved interferometry, shadowgraphy, and Faraday polarimetry, combined with hybrid PIC--fluid simulations, reproduce the formation of a high-field barrier that decelerates and laterally redirects plasma flows while producing multiple density jumps. We discuss the astrophysical implications: the compression of an initially weak ambient field by converging flows provides an efficient, non-turbulent channel for magnetic-field amplification that can render interstellar magnetic fields dynamically important - a condition essential for diffusive shock acceleration in supernova remnants. Our experiments therefore establish a laboratory platform bridging externally magnetized and self-magnetized regimes and offer a complementary route to magnetic-field amplification in high-energy astrophysical shocks.

</details>


### [7] [Linear-Stability-Guided Two-Stage Surrogate Model for Nonlinear ITG-TEM Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39381)

Deuteron-induced neutron generation shows an average flux enhancement in the target normal direction of 13x compared to proton-induced neutron generation, achieving peak fluxes of $7.9\times10^{5}$ neutrons/sr. The experimentally predicted ion-to-neutron conversion efficiency of 1.5$\times 10^{-5}$ for protons and 1.1$\times 10^{-3}$ for deuterons indicates a significant yield contribution from the deuteron breakup reaction. High shot-to-shot stability neutron spectra up to 8.7~MeV ($^9$Be(d,n)$^{10}$B) are observed for more than 200 consecutive shots.

  <summary>详细分析</summary>

方法： 采用Hermite-Laguerre速度空间多项式展开进行径向局部新古典计算，结合先进线性化碰撞算子，包括精确Fokker-Planck-Landau算子；使用多组分LBD类算子和FPL算子与SFINCS代码验证；通过收敛性研究比较HL与Legendre-Laguerre基函数；并将LBD、原始Sugama（OS）和改进Sugama（IS）算子与FPL算子比较。

结论： 本工作将HL基函数的应用从湍流计算扩展到新古典物理，并据作者所知首次实现基于HL基函数的新古典计算。结果表明HL基函数具有可用性但相对LG并无统一精度优势，且碰撞算子选择显著影响新古典通量，尤其离子热通量和bootstrap current。

原始摘要： We present radially local neoclassical (NC) calculations based on a Hermite-Laguerre (HL) velocity-space polynomial expansion combined with advanced linearized collision operators, including the exact Fokker-Planck-Landau (FPL) operator. The implementation is verified against the NC code SFINCS using both a multi-species Lenard-Bernstein-Dougherty-like (LBD) operator and the FPL operator, the NC fluxes agreeing to within 3% in the Pfirsch-Schlüter regime. The accuracy of the HL basis is then assessed by convergence studies against the Legendre-Laguerre (LG) basis. At an equal number of spectral coefficients, neither basis is uniformly more accurate. In the plateau regime, the LG basis is more accurate on the particle and heat fluxes, which are sensitive to the trapped-passing boundary, whereas the HL basis is more accurate on the bootstrap current, carried by the parallel flow. In the banana regime, both bases converge slowly. Finally, we compare the LBD and the original (OS) and improved Sugama (IS) operators against the FPL operator. The IS operator retrieves the NC fluxes to within 6% over four decades of collisionality, whereas the LBD operator overestimates the ion heat flux by a factor of 2.6 to 4.7. The OS operator overestimates the ion heat flux by 4 to 33%, while retrieving the particle flux and the bootstrap current to within 9% and 18%, respectively. The convention adopted for the LBD collision frequency also changes the bootstrap current by up to two orders of magnitude. The present work extends the use of the HL basis, previously used in turbulent calculations, to NC physics and, to the knowledge of the author, these are the first NC calculations using the HL basis.

</details>

结论： MANTA可通过协同优化加料、ICRF和LHCD方案提升等离子体增益并改善功率处理；NT几何的内侧空间为LHCD提供额外优势。摘要未给出各项改进对应的具体增益数值。

原始摘要： In this work, we assess how the performance of MANTA, a high-field, highly radiative, negative triangularity tokamak concept, depends on pellet fueling, ion cyclotron resonant frequency (ICRF) heating, and lower hybrid current drive (LHCD) schemes. Negative triangularity (NT) has been gaining momentum in the fusion community as a naturally, robustly ELM-free regime that is capable of power plant level performance. The MANTA device has been the subject of previous works regarding the whole-device design, power handling and divertor assessments, and core transport optimizations. Informed by these previous studies, we assess whether changes to the original MANTA heating and current drive scheme can positively affect performance, both in terms of lowering scrape-off layer power (improving power-handling, a primary benefit of NT) and maximizing plasma gain to levels necessary for an attractive fusion pilot plant. In this work, we find that the MANTA concept can yield improved performance in terms of plasma gain and scrape-off layer power by simultaneously going to lower input power and higher seeded impurity fraction. Also, we find that increasing the fueling magnitude can increase fusion power while minimally impacting scrape-off layer power. Performance can also be improved by optimizing the ICRF design; decreasing the minority ion fraction, decreasing the toroidal mode number of the injected wave, and switching from a helium-3 minority ion to a hydrogen-1 minority ion all result in increases to fusion power. The additional inboard space afforded by the NT geometry provides an opportunity for wave launch to be moved to the inboard side. This is beneficial for LHCD access and results in a wide range of potential current profiles in the edge, with the total driven current due to 25 MW of LHCD reaching up to 1.2 MA, or about 12% of the total current.

</details>

原始摘要： A diffusion model is developed to describe fast alpha-particle transport and confinement in dense magnetized DT fusion plasmas, tailored to Magneto-Inertial Fusion conditions. The model, derived from the Fokker-Planck kinetic equation using the Chapman-Enskog approximation, includes dynamic Coulomb friction from electrons and ions. This approach improves upon earlier heuristic model [Liberman and Velikovich, J. Plasma Phys. 31, 369 (1984)], which, while valid in the ignition range near 5 keV, lacks accuracy at the higher temperatures ~20 keV required for high-gain Magnetized Liner Inertial Fusion (MagLIF). This paper provides explicit analytic formulas for all transport coefficients and characterizes the relationship between the alpha-particle pressure gradient and the associated Ampère force in magnetized plasmas, which are both relevant for MagLIF at high temperatures.

</details>

原始摘要： Recent studies on Electro-Aero-Dynamic (EAD) thrusters have demonstrated that optimized, multi-stage, electrode arrangements can lead to substantial performance improvements. Within this context, numerical models can play a crucial role in the design process. Existing models can be broadly classified into full plasma models, that account for the chemical kinetics in the ionization region and reduced models, that rely on simplifying assumptions, e.g. Kaptzov's hypothesis, to avoid the computation of the ionization region. Full plasma models, despite their accuracy, are computationally demanding, especially when coupled with the Navier-Stokes equations. Reduced models are computationally cheaper and therefore are well suited for the design process. Compact design EAD thrusters that use multiple stages with alternating polarity have recently appeared in literature. Such a configuration allows smaller inter-stage distances, because the emitter(s) of a stage cannot be ignited by the collector(s) of the previous one, as no voltage difference is applied between them. In this work, we introduce a reduced bipolar corona discharge model coupled to the Navier-Stokes equations. A numerical simulation code is implemented based on the high performance finite element deal.II library. Our model can successfully simulate multi-stage thrusters with alternating polarity configurations and automatically detect the ignition pattern, when a configuration with multiple emitters is considered. Validation is performed against previous experimental and numerical campaigns, demonstrating the good predictive capabilities of our model.

</details>

研究动机： 高能量密度实验中，磁场可能自发产生或由外部施加，因此需要理解强磁场如何从根本上改变激光—等离子体相互作用；该问题对场诊断、等离子体光子学和惯性约束聚变具有重要意义。

方法： 采用动理学理论与粒子—细胞模拟，研究激光从外部垂直磁场中背向散射的过程，并考察磁场强度以及磁场偏离垂直方向的角度对散射共振的影响。

结果： 低磁场强度下，磁场作为电子脱陷机制抑制拉曼散射的非线性效应；当电子回旋频率ωce相对等离子体频率ωpe不可忽略时，拉曼共振分裂为由电子伯恩斯坦波介导的离散共振，这些共振比原拉曼共振更窄更强，且相邻共振之间拉曼散射不再存在；当磁场偏离垂直方向的角度α≳ωce/ωpe时，电子伯恩斯坦波共振的阻尼使其重新合并为一个宽的单拉曼峰。

结论： 强磁场会从根本上改变激光—等离子体相互作用，并产生无磁化等离子体中没有对应物的新现象；该结果对磁场诊断、等离子体光子学和惯性约束聚变具有启示意义。

原始摘要： When a laser backscatters from an external perpendicular magnetic field, stimulated Raman scattering transitions to stimulated Bernstein scattering, which we show using kinetic theory and particle-in-cell simulations. For low field strengths, the magnetic field acts as an electron detrapping mechanism, suppressing nonlinear effects of Raman scattering. At stronger fields, when the electron cyclotron frequency ωce becomes non-negligible compared to the plasma frequency ωpe, the Raman resonance splits into discrete resonances mediated by electron Bernstein waves (EBWs). Raman scattering ceases to exist between the EBW resonances, which are narrower and stronger than the Raman resonance they replace. The replacement is sensitive to angle: When the magnetic field is away from perpendicular by an angle α {\gtrsim} ωce/ωpe, the damping of EBW resonances blends them back into a broad single Raman peak. Our results demonstrate that strong magnetic fields, either spontaneously generated or externally imposed in high-energy-density experiments, fundamentally change laser-plasma interactions in a way that has no analog in unmagnetized plasmas, with implications for field diagnostics, plasma photonics, and inertial confinement fusion.

</details>

结果： 氮充足时，PAW种子引发使生菜头鲜重最高增加40%、叶绿素a+b增加25%，并提高新鲜度和多汁性感官评分；缺氮时，PAW部分补偿氮缺乏，使植株生长最高增加50%、光合色素和槲皮素最高增加20%，APX和G-POX等抗氧化酶活性最高增加约2倍。主成分分析显示氮含量是主要变异驱动因素（PC1为69%–71%），PAW以组织特异性方式调节氧化还原稳态。

原始摘要： Cold atmospheric plasma generates reactive oxygen and nitrogen species (RONS) that dissolve in water, forming plasma-activated water (PAW) and inducing complex chemical changes and biological responses. This study investigates the dual role of PAW in lettuce, Lactuca sativa (cv. Matilda), grown under either N-sufficient or N-deprived half-strength Hoagland solutions. Four treatments were evaluated: control, seed priming in PAW-activated Hoagland, PAW-activated Hoagland cultivation, and the combination of the latter two, under both N-content regimes.

Under N-deprived conditions, PAW partially compensated for N deficiency, increasing plant growth by up to 50%, photosynthetic pigments and quercetin by up to 20%, and antioxidant enzyme activities (e.g., APX and G-POX increased up to 2-fold). Principal component analysis confirmed N content as the primary driver of variance (PC1, 69-71%), with PAW modulating redox homeostasis in a tissue-specific manner.
  Our results demonstrate the capacity of PAW to enhance plant growth, metabolism, and quality even under N-deprived conditions, addressing key gaps in plasma agriculture by highlighting the nutritional and signaling contributions of PAW. PAW therefore represents a promising and environmentally friendly strategy.

TL;DR: 该文用Hasegawa–Wakatani方程的三波截断研究电阻漂移波湍流的非线性动力学，比较长波模为带状模或弱驱动漂移波的两种三波组，并考察背景密度梯度固定或由外部粒子源供给、被湍流粒子通量侵蚀而自洽演化时的稳定性、分岔与混沌行为；结果显示带状三波组几乎始终规则，非带状三波组易进入混沌，梯度反馈显著放大这一差别。

  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 摘要未明确给出独立的研究动机；其研究目的可概括为：通过仅使长波模产生小倾角差异的两种三波组，对比带状模与弱驱动漂移波模，揭示电阻漂移波湍流非线性动力学以及密度梯度反馈对规则性与混沌性的影响。

方法： 采用Hasegawa–Wakatani方程的三波截断；背景密度梯度分别设为固定和自洽演化，后者由外部粒子源P（如中性束注入）供给并被湍流粒子通量侵蚀；比较两个仅长波模有小倾角差异的三波组，其中一个长波模为不能由梯度驱动的带状模，另一个为弱驱动漂移波；在绝热性参数C与驱动构成的平面绘制周期图，并沿低绝热性下恒定C线分析分岔及通向混沌的路径。

结果： 周期图表明带状三波组几乎总是规则，而非带状三波组会变为混沌；通量对梯度的反馈显著提高非带状组中非周期状态的比例，而带状组中该比例仍可忽略。固定梯度、低绝热性并沿恒定C时，非带状组经环面破裂并伴随间歇进入混沌，经内部危机和倍周期级联退出混沌。自洽梯度下，Neimark–Sacker分岔导致锁相环面，随后突然破裂并伴随滞后，混沌持续到最大驱动，且梯度被间歇驱动至长波模阈值以上。当长波模为带状模时，这些通向混沌的路径均不存在。

结论： 长波模是否为带状模（仅小倾角差异）会根本改变三波截断的非线性动力学：带状情形高度规则且对梯度反馈不敏感，非带状情形易混沌；梯度自洽反馈放大两种情形间的差异，并引入不同的分岔、突然破裂与滞后路径。摘要未提供定量参数范围或实验/模拟验证细节。

原始摘要： We investigate the nonlinear dynamics of resistive drift-wave turbulence with a three-wave truncation of the Hasegawa--Wakatani equations, in which the background density gradient either is fixed or evolves self-consistently, fed by an external particle source $P$ (such as neutral beam injection) and eroded by the turbulent particle flux. Two triads that differ only by a small tilt of the long-wavelength mode are compared, in one this mode is zonal and cannot be driven by the gradient, in the other it is a weakly driven drift wave. Period maps in the plane of the adiabaticity parameter $C$ and the drive show that the zonal triad is almost always regular, whereas the non-zonal triad becomes chaotic. The feedback of the flux on the gradient amplifies this contrast: the fraction of aperiodic states grows considerably in the non-zonal triad and remains negligible in the zonal one. Along a line of constant $C$ at low adiabaticity, the non-zonal triad with a fixed gradient enters chaos through torus breakdown with intermittency and leaves it through an interior crisis and a period-doubling cascade. With the self-consistent gradient, a Neimark--Sacker bifurcation leads to a phase-locked torus that breaks down abruptly, with hysteresis, and chaos then persists up to the largest drive, with the gradient driven intermittently above the threshold of the long-wavelength mode. None of these routes to chaos exists when that mode is zonal.

</details>

方法： 构建 BoSS 数据集，将仿星器设计与导心模拟结果和轨迹配对，覆盖广泛磁几何与约束性能的 180,000 个不同磁流体力学平衡；并定义两类建模任务：一是生成建模，用于设计具有良好粒子约束的仿星器；二是代理建模，用于预测仿星器热沉积模式和粒子轨迹；同时建立基线模型。

结果： 基线能够生成多样且性能良好的仿星器，并提供有用的代理预测；任务结果显示了机器学习在仿星器领域的潜在影响，也揭示了提升机器学习模型科学适用性的重要机会。作者指出长时程轨迹预测和分布外生成是未来工作的关键方向，BoSS 可通过 https://boss.flatironinstitute.org/ 访问。

结论： BoSS 为机器学习加速仿星器设计与评估提供了大规模基准数据集，并通过生成建模和代理建模任务证明其效用；同时表明长时程轨迹预测与分布外生成是后续研究的重要方向。

原始摘要： Stellarators offer a promising magnetic confinement approach for achieving controlled nuclear fusion. However, stellarator design remains challenging, as evaluating configuration performance requires computationally expensive, high-fidelity simulations. In particular, key performance metrics derived from guiding center simulations are costly to include directly in the design optimization loop, and are almost exclusively used as diagnostics. Machine learning has the potential to advance fusion research by enabling rapid generation and evaluation of new stellarator configurations. Effectively applying ML for fusion demands large-scale datasets that capture both design diversity and high-fidelity simulation outputs. In this work, we introduce the Benchmark of Stellarator Simulations (BoSS), a dataset of stellarator designs paired with guiding center simulation results and trajectories. The BoSS is the first large-scale dataset of guiding center simulations, containing simulation data for 180,000 distinct magnetohydrodynamic equilibria spanning a wide range of magnetic geometries and confinement properties. To exemplify the utility of ML to accelerate stellarator design, we formulate and address two sets of modeling tasks: generative modeling for designing stellarators with good particle confinement, and surrogate modeling for predicting stellarator heat deposition patterns and particle trajectories. The tasks reveal the potential impact ML can have in the stellarator field and important opportunities to improve the applicability of ML models in the sciences. Our baselines generate diverse, performant stellarators and provide useful surrogate predictions, and point to long-horizon trajectory forecasting and out-of-distribution generation as key directions for future work. The BoSS can be accessed through https://boss.flatironinstitute.org/.

</details>

To investigate this discrepancy, a semi-analytical slice model is developed for bunches with non-Gaussian longitudinal distributions and slice-dependent transverse mismatch. The model predicts slice energy-spread evolution in beamlines with dispersion and provides an independent reference for assessing the simulation results. Benchmarks in an achromatic arc show good agreement with RF-Track for both ideal Gaussian and realistic mismatched bunches in the tested cases. The proposed model provides a practical tool for evaluating IBS in linacs beyond the ideal-Gaussian approximation. The comparison also suggests directions for improving the accuracy and efficiency of PIC-based IBS simulations.

First, a concise scientific motivation is given, including the exciting prospect of measurements of the Higgs boson self-interaction, and is followed by a presentation of the FCC-$μe$ layout -- from the source of ultra-cold anti-muons, through the muon booster, to the main muon ring. Basic considerations are also given regarding the machine-detector interface and the performance of the FCC-$μe$ and associated neutrino factory. The paper concludes with an outlook underlying relevance of such anti-muon beams for any future high energy storage ring as well as for neutrino science.

Additionally, the observed line shapes indicate the absence of significant intramolecular vibrational energy redistribution (IVR) within the investigated energy range, while a few discrepancies in the Hamiltonian fit suggest the onset of weak perturbations, demonstrating the sensitivity of the present approach to probe subtle intramolecular couplings in complex polyatomic spectra. Power-dependent photoluminescence further reveals a pronounced nonlinear response of the confined Jackiw-Rebbi polariton, including a blueshift of up to 5 meV and correlated changes in its intensity scaling and linewidth. These results establish vdW photonic heterostructures as a versatile platform for engineering tunable and nonlinear polaritons localised at topological interfaces.

原始摘要： Sommerfeld's half-plane problem, describing diffraction of a monochromatic wave by a semi-infinite perfectly electric conducting (PEC) infinitesimally thin plate, is one of the cornerstones of classical electromagnetic scattering theory. Here we study its temporal analogue: a semi-infinite medium with the Drude dispersion, adjacent to air, whose plasma frequency is rapidly reduced to zero, thus turning it into air, while a monochromatic wave with frequency below the plasma frequency illuminates it under normal incidence. Unlike the PEC plate, the Drude medium supports evanescent penetration of the field prior to its temporal transition; the temporal interface here acts as the analogue of the sharp spatial boundary in the Sommerfeld problem, but now separates two time intervals rather than two spatial half-planes. We show that this transition forces the standing wave in air in front of the Drude medium and the evanescent fields inside it to propagate in both directions, while preserving their spatial field structure. In addition, we observe the excitation of additional electric and magnetic field waves generated by a "DC" current density associated with the time interface. Our findings offer insight into the electromagnetic wave behavior at temporal transitions in dispersive media and may find applications in time- and space-time metamaterials

At a topological tri-critical point, the singularity of winding function manifests as an A2 fold catastrophe. This dictates a strictly linear spatial decay, marking a sharp departure from the conventional exponential scaling paradigm. Using acoustic metamaterial platforms tuned to a tricritical point, we experimentally demonstrate this linear decay envelope in 1D, and further generalize it to two-dimensional systems to realize scale-free corner modes with simultaneous linear decay along orthogonal directions. While existing critical topology frameworks rely on entanglement spectra to diagnose mode existence in gapless regimes, our work bridges catastrophe theory with topological matter at multicriticalities, advancing the field from abstract mode identification to deterministic real-space wavefunction engineering.

Finally, the capability of the proposed structure for tuning the stand-off distance by lateral displacement of the feeding source is verified. As a result, the proposed compact, low-cost configuration shows potential for practical mm-wave and THz stand-off imaging systems.

The mean $Q$ of the selected modes follows that of the manually labeled modes ($\langle Q\rangle\proptoσ^{k}$, $k=-0.80\pm0.05$ versus $-0.86\pm0.04$). After adaptation on 50 labeled realizations, the workflow identifies the quasi-BIC of a Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ bar metasurface in 98--100\% of labeled cases, including a lossy phase where $Q$ does not distinguish the mode.

This deviates from the historical Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te MREI (modified-random-element-isodisplacement) scheme inspired from classical Raman and infrared studies, in which the optical modes are blind to the bond environment (1-bond: 1-mode). The spectra are strongly tunable and split into two regimes at a critical angle where a single moiré wavelength spans the island; beyond it, an ordered ladder of resonances emerges whenever an integer number of moiré periods fits within the overlapping bilayer. In doped islands, the interlayer coupling also produces a moiré-enabled Born--Kuhn chiral plasmon: opposite transverse dipoles in the two layers form an electric quadrupole that hybridizes with the bright dipole, opening an anticrossing near the critical angle, with a handedness that reverses with the twist direction. Triangular islands confirm that the moiré lattice, not the flake boundary, governs both effects, establishing twist as a mechanical handle on chiral graphene plasmons.

研究动机： 传统弛豫铁电体虽具有优异电致伸缩响应，但通常依赖化学复杂性和结构无序，局部成分涨落形成极性纳米区，导致其微观起源难以控制和工程化；因此需要寻找可设计、可调控的新弛豫机制。

方法： 通过菱方氮化硼的堆垛相工程引入堆垛相异质性，构建滑动畴作为极性纳米区的结构类比物；这些滑动畴在电场下通过协同层间滑动实现集体取向，从而产生滑动弛豫铁电性。

结果： 在约300 nm厚（约1000原子层）晶体中获得最高约-1.6%的可逆电致机械变形且迟滞可忽略；电致伸缩系数Q33约1200 m^4C^-2，比多数晶态电致伸缩材料高数个数量级；该响应在低至1.6 K仍保持活性，不同于常因偶极冻结而受抑制的传统弛豫体。

结论： 研究确立了滑动弛豫铁电性作为实现弛豫行为的新途径，表明原子尺度层间运动可放大为跨约1000层的集体介观功能，并为基于范德华材料的电致机械、光子和量子技术提供材料按需设计平台。

原始摘要： Relaxor ferroelectrics are among the most important electromechanical materials owing to their exceptionally large electrostrictive responses. However, conventional relaxor behavior is generally associated with chemical complexity and structural disorder, where local compositional fluctuations generate polar nanoregions, making the microscopic origin of this behavior difficult to control and engineer. Here we report sliding relaxor ferroelectricity (S-RFE), a previously unknown relaxor state in which engineered stacking-phase heterogeneity enables cooperative interlayer sliding to generate collective relaxor behavior. Through stacking-phase engineering in rhombohedral boron nitride, we create sliding domains that act as structural analogues of polar nanoregions and cooperatively align under an electric field through interlayer sliding. This collective state produces reversible electromechanical deformation of up to approximately $-1.6\%$ with negligible hysteresis in approximately 300-nm-thick crystals, corresponding to approximately 1000 atomic layers. The resulting electrostriction coefficient ($Q_{33}$ of approximately $1200\,\mathrm{m}^{4}\mathrm{C}^{-2}$) exceeds those of most crystalline electrostrictors by orders of magnitude. Unlike conventional relaxors, whose electromechanical response is often suppressed by dipolar freezing at low temperatures, S-RFE remains active down to 1.6 K and maintains giant electromechanical functionality. Our results establish sliding relaxor ferroelectricity as a new route to relaxor behavior and reveal how atomic-scale interlayer motion can be amplified into collective mesoscale functionality across approximately one thousand layers, providing a materials-by-design platform for electromechanical, photonic, and quantum technologies based on van der Waals materials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 稳定隧穿动力学是非厄米量子系统量子输运研究的基础问题之一，而非互易耦合作为非厄米性的重要表现，会影响量子器件稳定性与自旋隧穿动力学，因此需要研究非互易耦合、增益损耗和周期驱动对稳定自旋翻转隧穿的协同调控机制。

方法： 将自旋轨道耦合玻色原子置于带增益和损耗的双势阱中，解析分析周期驱动下的Floquet态和准能谱，获得系统的PT对称相图，并考察非互易耦合强度、增益损耗导致的非厄米强度以及周期驱动对稳定自旋翻转隧穿的协同调控。

结果： 结果表明，精确调控非互易性与增益损耗可显著扩大稳定参数区域；在增益损耗平衡的非互易系统中，稳定参数区域的连续或离散由驱动频率比Ω/ω的奇偶性决定，自旋翻转隧穿可通过参数λ和2ε/ω精确控制；在增益损耗不平衡情形下，进一步给出了不同Ω/ω奇偶性下实现稳定自旋翻转隧穿的平衡条件。摘要未提供具体数值结果或实验参数。

结论： 研究为实现鲁棒的自旋翻转隧穿提供了新的理论途径，对非厄米超冷原子系统中的量子态输运研究具有指导意义，也可为新型自旋量子器件的实验设计提供参考。

原始摘要： The stable tunneling dynamics in non-Hermitian quantum systems is one of the fundamental issues in the study of quantum transport. Nonreciprocal coupling, as another crucial manifestation of non-Hermiticity, exerts significant regulatory effects on the stability of quantum devices and on spin tunneling dynamics. Here we place spin-orbit-coupled bosonic atoms in a double-well potential with gain and loss, and investigate the effect of nonreciprocal coupling on the system dynamics. Through an analytical analysis of the Floquet states and the quasi-energy spectrum under periodic driving, we obtain the PT-symmetry phase diagram of the system. We further explore the synergistic regulation mechanism of several factors---including the strength of nonreciprocal coupling, the non-Hermitian strength arising from gain-loss, and the periodic driving---on stable spin-flip tunneling. The results show that precise manipulation of nonreciprocity and gain-loss can substantially enlarge the stable parameter region. In nonreciprocal systems with balanced gain and loss, the continuity or discreteness of the stable parameter region is determined by the parity of the driving-frequency ratio $Ω/ω$, and the spin-flip tunneling can be precisely controlled through the parameters $λ$ and $2\varepsilon/ω$. For the unbalanced gain-loss scenario, the equilibrium conditions for achieving stable spin-flip tunneling under different parities of $Ω/ω$ are further provided. These findings offer a new theoretical approach for realizing robust spin-flip tunneling, provide guiding significance for the study of quantum-state transport in non-Hermitian ultracold atomic systems, and also serve as a reference for the experimental design of novel spin-based quantum devices.

</details>

*Will Gunn, Han-Hao Cheng, Daniel Peace, Fernando Gottardo, Lachlan Hanrahan, Zachary Degnan, Keith Moulding, Nit Taksatorn, Roger Wepf, Markus Rambach, Andrew G. White*

分类： physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于GDSII到SEM的软件工作流，将学术洁净室中已被取代的图案发生器改造为CD-SEM，实现无人值守图像采集及CD与LER提取，并在Raith eLine Plus上验证了光刻胶工艺窗口校准、集成光子器件芯片级CD监测和CNN训练数据批量获取三类应用。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>

方法： 利用学术洁净室现有硬件，构建GDSII-to-SEM软件工作流，将设施中已被取代的图案发生器转换为用于无人值守图像采集的CD-SEM，并高效提取CD和线边缘粗糙度（LER）。该方法在Raith eLine Plus上展示，预期适用于运行Raith NanoSuite的其他图案发生器。

结果： 展示了三类用例：加速两种光刻胶工艺窗口的校准；对集成光子器件进行芯片级计量以跟踪CD变化；批量获取数据集用于训练卷积神经网络以预测工艺畸变。

结论： 该方法可在学术洁净室中实现foundry式、由计量驱动的工艺校准，并可推广到光子学之外任何需要纳米级CD控制的EBL纳米制造。

原始摘要： Design-based metrology of academic electron beam lithography (EBL) is bottlenecked by low-throughput imaging. For integrated photonics, where nanometre-scale structural variation can significantly limit device performance, scanning electron microscopy (SEM) is the standard tool for critical dimension (CD) metrology, revealing the structure of devices that is not available from optical measurements. Manual SEM imaging on a wafer-scale is time-intensive, can feature operator-dependent distortions, and yields too few data to reliably close the nanofabrication calibration loop. The following details a methodology that utilises existing hardware in an academic cleanroom: a GDSII-to-SEM software workflow that converts a facility's superseded pattern generator into a CD-SEM for unattended image acquisition and efficient CD and line edge roughness (LER) extraction. This is shown for a Raith eLine Plus, and is expected to be applicable to other pattern generators running Raith's NanoSuite. Presented are results demonstrating its three use cases within a facility: accelerated calibration of two photoresists' process windows; chip-scale metrology of integrated photonic devices to track CD variation; and mass dataset acquisition for convolutional neural network training to predict process distortion. This methodology enables foundry-style, metrology-driven process calibration in academic cleanrooms, generalisable beyond photonics to any EBL nanofabrication that requires nanometre-scale CD control.

</details>


### [61] [Fundamental limits on photonic state preparation from phase-space majorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37755)
*Twesh Upadhyaya, Oliver Hahn, Ulysse Chabaud, Nikolaos Koukoulekidis*

分类： physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文建立了基于相空间函数无序度的弱完全相对主化理论，用于给出多模光子系统中最优概率性量子态转换的基本界限，并据此分析压缩态、猫态、GKP态和Fock态在精确与近似转换下的限制。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 制备复杂量子光态通常需要从较简单态出发，通过实验可实现的操作用概率性转换完成；因此需要理解并界定这类转换在精确和近似情形下的根本极限。

方法： 建立弱且完全相对的主化理论，通过比较相空间函数所体现的无序程度来刻画转换可能性；该框架可适配不同系统与允许操作集合，并以多模光子系统中的Wigner函数为主要应用对象。

结果： 针对压缩态、猫态、Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill态和Fock态的精确及近似转换，得到严格的不可行性结果与权衡关系，导出新的界限并恢复已知的紧致界限；摘要未提供具体数值界限或实验数据。

结论： 相空间无序度对光子态制备构成强限制，表明概率性转换的基本界限会显著约束复杂量子光态的制备。

原始摘要： Preparing complex quantum states of light for quantum information processing tasks often proceeds by converting from simpler states via experimentally accessible operations. We provide fundamental bounds on such probabilistic conversions, both exact and approximate. To do so, we establish a theory of weak, fully relative majorization that compares the disorder of phase-space functions. Our framework is versatile and can be tailored to different systems and sets of allowed operations. Focusing on Wigner functions in multimode photonic systems, we establish rigorous no-go results and tradeoff relations for exact and approximate conversions involving squeezed states, cat states, Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill states, and Fock states. In doing so, we derive novel bounds and recover known tight ones. Our results demonstrate that phase-space disorder imposes strong limitations on photonic state preparation.

</details>

方法： 在非正交二维组分空间框架下，推导标量耗散率与曲率之间的形式关系，并分析仅以应变率实现火焰面理论一般闭合所需的条件。

结果： 研究表明，可以仅用应变率实现火焰面理论的一般闭合，但无论非正交空间还是正交空间，都至少需要考虑三个不同参数。

结论： 标量耗散率与曲率的形式关系支持以应变率进行一般闭合，但闭合模型不能只依赖少于三个参数；该要求同时适用于非正交和正交空间。

原始摘要： Formal relations between scalar dissipation rates and curvatures are developed in the context of non-orthogonal two-dimensional composition spaces. These show that it is possible to achieve general closure of flamelet theory in terms of strain rates only, but that this requires the consideration of at least three different parameters (both in non-orthogonal and orthogonal spaces).

*Hamid R. Karbasian, Brian C. Vermeire*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文提出一种面向PDE约束优化的物理约束数据驱动降阶模型（ROM）方法，将大规模非线性优化转化为ROM约束优化，并在二维/三维扑翼形状优化中验证其稳定性、效率与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 传统方法在构建ROM替代模型及求解PDE约束优化时，常受前向灵敏度函数不稳定的影响，且内存需求大、计算成本高；作者希望在大规模非线性优化中提高稳定性并降低内存与计算开销。

方法： 构建物理约束的数据驱动框架，将大规模非线性优化问题转化为ROM约束优化问题；不同于传统方法，该方法把优化问题投影为Hilbert空间中的系统动力学优化问题，以提高稳定性、减少内存需求并降低计算成本。摘要未提供ROM的具体降阶基、数据采样或训练细节。

结果： 在Re=2400的二维扑翼截面形状优化中（翼与流体非线性相互作用并包含混沌流动结构），该方法使时间平均推力提高37%、推进效率提高40%；作者还将方法用于三维扑翼测试，结果表明其在高复杂度航空航天优化设计中具有潜力。

结论： 所提出的物理约束数据驱动ROM约束优化框架能够稳定、低内存、低成本地处理PDE约束优化，并在二维扑翼形状优化中显著提升推力与推进效率，同时展现出向三维及更复杂最优设计扩展的潜力。

原始摘要： This study introduces a new approach for building surrogate models, in the form of Reduced Order Models (ROMs), for PDE-constrained optimization. A physics-constrained data-driven framework is developed to transform large-scale non-linear optimization problems into ROM-constrained optimization problems. Unlike conventional methods, which suffer from instability of the forward sensitivity function, the proposed approach projects optimization problems to system dynamics optimization problems in Hilbert space to increase stability, diminish memory requirements, and lower computational cost. The present approach is applied to shape optimization of a 2D flapping wing section at $Re=2,400$, where the non-linear interaction of the wing with fluid includes chaotic flow structures. It is shown that the proposed approach can significantly enhance the time-averaged thrust force and propulsive efficiency by 37\% and 40\%, respectively. We also tested this approach for a 3D flapping wing and the results reveal the potential of the proposed approach for more complex optimal designs in the aerospace industry.

</details>


### [64] [Dual Area-Volume-of-Fluid (DAVOF): co-advecting volume and face-area fractions for a closed-form, cell-local interface normal and position on unstructured meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38863)
*Tomislav Marić*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 论文针对非结构多面体网格上几何VOF界面重建依赖邻居体积分数、在不规则单元和wisp存在时退化、且曲率误差驱动毛细流寄生电流的问题，提出输运面润湿分数并利用单元自身面的闭式恒等式恢复界面法向与位置；在球和椭球上验证精度，并用平面裁剪、环法向及初步抛物面传输展示稳定、守恒的界面输运。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 几何VOF在非结构多面体网格上通常由邻居模板的体积分数重建界面方向，在不规则单元和存在wisp时精度下降，而基于该重建得到的曲率会驱动毛细流动中的寄生电流。因此需要一种不依赖邻居、不迭代且对不规则网格更稳健的界面几何恢复与输运方法。

方法： 在输运每个单元体积分数的同时，一致地输运每个面面积的润湿分数。由单元自身各面的求和及液体子体积上的散度定理，以闭式方程恢复界面法向；再由第二个恒等式恢复平面位置，不需要邻居或迭代。将平面裁剪与环法向结合，环法向即对单元环邻域并集应用同一恒等式。还给出初步的每单元一个抛物面的传输实现：由法向确定框架，由环确定曲率张量，由体积分数确定位置。

结果： 在球和椭球上，法向达到面分数阶，位置在所述各种状态和网格上均为二阶；通过一环恢复法向构造二次曲面可得到收敛曲率，而参考模型未显示曲率收敛。与平面裁剪一起输运时，环法向使球在超过3.3个直径的输运中保持有界和守恒，面分数为一阶，符合裁剪规律，位置和形状误差与plicRDF相当。初步抛物面传输在N=32至128的分辨率阶梯上保持球有界守恒，每周期面分数量级为2.8（精确法向）和2.1（环二次曲面法向）。循环的收敛阶受输入法向阶数限制，二阶输运法向仍是研究目标。

结论： 该方法提供了仅依赖单元自身面信息的闭式法向和位置恢复，在六面体、扰动及多面体单元上对平面界面精确，并改善了曲率、稳定性和守恒性，为方法后续发展奠定基础。当前输运法向尚未达到二阶，获得二阶输运法向是未来目标；摘要未提供更完整的定量误差常数、计算成本及更广泛验证细节。

原始摘要： Geometric Volume-of-Fluid (VOF) on unstructured polyhedral meshes reconstructs the interface orientation from the volume fractions of a stencil of neighbours; on irregular cells and in the presence of wisps it degrades, and the curvature built on it drives the parasitic currents of capillary flows. We transport, consistently to the volume fraction of every cell, the wetted fraction of every face area. From the area and volume fractions, closed-form equations recover the interface normal and position of a cell, for the first time in VOF: the normal from one sum over the cell's own faces, the divergence theorem on the liquid sub-volume, and the plane's position from a second, without neighbours or iteration. Both identities are exact for a plane on hexahedral, perturbed and polyhedral cells. On a sphere and an ellipsoid the normal has the order of the face fractions, the position is second order for every state and mesh, and a quadric through one ring of recovered normals gives a converging curvature where the reference models show none. Transported with the plane clipping, the ring normal, the same identity on the union of the cell's ring, stabilises the state and carries a sphere over 3.3 diameters bounded and conservative, first order in the face fractions as the clipping law predicts, at plicRDF's position and shape errors. A preliminary transport implementation with one paraboloid per cell, its frame from the normal, its curvature tensor from the ring and its position from the volume fraction, carries the sphere bounded and conservative over the resolution ladder with face fractions of order 2.8 per period with the exact normal and 2.1 with the ring quadric's normal over N = 32 to 128. The loop has the order of the normal it is fed; a transported normal of second order is the research goal. This document is the basis on which the further development of the method is documented.

</details>


### [65] [Wave Turbulence of Capillary Waves in Finite Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40217)
*Alexander O. Korotkevich*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

方法： 采用一系列毛细波湍流数值模拟，在不同非线性水平和多种模拟配置下考察Zakharov-Filonenko谱的动态范围，并分析其与平均陡度μ的缩放关系。摘要未给出具体的控制方程、数值格式和参数设置。

原始摘要： We consider classical problem of turbulence of capillary waves. In a series of numerical simulations it is shown that Zakharov-Filonenko spectrum corresponding to direct cascade of energy has a limited dynamic range of realization in a finite system, which shrinks with decreasing level of nonlinearity. Recent hypothesis that this dynamic range scales with average steepness as $\simμ^{8/3}$ is confirmed. As a byproduct the Zakharov-Filonenko spectrum was observed for record dynamic range close to or beyond one decade in a variety of simulation configurations.

  <summary>详细分析</summary>

结果： 各向同性初始条件的模拟结果重现了波作用量的Zakharov--Filonenko幂律湍流谱，n_k∼k^{-17/4}；强各向异性初始条件演化后形成因子化谱n(k,φ)∼k^{-17/4}F(φ)，其中F(φ)为傅里叶空间中的角度函数；在k方向稳态幂律分布区间，计算得到的碰撞积分在波数惯性区内以高精度趋零。

结论： 结果表明，在所用模型与模拟条件下，可实现一种新的强各向异性湍流谱，其性质类似于Kolmogorov--Zakharov谱。

原始摘要： In the present work, numerical simulation of the evolution of capillary turbulence was performed on the basis of the wave kinetic equation taking into account energy dissipation at small scales. For an isotropic initial condition, the simulation results reproduce the Zakharov--Filonenko power-law turbulence spectrum for the wave action, \(n_k \sim k^{-17/4}\). The main result of the work is the calculation of the evolution for a substantially anisotropic initial condition. The numerical data indicate that, during the evolution of an anisotropic wave-action distribution, a factorized spectrum \(n(k,φ) \sim k^{-17/4}F(φ)\) is formed in polar coordinates \((k,φ)\), where \(F(φ)\) is a function of the angle in Fourier space. In the regime of a steady power-law distribution in \(k\), the calculated collision integral vanishes with high accuracy in the inertial interval of wavenumbers (the region of the power-law spectrum). Thus, the work provides evidence for the realization of a new strongly anisotropic turbulence spectrum, similar to Kolmogorov--Zakharov spectra.

</details>

原始摘要： Wave-mean flow interactions in stratified fluids can drive spontaneous oscillations, yet the mechanisms controlling transitions between periodic and chaotic regimes remain poorly understood. Using a minimal one-dimensional model of wave-driven mean-flow reversals motivated by the quasi-biennial oscillation (QBO), we identify two distinct mechanisms that suppress the formation of multiple extrema in the velocity profile: critical-level momentum deposition by a broad spectrum of undamped small-scale gravity waves, and wave-amplitude growth due to exponential density stratification. Although physically distinct, both mechanisms inhibit the route to chaos and reduce sensitivity to small-scale viscosity, substantially widening the region of periodic behavior in parameter space. These results identify a general internal mechanism for the stabilization of wave-driven oscillations, with direct implications for QBO-like phenomena in geophysical and astrophysical flows.

</details>

In this work, we demonstrate experimentally and numerically that instead of being slowed down by the attached drop, a Taylor bubble-drop assembly rises at the same velocity as an isolated Taylor bubble, leading to a significantly faster rise of the Taylor drop. We discuss the underlying mechanism and use an analytical model to predict the drop radius within the assembly. This phenomenon offers a means for bubble-assisted rapid transport of an immiscible liquid phase in vertical tubes, with potential applicability in gas-lifting systems in confined environments.

### [69] [Causality-informed dynamic mode decomposition for unsteady flow analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39522)
*Yihua Pan, Jiaqing Kou*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 论文提出 Granger 因果引导的动态模态分解（GC-DMD），用条件 Granger 因果估计正则化 DMD 算子识别，以获得稀疏、可预测且动态相关的降阶模型；在线性耦合系统、不同复杂度盖驱动腔流和宽带跨声速湍流射流上验证，较标准 DMD 在算子识别、谱鲁棒性和预测精度方面均有改进。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 因果分析能够刻画动力系统中的方向性依赖，但这类信息通常未被用于非定常流动的降阶建模。作者希望将方向性预测依赖信息引入高维流动数据的数据驱动建模与模态分析，以改进 DMD 算子识别和预测性能。

方法： 提出 GC-DMD 框架：利用条件 Granger 因果估计对 DMD 算子识别进行正则化，优先抑制缺乏弱支持或弱支持的相互作用，同时保留动态相关耦合，从而促进由方向性预测依赖引导的稀疏算子。评估对象包括具有已知耦合结构的线性系统、不同动态复杂度的盖驱动腔流以及宽带跨声速湍流射流，并与标准 DMD 比较。

结果： 在线性基准中，GC-DMD 提高了算子识别精度；在腔流中提高了识别谱的鲁棒性并降低长期预测误差；在湍流射流中获得更稀疏的算子并提高预测精度。摘要未提供具体数值指标。

结论： 结果表明，引入因果信息有助于识别复杂流动的稀疏、可预测降阶模型，展示了因果信息指导降阶建模的潜力；相关源代码已公开。摘要未给出定量结论或更广泛的适用性验证。

原始摘要： Causal analysis characterizes directional dependencies in dynamical systems, while such information is not typically used to inform reduced-order modeling of unsteady flows. This study proposes a Granger Causality-informed Dynamic Mode Decomposition (GC-DMD) framework that incorporates such information into data-driven modeling and modal analysis of high-dimensional flow data. The framework uses conditional Granger causality estimates to regularize the identification of the DMD operator, preferentially suppressing weakly supported interactions while preserving dynamically relevant couplings. This regularization promotes sparse operators informed by directional predictive dependencies, leading to improved predictive accuracy and identification of dynamically relevant modes. GC-DMD is evaluated on a linear system with a known coupling structure, lid-driven cavity flows with varying dynamical complexity, and a broadband transonic turbulent jet. Compared with standard DMD, GC-DMD improves operator identification accuracy in the linear benchmark and improves the robustness of the identified spectrum while reducing long-horizon prediction errors in the cavity flows. For the turbulent jet, it yields a sparser operator with improved prediction accuracy. These results demonstrate the potential of incorporating causality information to guide the identification of sparse, predictive reduced-order models of complex flows. The \href{https://github.com/JiaqingKou/GC-DMD-Granger-Causality-informed-DMD}{source codes} \footnote{https://github.com/JiaqingKou/GC-DMD-Granger-Causality-informed-DMD} from this research are publicly available in the GitHub repository.

</details>

*Daisuke Nishiyama, Yuji Hattori*

TL;DR: 该文通过直接数值模拟研究带轴向流量的螺旋涡在椭圆或曲率不稳定模态扰动下的非线性演化，系统改变涡核半径与折合螺距以覆盖不同线性增长率，发现小增长率下弱非线性饱和、大增长率下显著非线性变形，并讨论对风力机叶尖涡控制的启示。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 研究螺旋涡在椭圆或曲率不稳定模态扰动下的非线性演化，以厘清不同线性增长率对应的非线性行为；螺旋涡可作为旋转叶片叶尖涡的模型，因此结果旨在为风力机叶尖涡控制提供依据，潜在加速尾流恢复并改善机组性能。

方法： 采用直接数值模拟，系统改变归一化涡核半径和折合螺距，以覆盖较宽的线性增长率范围，并将结果与弱非线性分析进行比较。

结果： 观察到两类非线性演化：线性增长率较小时，椭圆和曲率不稳定均表现为弱非线性饱和，涡与不稳定模态结构基本保持；结合以往其他涡构型结果，表明弱非线性饱和是短波不稳定性的普遍特征。线性增长率较大时，不稳定性达到更高能量水平并超出弱非线性区，涡与不稳定模态结构显著变形；比较表明更大的线性增长率需要更大的扰动幅值才能使非线性效应平衡线性增长，此时高阶非线性效应及弱非线性分析未保留的模态分量变得更重要，从而给出偏离弱非线性区的新物理解释。

结论： 研究揭示了螺旋涡非线性演化的两类路径，并强调弱非线性饱和对短波不稳定性的普遍性以及大增长率下高阶非线性效应的重要性；由于螺旋涡可作为旋转叶片叶尖涡的模型，结果可为风力机叶尖涡控制提供参考，有望加速尾流恢复并改善性能。

原始摘要： The nonlinear evolution of a helical vortex with axial flow disturbed by an elliptic or curvature instability mode is investigated by direct numerical simulations. The normalized vortex core radius and reduced pitch are systematically varied to examine a wide range of linear growth rates, revealing two types of nonlinear evolution. At relatively small growth rates, both instabilities exhibit weakly nonlinear saturation, with the vortex and unstable mode structures largely preserved. Together with previous findings for other vortex configurations, these results reveal that weakly nonlinear saturation is a generic feature of short-wave instabilities. At larger growth rates, the instabilities reach higher energy levels and evolve beyond the weakly nonlinear regime, with the vortex and unstable mode structures substantially deformed. Comparison with weakly nonlinear analysis suggests that larger linear growth rates require larger disturbance amplitudes for nonlinear effects to balance linear growth. At these larger amplitudes, higher-order nonlinear effects and modal components not retained in the weakly nonlinear analysis become increasingly important, as demonstrated by the numerical results. This provides a new physical interpretation of the departure from the weakly nonlinear regime. Since helical vortices serve as a model for tip vortices shed from rotating blades, these results offer useful insights for tip-vortex control in wind turbines, which can potentially accelerate wake recovery and thereby improve turbine performance.

</details>

  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 旨在理解液态金属电池电极中电磁驱动旋流的流动演化与混沌转捩，表征背景磁场、电流和几何构型对转捩阈值的影响，并解释已有研究报告的混沌起始磁场随电流和长径比变化的现象。

结果： 在多种几何和强迫条件下观察到一致的动力学转捩序列；流动经Ruelle–Takens–Newhouse路径进入混沌，奇异非混沌吸引子（SNA）作为混沌前的中间状态；固定长径比时转捩由Qm控制，振荡流起始的临界Qm几乎不变，但改变域高度时例外；结果在中等强迫电磁驱动旋流中遵循Re∼Qm^{2/3}的扭矩平衡标度律。

原始摘要： Flow transitions in electromagnetically-driven swirl in a cylindrical domain are investigated using three-dimensional simulations for a liquid metal battery electrode. The parameter space considered: applied current $I=11.11-100A$; Current density, $J_0 \approx 4,000-35,000 A/m^2$, domain radius, $R=3-9cm$, domain height, $H=3$ and $4.5cm$). The background magnetic field, $B_0$, strength is varied systematically from 0.1 to 20 G to characterize the route to chaos. \cite{davidson2022swirling} found the onset of chaos occurs around $1-1.5 G$ for aspect ratio $1/6$ and current of 100A, however, this is shifted to $16-17 G$ for a lower current of 16A and aspect ratio $1/2$. Across a range of geometrical configurations and forcing conditions, including variations in aspect ratio, current and current density, a consistent sequence of dynamical transitions is evident from phase portraits, FFT spectrum, 0-1 chaos test, autocorrelation function and correlation dimension. The flow evolution is by the Ruelle--Takens-Newhouse route to chaos, with the strange non-chaotic attractor (SNA) regime providing an intermediate state prior to the onset of chaos. In our results, it is observed that, for a fixed aspect ratio, the transition is governed by a dimensionless group, $Q_m$, representing the ratio of applied Lorentz force to viscous force. The critical value of $Q_m$ at the onset of oscillatory flow is found to be nearly invariant except when the domain height is varied. Moreover, our results follow the $Re \sim Q_m^{2/3}$ scaling law expected from torque balance moderately-forced electro-magnetically driven swirl flows.

</details>

原始摘要： This study numerically investigates the effects of geometrical curvature on drop deformation and breakup in shear flow between counter-rotating concentric cylinders. Three circular geometries with different values of the non-dimensional geometrical curvature, $κ_{\mathrm F}$, were considered and compared with a planar shear flow system. At fixed Reynolds and Capillary numbers ($Re$ and $Ca$), the breakup morphology differed substantially between the planar and circular systems. In the planar system, the drop underwent relatively symmetric neck thinning, whereas in the circular systems, the drop exhibited asymmetric deformation and pronounced azimuthal elongation. At higher curvature, the drop formed a thin filament that subsequently fragmented, producing multiple satellite drops. Analysis of the internal velocity field and the magnitude of the rate-of-deformation tensor revealed distinct flow and deformation structures associated with these breakup behaviors. Furthermore, the critical $Re$ for breakup, $Re_{\mathrm{cr}}$, decreased with increasing $κ_{\mathrm F}$ at nvestigated $Ca$ values. In contrast, the maximum dimensionless elongation length immediately before breakup at $Re=Re_{\mathrm{cr}}$ increased with $κ_{\mathrm F}$. These findings suggest that geometrical curvature affects not only the breakup threshold but also the deformation process leading to breakup, providing insights into drop breakup in curved shear flows.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>

原始摘要： Direct numerical simulations are performed to investigate supersonic turbulent boundary layers subjected to continuous concave and convex surface curvatures. Scale-resolved spectral turbulent kinetic energy (TKE) budgets, triadic energy-transfer bispectra, spectral proper orthogonal decomposition (SPOD), and global resolvent analysis are employed to elucidate the effects of compressibility and pressure gradients, and to distinguish the linear and nonlinear mechanisms at play. Over concave walls, the destabilizing curvature enhances pressure-strain redistribution, shifting near-wall turbulence toward a two-component state, and induces a shear-driven outer-layer production peak at low spanwise wavenumbers. Bispectral and SPOD analyses reveal that this outer-layer energy feeds a structured, multi-step triadic transfer toward fundamental large-scale and spanwise-uniform modes, which physically manifests as streamwise vortex breakdown rather than a three-dimensional inverse cascade. Global resolvent analysis further demonstrates that while nonlinear triadic interactions select the dominant scale amplitudes, the spatial topology of these coherent structures is dictated by linear mean-flow receptivity and spatially developing convective instabilities. Conversely, over convex walls, stabilizing curvature and dilatation attenuate pressure fluctuations and suppress pressure-strain redistribution, breaking the near-wall turbulent cycle and trapping energy within elongated, one-component streamwise streaks, while a localized resolvent wavemaker energizes the downstream recovery region.

</details>

方法： 文中通过论证展示：在适当情况下，染料可准确追踪涡丝中心线；并给出以涡旋中心线形状计算能量和螺旋度等重要流体量的方式；同时讨论未来可用于提取更详细涡旋信息的技术。摘要未提供这些条件、推导细节或具体实验方案。

结果： 研究表明，在适当条件下染料能够准确追踪涡丝中心线；能量和螺旋度等重要流体量可由涡旋中心线形状计算；因此染料追踪的涡旋可作为在多种实验情形下测量流体运动性质的有价值工具。摘要未给出定量验证结果或具体实验数据。

结论： 染料并非在所有情况下都能追踪涡旋运动，但在适当条件下可准确追踪涡丝中心线，并用于推断能量、螺旋度等流体性质；染料追踪涡旋因而具有实验应用价值，未来还可发展更详细的涡旋信息提取技术。

原始摘要： Although vorticity and passive tracers (such as dye) are both transported by fluid flows, there are subtle differences in how this transport happens. This indicates that dye cannot be used to track vortex motion in experiments, even though it has often been used for this purpose. In this manuscript we show that under proper circumstances dye can be used to accurately track the center-lines of vortex filaments. Moreover, we show that a number of important fluid quantities, such as energy and helicity, can be computed in terms of the shape of the vortex center-line. As a result, dye-tracked vortices are a valuable tool for measuring the properties of fluid motion in a variety of experimental situations. Moreover, we discuss techniques by which could be used to extract more detailed vortex information in future work.

</details>

原始摘要： In the study, the geometry of curved rectangular vortex generators (RVG) has been optimized to enhance heat transfer and overall performance of the analyzed water-to-air finned-tube heat exchanger (FTHE). The developed and validated conjugate numerical model includes a small section of the full-scale heat exchanger representing the computational domain, with fully developed velocity and temperature profiles specified at the inlet boundaries of the water. Four influential geometry parameters of the vortex generator were selected for the optimization; attack angle, curvature angle, axial position in the streamwise direction and height. The optimization process has been performed according to two criteria: maximization of heat transfer solely and maximization of heat transfer while considering the influence of pressure drop. Accordingly, one objective function related to the Colburn factor (j) and two objective functions combining the Colburn factor and the friction factor (f) were defined. The regression analysis has been used and the Central composite design of experiment was selected in order to obtain non-linear regression polynomial functions which were integrated into the investigated objectives. The optimization procedure has been performed using the genetic algorithm. Optimized configurations performance improvement was evaluated through comparison with the performance of the reference FTHE with plain fins. For the RVG configuration optimized according to the objective of maximizing heat transfer, the Colburn factor was enhanced by 53.1%. Optimizing the RVG configuration to maximize heat transfer while accounting for the influence of pressure drop resulted in an enhancement of the FTHE area goodness factor by 14.4% for one set of geometry parameters, and an enhancement of volume goodness factor by 26.5% for another.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： PINN用于浮力驱动流时存在训练困难与精度问题；作者希望确定无量纲化选择是否是影响PINN可训练性和准确性的重要设计因素，并据此改进训练表现。

方法： 在Pr=0.71、Ra=10^3–10^5的差异加热方腔中，在相同训练条件下比较热扩散缩放与自由落体缩放；分析损失梯度并进行直接梯度测量；测试稀疏数据监督和自适应损失加权；并将自由落体缩放PINN用于非定常对流。摘要未提供具体网络结构、优化器和训练超参数等细节。

结果： 自由落体缩放采用单位浮力系数，在Ra≤10^4时平均Nusselt数误差低于1.1%；扩散缩放的浮力系数为Ra Pr，产生157–650%的误差，并在Ra=10^5时发散。主导的浮力相关损失梯度按(Ra Pr)^2缩放，直接梯度测量证实了系数诱导的梯度失衡。稀疏数据监督和自适应损失加权在测试条件下均未解决训练困难。非定常情况下，自由落体PINN能准确预测长期传热，但未能捕捉瞬态Nusselt数过冲。

结论： 无量纲化是提升浮力驱动流PINN可训练性和精度的重要设计考虑；自由落体缩放表现更优，而扩散缩放会引入严重梯度失衡和训练失败；稀疏监督与自适应损失加权未能缓解该问题，非定常瞬态过冲预测仍存在不足。

原始摘要： We investigate the influence of non-dimensionalization on the trainability and accuracy of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for buoyancy-driven flows. Using a differentially heated square cavity at $\mathrm{Pr}=0.71$ and $\mathrm{Ra}=10^3$--$10^5$, we compare thermal-diffusive and free-fall velocity scalings under identical training conditions. The free-fall scaling, with a unit buoyancy coefficient, achieves errors below 1.1\% in the mean Nusselt number for $\mathrm{Ra}\le10^4$, whereas the diffusive scaling, with a buoyancy coefficient of $\mathrm{Ra}\,\mathrm{Pr}$, produces errors of 157--650\% and diverges at $\mathrm{Ra}=10^5$. We identify a coefficient-induced gradient imbalance, with the dominant buoyancy-related contribution to the loss gradient scaling as $(\mathrm{Ra}\,\mathrm{Pr})^2$, and confirm it through direct gradient measurements. Neither sparse-data supervision nor adaptive loss weighting resolves the training difficulties under the tested conditions. For unsteady convection, the free-fall PINN accurately predicts long-time heat transfer but fails to capture the transient Nusselt-number overshoot. These findings establish non-dimensionalization as an important design consideration for improving PINN trainability in buoyancy-driven flows.

</details>

*Ehsan Roohi, Christopher J. McDevitt*

TL;DR: 该文用无数据PINNs求解深宽比5的二维稳态不可压缩顶盖驱动方腔流（Re=100、250、500、1000），以独立谱/hp解为参考，评估硬约束流函数、区域配点、迁移初始化及残差缩放/包络变体；内部相对速度误差为5.130%–7.850%，底板区低于5%，Re=1000细化流程可将内部速度误差由5.624%降至2.658%。

结果： 在121×605网格上，内部相对速度差为5.130%–7.850%；底板区相对速度差低于5%，绝对RMS差为1.255×10^-10U至2.332×10^-9U。Re=1000的161×801评估中，细化工作流将内部速度差从5.624%降至2.658%，规范对齐压力RMS差从2.793×10^-3降至1.167×10^-3 ρU²。改进来自累积训练流程比较，未单独隔离区域采样贡献；配置排名随区域和观测量变化。

结论： 有限预算比较表明，保留的工作流在部分区域与参考解一致，但内部速度及旋转场差异仍可能显著；深腔PINN评估不能仅依赖整体速度一致性，应按区域和观测量分别报告。摘要未提供训练成本、逐案例误差分解及区域采样的单独消融结果。

原始摘要： Deep lid-driven cavities contain strong motion beneath the lid and much weaker recirculation at depth, so agreement in bulk velocity is an incomplete assessment of a computed flow. We examine data-free physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for steady, two-dimensional incompressible flow in a cavity of depth-to-width ratio five at Reynolds numbers 100, 250, 500 and 1000. An established hard-constrained streamfunction formulation is combined with prescribed regional collocation, transferred initialization, and selected residual-scaling and envelope variants. Independent spectral/$hp$ calculations with the same Gaussian-regularized lid provide the reference fields. For a cavity of depth-to-width ratio $D/W=5$ at Reynolds numbers $\mathrm{Re}=100$, 250, 500, and 1000, evaluation on a common $121\times605$ grid gives interior relative velocity discrepancies of 5.130\%--7.850\%. Over the same cases, floor-region relative velocity discrepancies remain below 5\%, with absolute RMS discrepancies ranging from $1.255\times10^{-10}U$ to $2.332\times10^{-9}U$, where $U$ is the lid-velocity scale. In a separate $161\times801$ assessment at $\Rey=1000$, the refined workflow lowers interior velocity discrepancy from 5.624\% to 2.658\% and gauge-aligned pressure RMS discrepancy from $2.793\times10^{-3}$ to $1.167\times10^{-3}ρU^2$. The improvements compare accumulated training workflows and do not isolate regional sampling. Configuration rankings depend on both region and observable. These finite-budget comparisons establish where the retained workflows agree with the reference and where rotational-field discrepancies remain substantial.

</details>


### [78] [Identifiability Limits of Reynolds-Stress Attribution in Localized Nonorthogonal Flow Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38743)
*Peng Cheng, Ming Jian, Wen Tong*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 论文检验Reynolds应力能否按命名涡结构可分离地组装：在JHTDB均匀各向同性湍流中，用冻结无散的各向异性Gaussian涡量函数展开构造blob、sheet、tube局部几何，并通过Biot-Savart映射把系数协方差精确传入Gaussian速度应力。结果表明，sheet与tube家族速度张成空间高度重叠，共享方向内乘积贡献99.6%的协方差抵消；移除家族间协方差会使DNS参考应力误差增至53.6–87倍，而Fourier/POD正交重组会消除该抵消。因此局部Reynolds应力由耦合速度脉动承载，而非可加分量能量。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： Reynolds应力由速度脉动间相关组装；若要把应力归因于命名涡结构，这些结构的 velocity field 必须可分离。论文旨在检验这一可分离性要求，并厘清局部涡形态标签与Reynolds应力组装之间的关系。

方法： 使用Johns Hopkins Turbulence Databases（JHTDB）的均匀各向同性湍流，采用curl-lifted各向异性Gaussian涡量函数的冻结、无散展开，保留局部可寻址的blob状、sheet状和tube状几何；其固定Biot-Savart映射将系数协方差精确转化为所表示Gaussian速度的应力。利用主角度几何及自协方差/交叉协方差，将不同家族群速度张成空间的重叠与应力组装联系起来；并比较去除家族间协方差以及正交Fourier、POD重组的结果，以DNS参考应力评估误差。

结果： 在主角余弦为0.95时，55%的sheet关联方向也可由tube-like家族紧密表示，且共享方向内的乘积承载99.6%的协方差抵消。去除家族间协方差后，DNS参考的应力误差增加53.6至87倍。对同一速度进行正交Fourier和POD重组会消除这种抵消，说明抵消来自局部非正交组织，而非仅由所表示的速度场决定。形态标签仍依赖表示方式，但其物理后果是直接的。

结论： 在该局部描述中，Reynolds应力由耦合的速度脉动承载，而不是由可加的分量能量承载。局部非正交表示能够揭示湍流动量通量如何组装，但前提是保留速度张成空间重叠与交叉协方差；形态标签本身具有表示依赖性。

原始摘要： Reynolds stress is assembled from correlations among velocity fluctuations; assigning it to named vortical structures additionally requires their velocity fields to be separable. We examine this requirement in homogeneous isotropic turbulence from the Johns Hopkins Turbulence Databases (JHTDB) using a frozen solenoidal expansion of curl-lifted anisotropic Gaussian vorticity functions. The expansion retains locally addressable blob-, sheet-, and tube-like geometry, while its fixed Biot-Savart map carries coefficient covariance exactly into the stress of the represented Gaussian velocity. Principal-angle geometry and self-cross covariance then connect overlap between group velocity spans to stress assembly. At a principal cosine of 0.95, 55% of the sheet-associated directions are also closely represented by the tube-like family, and products within the shared directions carry 99.6% of the covariance cancellation. Removing between-family covariance increases the direct numerical simulation (DNS)-referenced stress error by factors of 53.6 to 87. Orthogonal Fourier and proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) regroupings of the same velocity remove this cancellation, showing that it follows from the local nonorthogonal organization rather than from the represented field alone. The morphology labels remain representation-dependent, but their physical consequence is direct: Reynolds stress in this local description is carried by coupled velocity fluctuations, not by additive component energies. Localized nonorthogonal representations can therefore reveal how turbulent momentum flux is assembled, provided that span overlap and cross covariance are retained.

</details>

*Vassilis Theofilis*

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 将不可压缩剪切流的模态与非模态线性稳定性分析从宏观方程推进到动理论层面，直接线性化Boltzmann方程及完整碰撞算子，并让硬球算子与多种松弛模型在相同离散框架下可直接比较。

方法： 在Chapman-Enskog基态下以特异速度表述，使碰撞算子与横向坐标无关并只需构造一次；硬球算子及BGK、ES-BGK、Shakhov模型采用相同离散。数值上采用分部求和Legendre配点、弱扩散反射壁条件和矩一致的速率导数，构造能量稳定离散算子并用制造解验证谱精度；用Gamba等快速谱方法处理完整算子。模态kLST采用无矩阵位移-反演Arnoldi迭代并以精确松弛模型逆预条件；非模态kLST采用含Chu扰动能量的能量范数和动理论Reynolds-Orr方程，瞬态增长与伪谱由同类预条件求解得到。

结果： 对Re=10^2–10^5的平面Couette流、Poiseuille流、混合层和Blasius边界层，四种算子均在不可压缩极限下恢复Orr-Sommerfeld特征值：模型误差约为10^-7量级，完整算子约为10^-6；Butler和Farrell的最优增长及Trefethen等的预解范数复现到0.1%，伪谱在病态特征值不收敛处仍收敛。

结论： kLST使与TriGlobal问题规模相当的四维动理论线性稳定性计算成为可行，提供了统一、能量稳定且高精度的动理论模态与非模态稳定性框架，并验证了完整碰撞算子及松弛模型与经典不可压缩稳定性结果的一致性。

原始摘要： Modal and non-modal linear stability analysis of incompressible shear flows is performed for the first time by linearizing the Boltzmann equation and its full collision operator. In this kinetic linear stability theory (kLST) the hard-sphere operator and its BGK, ES-BGK and Shakhov relaxation models share the same discretization, so that their results can be compared directly. Written in the peculiar velocity about a Chapman-Enskog base state, the collision operators are independent of the cross-stream coordinate and are formed once. Summation-by-parts Legendre collocation, weak diffuse-reflection wall conditions and a moment-consistent velocity derivative yield an energy-stable discrete operator, verified to spectral accuracy by a manufactured solution. Numerical solution of the four-dimensional problem, comparable in size to a TriGlobal problem (Theofilis 2011), is made feasible by the fast spectral method of Gamba, Haack, Hauck and Hu (2017) for the full operator. Modal kLST uses matrix-free shift-invert Arnoldi iteration, preconditioned by an exact relaxation-model inverse. Non-modal kLST is based on an energy norm containing Chu's disturbance energy, a kinetic Reynolds-Orr equation identifying the mean shear as the only energy source. Transient growth and pseudospectra follow from the same preconditioned solves. For plane Couette and Poiseuille flows, the mixing layer and the Blasius boundary layer at Reynolds numbers 10^2-10^5, all four operators recover the Orr-Sommerfeld eigenvalues (Orszag 1971; Mack 1976) in the incompressible limit to a few parts in 10^7 for the models and 10^6 for the full operator. The optimal growth of Butler and Farrell (1992) and the resolvent norms of Trefethen et al. (1993) are reproduced to 0.1%, while the pseudospectra converge where ill-conditioned eigenvalues do not.

</details>

These models typically assume that the wave trains are comprised of a narrow band of frequencies. We present measurements from a new series of laboratory experiments in which the broadbandedness is increased and show that generalizations of the NLS equation can accurately model these measurements.

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 高Péclet数输运问题会产生薄边界层和陡峭近壁梯度，精确求解需要高分辨率网格，并给反演建模带来挑战；论文旨在降低此类多尺度输运问题正演与反演的计算成本。

方法： 构建基于扰动理论的重叠Schwarz域分解框架：内方程描述近壁动力学，外方程描述主流输运，二者均采用有限元离散并组装为完全可微的多域物理求解器；利用耦合求解器的可微性，将控制方程和边界条件作为硬约束、由Schwarz耦合保证界面连续性来建立反问题，并采用基于伴随的优化。为加速优化，引入LLM的两种配置：一是根据伴随灵敏度提出连续参数更新的独立优化器；二是可访问完整求解流程、执行多阶段优化的智能体AI。当空间变化未知量采用函数基而非高维节点场表示时，智能体AI按预定准入策略在优化过程中扩充候选基函数库，分阶段接纳预定义函数族并初始化其参数。

结果： 该表述允许使用较粗网格，相对单域表述降低计算成本；智能体AI配置在未知量采用函数基表示而非高维节点场表示时最具优势，并能在优化中扩充候选基函数库。摘要未提供定量精度、加速比或具体反演误差数据。

结论： 所提框架将Schwarz耦合的可微物理求解与智能体AI优化相结合，用于多尺度输运问题的反演建模。

原始摘要： High-Péclet-number transport problems give rise to thin boundary layers with steep near-wall gradients whose accurate solution requires high-resolution meshes and could challenge inverse modeling. To address this challenge, we develop an overlapping Schwarz domain-decomposition framework grounded in perturbation theory, in which an inner equation governs near-wall dynamics and an outer equation governs bulk transport, and both subproblems are discretized using the finite element method and assembled into a fully differentiable multidomain physics solver. The formulation permits coarser mesh resolutions, reducing computational cost relative to a single-domain formulation. Exploiting the differentiability of the coupled solver, we formulate inverse problems in which the governing equations and boundary conditions are imposed as hard constraints, with the Schwarz coupling enforcing interface continuity. To accelerate the resulting adjoint-based optimization, we integrate large language models (LLMs) into the inverse workflow in two distinct configurations: a standalone optimizer that proposes successive parameter updates from the adjoint-computed sensitivities and an agentic artificial intelligence (AI) approach with access to the complete solver workflow, enabling it to perform multistage optimization procedures. The agentic AI configuration is shown to be most advantageous when the spatially varying unknown is represented in a functional basis rather than a high-dimensional nodal field representation. In this case, the AI agent grows the library of candidate basis functions during optimization under a prescribed admission policy, admitting predefined families in stages and initializing their parameters. In short, our proposed framework integrates Schwarz-coupled differentiable physics with agentic AI optimization for the inverse modeling of multiscale transport problems.

</details>
