TL;DR: 研究了圆盘内正负 Onsager 点涡组成的不平衡气体的统计力学与非平衡动力学，并通过保持涡量不平衡的湮灭-再注入机制，分析其向偶极相干结构或纬向流的自组织及二者之间的转变。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 受旋转玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚实验启发，希望理解受限圆盘中相反符号 Onsager 点涡构成的不平衡气体在外部驱动和耗散下的统计力学与非平衡动力学。

方法： 在点涡哈密顿动力学基础上加入保持不平衡的湮灭-再注入机制：异号涡旋距离小于代表愈合长度的尺度时湮灭，并在随机位置重新注入；结合统计与流体力学分析，并在强混合区域通过将 Joyce-Montgomery 平均场理论在弱非均匀态附近线性化来刻画相图。

结果： 在正能量下，低不平衡时系统自组织为大规模相干偶极，较高不平衡时形成纬向流；尽管湮灭-再注入破坏能量和角动量守恒，涡旋气体被认为沿平衡相空间准绝热演化。偶极与纬向流之间的拓扑转变反映在能量 e、角动量 μ 和涡旋不平衡 η 构成的非平凡平衡相图中；线性理论对含湮灭-再注入的数值模拟出人意料地适用，并表明 (e,μ,η) 相空间中偶极与纬向构型的选择由流体力学剪切不稳定性驱动。

结论： 该工作从统计和流体力学两个角度描述了偶极流与纬向流之间的转变，指出湮灭-再注入下系统可准绝热地经过平衡相空间，并强调流体力学剪切不稳定性在构型选择中的作用；摘要未提供具体定量参数或实验验证细节。

原始摘要： Motivated by experiments on rotating Bose-Einstein condensates, we investigate the statistical mechanics and the non-equilibrium dynamics of an imbalanced gas of oppositely signed Onsager point vortices confined in a disk. To model external forcing and dissipation, we supplement their Hamiltonian dynamics with an annihilation-reinjection (AR) mechanism that preserves the imbalance: oppositely signed vortices are annihilated when they approach closer than a distance representing the healing length, and are subsequently reintroduced into the domain at random locations. At positive energies, the system self-organizes into a large-scale coherent dipole at low imbalance and into a zonal flow at higher imbalance. While AR breaks energy and angular momentum conservation, we argue that the vortex gas evolves quasi-adiabatically through the equilibrium phase space. We capture the topological transition between dipolar and zonal flows both from a statistical and a hydrodynamical perspective. In particular, the bifurcation reflects a non-trivial equilibrium diagram in terms of energy $e$, angular momentum $μ$, and vortex imbalance $η$. We characterize this phase diagram in the "strong mixing'' regime studied by linearizing the Joyce-Montgomery mean-field theory about the weakly non-uniform state. We show that the linear theory is surprisingly relevant for the numerical simulations with AR and suggest that the selection between the dipolar and the zonal configurations in the $(e,μ,η)$ phase space is driven by a hydrodynamical shear instability.

</details>

*Sahrish B. Naqvi, Dawid Strzelczyk, Maciej Tadej, Maciej Matyka*

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 核心动机是检验一个假设：多孔介质流动中已知的惯性效应，是否也能在仅由单个通道构成的简单系统中观察到；惯性强度由Re控制，并选用6级Koch叶形腔体，以兼顾规则性、可重复性和代表性，同时引入复杂多尺度结构与非平凡水动力相互作用。

方法： 研究流体通过带Koch叶形腔体通道的流动；通过摩擦因子刻画非线性效应及对Darcy定律的偏离，并考察惯性对流动结构的影响，包括参与数π、回流分数ρ⁻、迂曲度和表观渗透率，以及局部动能分布P(ε)∝ε^(-m)。摘要未给出具体实验或数值方法、Re范围及通道几何尺寸。

结果： 摩擦因子显示对Darcy定律的偏离很细微；与无序多孔介质相反，参与数π随Re降低，说明惯性将动能集中于主通流，而回流（逆向流）在Koch叶腔体中所占比例增大；迂曲度随Re非单调变化，表观渗透率下降，惯性区二者呈幂律关系且指数与天然岩石相当；在最低和最高Re下，局部动能分布在多个数量级上均呈P(ε)∝ε^(-m)，m≈0.83，惯性仅改变分布尾部。

结论： 该单通道Koch叶腔体系统可呈现多孔介质流动中的惯性效应，但其对Darcy定律的偏离较隐蔽；惯性会重塑流场并降低表观渗透率。由于孔隙率φ≈1，该研究可视为对极端多孔系统中输运问题的考察。摘要未给出更广泛的工程外推或定量验证细节。

原始摘要： The main hypothesis of this paper is that inertial effects known from porous-media flow can be observed even in a simple system consisting of a single channel. The strength of inertia is controlled by the Reynolds number, $Re$. We investigated fluid flow through a channel with a cavity in the shape of a Koch leaf. The Koch leaf of level-6 was chosen because it combines regularity, repeatability, and representativeness with a complex multiscale structure and non-trivial hydrodynamic interactions. We analyzed nonlinear effects through the so-called friction factor and show that its deviation from Darcy's law is very subtle. We further examined the influence of inertia on the flow structure, including the participation number $π$ and the backward-flow fraction $ρ^{-}$. In contrast to disordered porous media, the participation number decreases with $Re$, indicating that inertia concentrates kinetic energy in the main through-flow, while recirculation (backward flow) increasingly occupies the Koch leaf cavity. The tortuosity varies non-monotonically with $Re$, the apparent permeability decreases, and in the inertial regime the two are related by a power law with an exponent comparable to that found for natural rock. At the lowest and highest Reynolds numbers, the local kinetic energy followed a power-law distribution, $P(\varepsilon)\propto\varepsilon^{-m}$, over several orders of magnitude with the same exponent, $m\approx0.83$; inertia modified only the tails of the distribution. Our research can be regarded as an investigation of transport in an extremely porous system with porosity $\varphi \approx 1$.

</details>

*Matei Ioan Radulescu*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文解释爆轰胞格尺寸λ为何能作为爆轰动力学的特征尺度，并建立基于点火延迟分布的宏观反应率模型，将其用于全局弯曲胞格爆轰，获得与实验的良好一致。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 现有研究表明λ可作为多种爆轰动力学参数的缩放尺度，例如向开放空间传播的临界管径约为13λ，但为何这种缩放关系在多种反应体系中普遍成立尚缺乏解释。

方法： 构建以胞格爆轰点火延迟分布为核心的宏观反应率模型，并用λ对分布进行条件化；点火延迟分布由Radulescu（JFM 2025）提出的单胞前导激波动力学闭式模型描述，所得闭式分布用作胞格平均描述中的相平均反应率模型，并应用于全局弯曲胞格爆轰问题。

结果： 模型应用于全局弯曲胞格爆轰时与实验取得良好一致；论文首次给出了胞格爆轰的简单动力学闭合，并说明胞格尺寸提供爆轰相关长度尺度的原因。摘要未提供具体实验数据或误差指标。

结论： λ之所以能作为爆轰的缩放参数，是因为它给出了平均爆轰结构的长度，即爆轰的流体动力学厚度。

原始摘要： The detonation cell size $λ$ is known to provide a characteristic scale for the detonation dynamics and serves as scaling parameter for numerous correlations with other dynamic parameters of detonations, e.g., the critical detonation tube diameter for transmission to open space of $d\simeq13λ$ across many reactive systems. In the present paper, we address the reason why such scaling works so well. We formulate a macroscopic reaction rate model for cellular detonations relying on the distribution of ignition delays in cellular detonations, conditioned by $λ$. The ignition delay distribution is modeled in terms of the cellular dynamics, using the closed form model recently proposed by Radulescu (JFM 2025) for the lead shock dynamics in a unit cell. The distribution of ignition delays obtained in closed form serves as a simple model for the phase-averaged reaction rate in a cell-averaged description. The global reaction rate model is applied to the problem of globally curved cellular detonations. Excellent agreement with experiment is obtained. The paper thus highlights for the first time a simple dynamic closure for cellular detonations and provides the simple answer why the cell size provides the relevant length scale for detonations: it provides the length of the mean detonation structure, or its hydrodynamic thickness.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 管道流中的局部湍流 puff 通过增殖维持自身，这一过程对亚临界层流-湍流转捩至关重要；该转捩是涉及广泛自然与工程系统的百年基础问题，因此需要刻画 puff 分裂的完整动力学。

方法： 对约化模型开展非线性非模态分析，以捕获分裂过程的完整 Navier-Stokes 动力学，包括从分裂种子到边缘态再到成功分裂的演化，即使预边缘演化被 puff 内在混沌涨落掩盖；进一步利用优化演化提供分裂进程坐标，构建增殖的相空间表征，解决不同混沌实现之间的时间去同步问题，并进行动态对齐的系综平均。

结果： 分裂种子具有强速度拐点特征；其能量阈值随雷诺数呈幂律缩放，使最优解在自发 puff 分裂中具有直接的物理一致性；所构造的分裂进程坐标和相空间表征可对齐混沌实现并揭示潜在分裂动力学。

原始摘要： The localized turbulence in pipe flow, known as puffs, is sustained by their proliferation. This process is crucial to the subcritical laminar-turbulent transition, a century-old fundamental problem related to a wide range of natural and engineered systems. Here, we leverage the nonlinear nonmodal analysis of a reduced-order model to capture the full Navier-Stokes dynamics of the splitting process: from the splitting seed through the edge state to successful splitting, even when the pre-edge evolution is obscured by the puff's intrinsic chaotic fluctuations. Moreover, the optimized evolution provides a splitting-progress coordinate to frame a phase-space representation of the proliferation, resolving the temporal desynchronization among chaotic realizations and thereby enabling dynamically aligned ensemble averaging to uncover the underlying splitting dynamics. The splitting seed is characterized by a strong velocity inflection, and its energy threshold exhibits a power-law scaling with the Reynolds number, giving the optimal solution direct physical consistency in spontaneous puff splitting.

</details>


### [86] [Stationary Structures of the Boussinesq equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40334)
*Marc Nualart, Jaemin Park, Javier Peñafiel-Tomás*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 文章研究周期通道中无粘二维 Boussinesq 方程的稳态构型，证明闭非收缩层流速度场与单调密度分层耦合时必为分层剪切流，并给出非剪切稳态解及分层剪切流附近的刚性与柔性结果。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 探究周期通道内无粘二维 Boussinesq 方程稳态解的结构，重点理解密度分层与层流速度场耦合时的刚性约束，并与 Euler 方程进行对比。

方法： 采用理论研究：分析稳态 Boussinesq 方程；利用密度沿水平集的单调性；构造周期通道中的层流非停滞非剪切稳态解；研究分层剪切流附近非剪切解的层流保持或岛状结构出现；并建立统一的 Crandall-Rabinowitz 分岔定理作为副产品。

结果： 证明闭非收缩流体轨迹与不减密度分层耦合必然导致分层剪切流；与 Euler 方程形成鲜明对比，存在层流非停滞非剪切稳态解；根据驻点给出分层剪切流附近不同的刚性与柔性结果；建立统一的 Crandall-Rabinowitz 分岔定理。

结论： 密度单调性沿水平集的方向是关键因素，决定了 Boussinesq 方程与 Euler 方程在稳态解结构上的差异；分层剪切流附近的行为由驻点决定；统一的 Crandall-Rabinowitz 分岔定理可能具有独立价值。摘要未提供具体证明细节、定量估计或参数范围。

原始摘要： We investigate the steady configurations supported by the inviscid two-dimensional Boussinesq equations in the periodic channel. We show steady laminar velocity fields (closed non-contractible fluid trajectories) coupled to non-decreasing density stratifications are necessarily stratified shear flows. The direction of density monotonicity along level sets is in fact fundamental: in striking contrast with the Euler equations, we exhibit laminar non-stagnant non-shear stationary solutions in the periodic channel. We also provide distinct flexibility and rigidity results (laminar persistence or appearance of islands for nearby non-shear solutions) close to stratified shear flows according to their stagnation points. As a by-product of our analysis, we establish a uniform Crandall-Rabinowitz bifurcation theorem that might be of independent interest.

</details>


### [87] [Agentic AI Reasoning and Learned Sampling for Task-Aware Projection Selection in Sparse-View CT: Predicting Hemodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39003)
*Mahmoud Elhadidy, Roshan M. D'Souza, Amirhossein Arzani*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏视图CTA中面向下游血流动力学预测的任务感知投影选择：用CFD仿真生成合成扇束CTA投影，训练FNO预测TAWSS，并比较固定采样与多种优化选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 稀疏视图CT虽减少投影数量，但传统采样策略主要面向图像重建而非下游物理推断；作者希望研究智能体AI能否发现优于现有方法的采样策略，并直接围绕下游血流动力学量设计稀疏采集。

方法： 基于颅内动脉瘤的CFD仿真生成合成扇束CTA投影，训练FNO预测空间分辨的TAWSS场和空间平均TAWSS，并将FNO与最优输入选择耦合以同时优化采样与训练；在不同视图选择预算下，将固定均匀采样、黄金角采样与Gumbel-Softmax、Soft top-K及智能体AI选择器三种任务感知策略进行比较，并分析AI智能体的推理与性能。

结果： 对空间分辨TAWSS，Agentic与Soft top-K平均误差最低，Agentic略优，并在极端稀疏下显著优于固定采样；对空间平均TAWSS，优化选择在极稀疏采集时更重要。所选掩码呈现多个集中于信息区域的 distinct 子集；面向血流动力学预测优化的掩码图像重建效果差，表明任务最优采集与重建最优采集存在根本差异。

结论： 结果表明可围绕下游物理量直接设计稀疏采集策略；任务感知采样可能不同于重建导向采样。摘要未提供具体误差数值、视图预算范围及临床验证细节。

原始摘要： Sparse-view computed tomography (CT) reduces the number of acquired projections, but conventional sampling strategies are designed primarily for image reconstruction rather than for downstream physical inference. Here, we investigate optimal task-aware projection selection for directly predicting hemodynamics from sparse CT angiography (CTA) measurements. Using computational fluid dynamics simulations of intracranial aneurysms, we generate synthetic fan-beam CTA projections and train a Fourier neural operator (FNO) to predict two targets: the spatially resolved time-averaged wall shear stress (TAWSS) field and the spatially averaged TAWSS. The FNO was coupled with optimal input selection for simultaneous optimal sampling and training. Our goal was to investigate if agentic AI could discover optimal sampling strategies that outperform current methods. We compared fixed uniform and golden-angle sampling with three task-aware optimal selection strategies: Gumbel-Softmax, a Soft top-K method, and an agentic AI selector. The methods were compared at different view selection budgets. A detailed analysis of the AI agent was performed to understand its reasoning and performance. For spatially resolved TAWSS, Agentic and Soft top-K achieve the lowest mean errors (Agentic performs slightly better) and substantially outperform fixed sampling under extreme sparsity. For spatially averaged TAWSS, optimized selection becomes important at extremely sparse acquisitions. Analysis of the selected masks reveals multiple distinct subsets concentrated around informative regions. Moreover, masks optimized for hemodynamic prediction yield poor image reconstructions, showing that task-optimal acquisition differs fundamentally from reconstruction-optimal acquisition. This work suggests the possibility of designing sparse acquisitions directly around downstream physical quantities.

</details>


### [88] [Suppression of the Benjamin-Feir instability by constant vorticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39000)
*Bowen Chen, Huy Q. Nguyen*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文研究无限深水中恒定涡量对小幅斯托克斯波本杰明-菲尔不稳定性的影响，并在完整水波方程框架下严格证明：涡量穿过临界值 2/√3 时，波从调制不稳定转为稳定。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 旨在严格理解恒定涡量如何影响小幅斯托克斯波的本杰明-菲尔不稳定性，并检验 Thomas、Kharif 和 Manna 由形式非线性薛定谔近似所预测的稳定性转变。

方法： 基于完整水波方程进行严格分析；考虑右行波，恒定涡量为 γ，背景剪切流为 -γ y e_x，并将重力加速度和载波波数归一化为 1；分别研究 γ<2/√3 与 γ>2/√3 情形，并针对纵向长波扰动讨论谱稳定性。

结果： 对每个固定 γ<2/√3，证明足够小幅的斯托克斯波是本杰明-菲尔不稳定的；对每个固定 γ>2/√3，证明这些波关于纵向长波扰动是谱稳定的。

结论： 结果严格确认了在临界值 2/√3 之外由形式非线性薛定谔近似预测的稳定性转变：低涡量下调制不稳定，高涡量下谱稳定；临界值附近的严格结论未在摘要中给出。

原始摘要： We study the effect of constant vorticity on the Benjamin-Feir instability of small-amplitude Stokes waves in infinite depth. Working with the full water-wave equations, we rigorously establish a transition between modulational instability and stability as the vorticity crosses a critical value. We consider right-propagating waves with constant vorticity $γ$, associated with the background shear current $-γy\mathbf e_x$, and normalize the gravitational acceleration and carrier wavenumber to one. For each fixed $γ<2/\sqrt{3}$, we prove that sufficiently small-amplitude Stokes waves are Benjamin-Feir unstable. For each fixed $γ>2/\sqrt{3}$, these waves are spectrally stable with respect to longitudinal, long-wavelength perturbations. These results rigorously confirm, away from the critical value, the stability transition predicted by the formal nonlinear Schrödinger approximation of Thomas, Kharif, and Manna.

</details>


### [89] [Tayler Instability and Its Saturation in Rotating Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38107)
*Valentin Skoutnev, Andrei Beloborodov*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文通过全局模拟检验泰勒斯不稳定性线性理论并研究其非线性饱和，发现磁地转波与惯性波具有不同饱和行为；只有磁地转波支能支持泰勒斯-斯普劳特发电机，并可能对应Spruit (2002)提出的稳态情形。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 在旋转恒星的稳定分层区域，磁不稳定性对发电机过程和角动量输运至关重要；绕制环向场的泰勒斯不稳定性长期被认为驱动泰勒斯-斯普劳特发电机，但其非线性饱和仍缺乏理解，而这是发电机循环的核心环节。

方法： 采用全局模拟，验证近期完成的泰勒斯不稳定性线性理论，并确定该不稳定性的饱和过程。

结果： 确认磁地转波和惯性波均表现为过稳定性，它们在相近的磁地转时标上增长但振荡频率不同；磁地转波以δB/B约为1的大振幅饱和，并在磁地转时标上耗散环向场，而惯性波以δB/B远小于1的振幅饱和，并在长得多的时标上侵蚀环向场；磁地转波还能通过非线性耦合有效再生轴对称极向场，惯性波则不能。

结论： 结果表明泰勒斯-斯普劳特发电机只能通过磁地转波支运行，其稳态可能遵循Spruit (2002)提出的情形，而排除Fuller等(2019)提出的小振幅饱和情形；文中还讨论了对演化恒星和新生中子星中角动量输运与磁场放大的意义。

原始摘要： In stably stratified regions of rotating stars, magnetic instabilities play a key role by enabling dynamo and angular momentum transport. In particular, the Tayler instability of wound-up toroidal fields has long been considered to drive the Tayler-Spruit dynamo. However, the nonlinear saturation of the instability is poorly understood, despite its central role in the dynamo loop. In this paper, we use global simulations to verify the recently completed linear theory of the Tayler instability and determine its saturation. We confirm the picture of overstability of magnetostrophic and inertial waves, which grow on similar magnetostrophic timescales but oscillate with different frequencies. We find that magnetostrophic waves saturate with large amplitudes $δB/B\sim 1$ and dissipate the toroidal field on magnetostrophic timescales, while inertial waves saturate with $δB/B\ll 1$ and erode the toroidal field over much longer timescales. Moreover, magnetostrophic waves efficiently regenerate axisymmetric poloidal fields through nonlinear couplings while inertial waves do not. Our results indicate that the Tayler-Spruit dynamo can operate only through the magnetostrophic wave branch and that its steady-state may follow the scenario proposed by \cite{spruit2002dynamo}, ruling out the small-amplitude saturation scenario proposed by \cite{fuller2019slowing}. Implications for angular momentum transport and magnetic field amplification in evolving stars and nascent neutron stars are discussed.

</details>


### [90] [Predicting Sparse Observables in Parameterized Unsteady Flows Using Coarse-Mesh Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37986)
*Brener Ramos, William R. Wolf, Tarcísio C. D. Oliveira, Scott T. M. Dawson*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种直接在粗网格上部署图神经网络的降阶建模框架，无需全状态重构即可预测参数化非定常流动的稀疏探针与积分量，并结合自适应采样和噪声增强多步展开训练提升精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 许多应用只关注少量选定自由度，因此高维非线性流动的全阶代理模型往往不必要；研究旨在避免全状态重构，直接构建面向粗网格的降阶模型。

方法： 在粗网格上直接使用图神经网络（GNN）建模，应用于参数化二维/三维受限圆柱绕流及非均匀加热腔体，覆盖不同雷诺数和瑞利数范围；采用由频谱变化引导的动力学信息自适应采样策略，并利用粗网格训练使噪声增强的多步展开训练变得可行。

结果： 模型可泛化到未见工况，准确预测稀疏探针位置和积分量；粗网格GNN匹配或优于更细网格代理，并降低训练时间和内存，即使对三维混沌动力学亦然；自适应采样相较均匀采样以可忽略成本将最大误差降低一个数量级；噪声增强多步展开改善长时程自回归预测；方法以更低成本优于参数化DMD、稀疏MLP、U-Net和傅里叶神经算子，并支持稀疏观测下的系统辨识与状态估计。

结论： 结果表明，粗网格表示、自适应采样和噪声增强展开训练构成一个高效框架，可在不进行求解器分辨率全状态重构的情况下，实现参数化非定常流动的降阶建模。

原始摘要： Full-order surrogates for high-dimensional nonlinear flows are often unnecessary when applications require only selected degrees of freedom. We propose reduced-order models that bypass full-state reconstruction by deploying graph neural networks (GNNs) directly on coarse meshes. Applied to parameterized 2D/3D confined cylinder flows and an unevenly heated cavity over Reynolds/Rayleigh-number ranges, the models generalize to unseen conditions and accurately predict sparse probe locations and integrated quantities. Coarse-mesh GNNs match or outperform finer-mesh surrogates while reducing training time and memory, even for 3D chaotic dynamics. A dynamics-informed adaptive sampling strategy guided by frequency-spectrum variations reduces maximum errors by an order of magnitude versus uniform sampling at negligible cost. Coarse-mesh training also makes noise-augmented multi-step unrolling practical, improving long-horizon autoregressive predictions. The approach outperforms parametric DMD, sparse MLPs, U-Nets, and Fourier neural operators at lower cost, and enables system identification and state estimation from sparse observations. These results show that coarse-mesh representations, adaptive sampling, and noise-augmented unrolled training form an efficient framework for reduced-order modeling of parameterized unsteady flows without solver-resolution reconstruction.

</details>


### [91] [Effects of Deep Learning Architecture and Pretraining on Automated Vocal Fold Segmentation in Ex Vivo High-Speed Phonation Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37860)
*Varun Vinayak Kalaiarasan, Charles Farbos de Luzan, Khalid Malik, Ephraim Gutmark, Liran Oren*

分类： physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究在二维喉内PIV高频图像中系统比较四种深度学习架构及ImageNet预训练对声带自动分割的影响；六条流程的平均DSC均约0.973—0.976，差异主要体现在边界质量，预训练可显著改善TransUNet和Swin-UNet的边界指标。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 二维喉内PIV声带分割依赖人工标注，原因是残余眩光、组织与流场对比度低以及示踪粒子散斑会遮蔽声带边界；作者希望评估深度学习架构和ImageNet预训练如何影响这类高速图像中的自动声带分割，并据称首次将深度学习用于二维喉内PIV声带分割。

方法： 基准测试三类共四种架构：CNN类UNet和UNet++、CNN-Transformer混合的TransUNet、纯Transformer的Swin-UNet；后两者分别从零训练和使用ImageNet预训练权重，形成六条流程。使用来自227次离体犬喉发声试验的16,344张专家标注图像，在统一协议下以5个随机种子训练，并在50个留出测试试验上用试验级Wilcoxon符号秩检验和Holm校正比较DSC、BF1、HD95及HD95>50像素图像比例。

结果： 六条流程平均DSC仅在0.9734至0.9764之间，主要差异在边界保真度。ImageNet预训练显著提高TransUNet和Swin-UNet的BF1并降低HD95，对Swin-UNet的提升约大四倍；预训练后二者表现相近，而Swin-UNet可训练参数约少四分之三。未预训练时，TransUNet的BF1显著高于两种CNN流程、HD95显著更低，且HD95>50像素图像比例约为二者的一半。摘要未给出BF1、HD95的具体数值及精确参数量。

结论： 研究表明，在此类二维喉内PIV声带分割任务中，各流程整体DSC接近，预训练和架构选择主要影响边界质量；预训练可缩小Transformer类模型差距，Swin-UNet在参数量更少时仍具竞争力。该工作为噪声光学条件或高度可变形对象的实验流场可视化图像分割提供了参考框架。

原始摘要： Two-dimensional (2D) intraglottal particle image velocimetry (PIV) of ex vivo phonation experiments quantifies vocal fold medial surface dynamics and the intraglottal flow field. However, it relies on manual segmentation because residual glare, low tissue-to-flow contrast, and tracer-particle speckle obscure the vocal fold boundaries. This study assessed how deep learning architecture and ImageNet pretraining affect automated vocal fold segmentation in these high-speed images. Four established architectures from three categories were benchmarked: the convolutional neural network (CNN)-based UNet and UNet++, the hybrid CNN-transformer-based TransUNet, and the pure transformer-based Swin-UNet, the latter two trained from scratch and from ImageNet-pretrained weights. Using 16,344 expert-annotated images from 227 phonation trials of excised canine larynges, the six pipelines were trained with five seeds under a standardized protocol and compared on 50 held-out test trials using trial-level Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. Mean Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) spanned only 0.9734 to 0.9764, so the pipelines differed mainly in boundary fidelity. Pretraining significantly improved the boundary F1 score (BF1) and 95th percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95) of the TransUNet and Swin-UNet, about four times more for the Swin-UNet. Once pretrained, the two performed similarly, although the Swin-UNet has only about a quarter of the trainable parameters. Without pretraining, the TransUNet had a significantly higher BF1 and a lower HD95 than both CNN-based pipelines and about half their share of images with HD95 > 50 pixels. To our knowledge, this is the first study to apply deep learning to vocal fold segmentation in 2D intraglottal PIV, and it offers a reference framework for segmenting experimental flow-visualization images with noisy optical conditions or highly deformable objects.

</details>

### [92] [Ionization and Dissociation Energies of Molecular Hydrogen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40359)
*Ioana Doran, Charlaine Roth, Nicolas Hölsch, Maximilian Beyer, Andrés Martínez de Velasco, Vincent Barbé, Mathieu Collombon, Elmer Gründeman, Julian J. Krauth, Jinjun Liu, Josef A. Agner, Urs Hollenstein, Andreas Osterwalder, Hansjürg Schmutz, Daniel Sprecher, Andrea Wüest, Wim Ubachs, Christian Jungen, Kjeld S. E. Eikema, Frédéric Merkt*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过精密测量对氢分子（H₂）的15条跃迁频率，并结合过去三十年45个para-H₂与ortho-H₂能隙的全局网络分析，显著提高了H₂电离能和解离能的精度，并给出了超越标准模型贡献的上限。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： H₂的电离能与解离能是检验非绝热、相对论、量子电动力学和有限核尺寸等效应的第一性原理分子计算的关键基准，因此需要更高精度的实验值。

方法： 测量para-H₂中连接X ¹Σ_g⁺(v=0,N=0)基态、长寿命EF ¹Σ_g⁺(v=0,N=0)激发态以及主量子数n=48–70的np和nd Rydberg系列的15条跃迁频率；随后对para-H₂和ortho-H₂中过去三十年测得的45个能量间隔进行全局网络分析。

结果： 新数据单独使para-H₂基态电离能精度提高5倍，相对频率精度达5×10⁻¹¹；结合网络分析得到E_I(0,0)/(hc)=124 417.491 1573(58) cm⁻¹和D₀^{N=0}=36 118.069 6646(58) cm⁻¹，精度约175 kHz；还改进了相关激发态的项值和解离能，并由与第一性原理计算比较得到para-H₂与ortho-H₂能量差中假想超越标准模型贡献的上限为540 kHz。

结论： 该工作提供了高精度的H₂电离能和解离能基准值，可用于严格检验第一性原理计算并限制超越标准模型的贡献；摘要未提供具体实验装置和完整不确定度预算等细节。

原始摘要： Molecular hydrogen (H$_2$) is the simplest neutral molecule and its ionization and dissociation energies are key benchmarks for testing first-principles calculations of nonadiabatic, relativistic, quantum-electrodynamic and finite-nuclear-size effects in molecules. To obtain improved H$_2$ ionization and dissociation energies, we performed precision measurements of 15 transition frequencies in para-H$_2$. They connect the X $^1Σ_g^+(v=0,N=0)$ ground state, the long-lived EF $^1Σ_g^+(v=0,N=0)$ excited state, and several high members of the $n$p and $n$d Rydberg series with principal quantum number $n$ in the range between 48 and 70. The new data by itself leads to a five times more accurate value of the ionization energy of the ground state of para-H$_2$, equivalent to a relative frequency accuracy of $5\times 10^{-11}$. Combined with a global network analysis of 45 energy intervals in para- and ortho-H$_2$ that have been measured in the past three decades, we obtain new statistically robust values for the ionization energy [$E_{\rm I}(0,0)/(hc)= 124\,417.491\,1573(58)$~cm$^{-1}$] and the dissociation energy [$D_0^{N=0}=36\,118.069\,6646(58)$~cm$^{-1}$] of H$_2$. These values have an unprecedented accuracy equivalent to 175 kHz. The network analysis also leads to improved term values and dissociation energies of the excited states involved in the analysis. By comparing the results with first-principles calculations of the level structures of H$_2$ and H$_2^+$ , a significantly reduced upper bound of 540 kHz is derived for putative beyond-the-standard-model contributions to the energy difference between para- and ortho-H$_2$.

</details>


### [93] [High-Fidelity Universal Quantum Gates in a Cryogenic Atom Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40329)
*Junlan Jin, Donghyuk Seo, Yue Shi, Yukai Lu, Jeff D. Thompson, Waseem S. Bakr*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文在低温 $^{133}$Cs 光镊阵列中实现了通用量子门集：单比特门保真度为 99.970(2)%，原始两比特纠缠门保真度为 99.76(8)%；纠缠门采用基态与 Rydberg $P$ 态的直接单光子耦合，测得保真度与以原子运动为主的误差模型一致，Rydberg 衰变的基本误差贡献低于 $3\times10^{-4}$。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 中性原子光镊阵列已成为量子信息处理的重要平台，但 Rydberg 纠缠门保真度日益受黑体辐射诱导跃迁限制；这些跃迁会缩短 Rydberg 寿命并产生可导致相关误差的污染态。低温环境可将此类跃迁率抑制一个数量级以上，但在低温环境中实现通用量子门集仍是未解决的挑战。

方法： 在低温 $^{133}$Cs 原子阵列中演示通用量子门集；纠缠门利用电子基态与 Rydberg $P$ 态之间的直接单光子耦合，以避免双光子耦合相关的散射和光频移，并在 $2\pi\times4.3$ MHz 的较低 Rabi 频率下运行；随后测量单比特和两比特门保真度，并与误差模型比较。

结果： 获得单比特门保真度 99.970(2)% 和原始两比特纠缠门保真度 99.76(8)%，处于领先室温实现的范围；测得的纠缠门保真度与误差模型一致，该模型以原子运动为主要误差来源；Rydberg 衰变带来的基本误差贡献低于 $3\times10^{-4}$。

结论： 结果表明，低温抑制 Rydberg 衰变可为显著超越当前水平的纠缠门保真度提供路径，并降低容错量子计算的开销；改进冷却有望进一步提高保真度。

原始摘要： Neutral atoms in optical tweezer arrays have emerged as a leading platform for quantum information processing. The fidelity of entangling gates mediated by Rydberg interactions has improved rapidly and is increasingly constrained by blackbody-radiation-induced transitions, which shorten Rydberg lifetimes and create contaminant states that can cause correlated errors. Cryogenic environments can suppress these transition rates by more than an order of magnitude. However, realizing a universal gate set in such an environment has remained an outstanding challenge. Here, we demonstrate a universal quantum gate set in a cryogenic $^{133}$Cs atom array, with a single-qubit gate fidelity of 99.970(2)% and a raw two-qubit entangling-gate fidelity of 99.76(8)%, within the range of leading room-temperature implementations. The entangling gate uses direct single-photon coupling between the electronic ground state and a Rydberg $P$ state, avoiding scattering and light shifts associated with two-photon coupling. The gate operates at a modest Rabi frequency of $2π\times4.3$ MHz. The measured fidelity agrees with our error model dominated by atomic motion, indicating a straightforward route to higher fidelity through improved cooling, while the fundamental error contribution from Rydberg decay is below $3\times10^{-4}$. These results establish a path to entangling-gate fidelities substantially beyond the current state of the art through cryogenic suppression of Rydberg decay, reducing the overhead for fault-tolerant computation.

</details>

*Anthony El Bekai, Romain Granier, Miguel Angel Cifuentes Marin, Cédric Blanchard, Nassim Zahzam, Yannick Bidel, Alexandre Bresson, Alexis Bonnin, Sylvain Schwartz*

结论： 该方法可同时测量微波电场幅度与椭圆偏振度，借助小型磁光阱的局域测量抑制场不均匀性，并能分辨较小的偏振偏离；在Autler-Townes区具备约1%精度和SI溯源性，同时可提供微波到达角相关信息。摘要未提供该方法在其他场强、频率范围或复杂场环境下的适用性结论。

原始摘要： We present a method for the simultaneous measurement of the amplitude and the ellipticity of microwave fields using trap-loss spectroscopy based on Rydberg excitation of a cold ensemble of 87Rb atoms. The measurement consists of probing the energy spectrum of the $61S_{1/2}$ to $61P_{1/2}$ transition in the presence of microwave, which depends on the electric field amplitude $|E|$ and the third Stokes parameter $|S_3|$. We use a millimeter-size magneto-optical trap to perform strongly-localized measurements, which mitigates the effect of microwave field inhomogeneities. This allows us to work with large Rabi frequencies and thus resolve small deviations from linear or circular states of polarization. Taking into account the effect of non-resonant couplings to neighboring Rydberg states, we measure $|E|$ and $|S_3|$ with an accuracy on the order of 1 percent in the Autler-Townes regime where the measurements can be traced to the international system of units. By studying the visibility of the spectral features as a function of the polarization of the excitation laser beams, we extract some information on the angle of arrival of the microwave field onto the atoms, with good agreement between theory and experiment.

</details>


### [95] [Theoretical Study of Classical Dynamics of Electron in Two-Frequency Paul Trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38936)
*Payman Mahmoudi, Michal Tarana, Michal Hejduk*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究电子在双频 Paul 阱中的囚禁，面向由二价钙离子（Ca2+）与电子构成的未来混合架构；通过将电子与离子—电子相互作用隔离，分析其在时变阱势中的稳定性与动力学。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 研究动机来自未来由二价钙离子（Ca2+）和电子组成的混合架构，需要理解电子在双频 Paul 阱中的囚禁行为，并为电子—离子阱中电子—离子相互作用的物理建模奠定基础。

方法： 将电子从离子—电子相互作用中隔离后，建立电子在时变阱势中的运动方程，并写成 Hill 形式；用无量纲 q 参数分析稳定性与动力学，这些参数编码外加电压、驱动频率、阱几何和粒子荷质比；并给出近似方法以快速评估器件设计 q 参数与电子运动的关系。

结果： 在低、高两个射频场共同作用下，囚禁电子的经典运动可很好地近似为慢速长期振荡，并受到来自低频和高频射频驱动的两个不同运动边带的调制；所提近似方法可快速评估器件设计 q 参数与电子运动的关系。摘要未提供定量实验结果或实验验证。

结论： 该理论分析为电子—离子阱中电子—离子相互作用的物理模型发展提供了重要基础，并给出了双频 Paul 阱中电子运动与器件设计参数关系的快速评估方法。

原始摘要： We present a theoretical study of trapping an electron in a two-frequency Paul trap, motivated by future hybrid architecture consisting of a doubly charged calcium ion (Ca$^{2+}$) and an electron. By isolating the electron from ionic-electron interactions, we formulate the equations of motion for the electron in the time-dependent trap potentials and cast them in the Hill form. Stability and dynamics are then analysed in terms of the dimensionless q-parameters, which encode the applied voltages, drive frequencies, trap geometry, and the particle's charge-to-mass ratio. Under the combined action of the two radio-frequency fields, the trapped electron's classical motion is shown to be approximated well by a slow secular oscillation modulated by two distinct motion sidebands originating from the low- and high-frequency RF drives. The approximation method presented here enables a quick evaluation of the relation between the device-design q-parameters and the electron motion. It offers a crucial foundation for developing physical models of electron-ion interactions in electron-ion traps.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 摘要未明确陈述研究动机；其研究关注惰性液体中总量子产额和电荷复合的第一性原理描述，并试图用与物理可观测量相关的电子标度长度ξ_e统一刻画能量与电场依赖。

方法： 求解包含电子能歧化和原子间势细致建模的Lindhard积分微分方程；引入电子标度长度ξ_e；基于Bates-Griffing过程和半经典原子相互作用研究核反冲的激子-离子比随能量和电场的变化；用Thomas-Imel箱模型中的复合概率表达光产额和电荷产额，参数为计算得到的ξ_e和一个拟合常数；并与LAr和LXe中箱尺寸电场依赖的近期测量比较。

结果： 模型比较了LAr和LXe中箱尺寸随电场变化的测量并给出物理解释；可延伸至亚keV能量，在数keV至1MeV范围对现有数据给出一致描述，对现有LAr数据的整体吻合优于NEST，并提供LXe预测。摘要未给出具体定量数值。

结论： 该模型以ξ_e为核心参数，结合Lindhard方程、Bates-Griffing过程和Thomas-Imel箱模型，为惰性液体中光与电荷产额及复合行为提供跨能区和电场的一致描述，并具有对LXe的预测能力；摘要未提供更详细的误差、适用范围限制或实验验证结论。

原始摘要： We present a first-principles study of total quanta and charge recombination in noble liquids, based on a solution of Lindhard's integro-differential equation for atomic motion with electronic straggling and detailed modeling of inter-atomic potentials. We introduce the electronic scaling length $ξ_e$ as a fundamental parameter related to a physical observable of the system. We study the energy and electric-field dependence of the exciton-to-ion ratio for nuclear recoils based on the Bates-Griffing process and semiclassical atomic interactions. The resulting light and charge yields are expressed through the recombination probability in the Thomas-Imel box model using the calculated parameter $ξ_e$ and one fitted constant. We compare our model with recent measurements of the electric-field dependence of the box size in LAr and LXe and provide a physical explanation for this behavior. Our model extends to sub-keV energies and provides a consistent description of available data from a few keV to 1 MeV, yielding better overall agreement with existing LAr data than NEST and offering predictions for LXe.

</details>


### [97] [Cold Hypersonic Gas Jets for Precision Laser Spectroscopy of Radioactive Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38242)
*A. Claessens, R. Ferrer, Harshithbabu, L. M. Arndt, Ch. E. Düllmann, K. Gaul, F. Ivandikov, G. Neyens, D. Renisch, J. Stricker, W. Saelens, P. Van den Bergh, P. Van Duppen*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将超音速（de Laval）喷嘴与气体射流激光电离光谱应用于短寿命放射性分子，以229,230,232ThO为对象，获得约12.5 K平动温度、7.2 K转动温度和较大分子场移，并用相对论FSCCSD计算复现场移因子。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 核 Schiff 矩（NSM）对超出标准模型物理具有敏感性，推动了对短寿命放射性分子的激光光谱研究；但分子自由度冷却效率不足导致光谱复杂且难以解释，因此需要改善冷分子产生与光谱测量条件。

方法： 采用超音速（de Laval）喷嘴产生气体射流，结合气体射流内激光电离光谱，对激光烧蚀产生的229,230,232ThO分子进行研究；通过激光光谱表征平动和转动温度，利用分子King-plot分析场移，并使用相对论FSCCSD计算分子场移因子。

结果： 在喷嘴准直的60 mm长、5 mm直径气体射流中测得平动温度为12.5(25) K、转动温度为7.2(6) K；因未观察到来自v''=1的跃迁而估计了振动温度的上限（摘要未给出具体数值）。分子King-plot分析显示场移为-4.0(13) cm^-1/fm^2，表明对差分核电荷半径具有增强的敏感性；相对论FSCCSD计算复现了测得的分子场移因子。

结论： 该工作证明超音速喷嘴气体射流激光电离光谱可用于研究冷的短寿命锕系分子，为NSM相关研究提供更易解释的光谱条件，并在先进分子与原子计算之间提供了基准。

原始摘要： The sensitivity to possible beyond standard model physics via a study of the nuclear Schiff moment (NSM) has since recently been driving laser spectroscopy of short-lived radioactive molecules. These experiments have in part been hindered by inefficient cooling of the molecular degrees of freedom, resulting in complex, difficult to interpret molecular spectra. Hypersonic (de Laval) nozzles have been shown to provide ideal environments to study cold short-lived actinides by means of in-gas-jet laser ionization spectroscopy. We present here the first application of this technique on molecules using $^{229,230,232}$ThO laser ablation targets. Translational and rotational temperatures were characterized via laser spectroscopy and found to be $12.5(25)\,\mathrm{K}$ and $7.2(6)\,\mathrm{K}$, respectively, along the nozzle-collimated $60\,\mathrm{mm}$-long, $5\,\mathrm{mm}$-diameter gas jet. An upper bound of the vibrational temperature was estimated as no transitions from $v''=1$ were observed. Molecular King-plot analysis shows a large field shift of $-4.0(13)\,\mathrm{cm}^{-1}/\mathrm{fm}^2$, indicating enhanced sensitivity to the differential nuclear charge radii. Relativistic FSCCSD calculations reproduce the measured molecular field-shift factor, providing a benchmark between state-of-the-art molecular and atomic calculations.

</details>


### [98] [Exceptional Points as Witnesses of Non-Gaussian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40612)
*Aritra Ghosh, Subhashish Banerjee, M. Bhattacharya*

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 量子器件的高效控制需要了解环境噪声，但常用功率谱无法完整表征真实环境中的非高斯涨落，因此需要能够区分具有相同低阶统计的环境并刻画高阶噪声统计的方法。

方法： 研究表明噪声平均退相干响应中的例外点可暴露非高斯统计的可观测特征；引入在保持功率谱条件下以相同独立噪声源描述退相干环境的理论模型；在单源模型已标定时，从测量退相干响应中提取最大EP阶数，以确定噪声源数量并预测组合噪声的高阶统计；并用原子电子学读出将不同EP特征映射到演化中的系综平均原子干涉图案。

结果： EP特征能够区分具有相同低阶统计的不同环境；最大EP阶数可确定噪声源数目并预测组合噪声的高阶统计；原子干涉图案示例说明不同EP特征可被映射为可观测的系综平均干涉图样。

结论： 研究将非厄米动力学与环境噪声统计联系起来，为计算、计量和精密测量中的量子器件噪声表征提供了一条途径。

原始摘要： Efficient control of quantum devices requires the knowledge of their environmental noise, yet the commonly measured power spectrum does not fully characterize non-Gaussian fluctuations of realistic environments. Here we show that exceptional points (EPs) in the noise-averaged dephasing response can expose observable signatures of non-Gaussian statistics, distinguishing environments with identical lower-order statistics. We also introduce a theoretical model that describes dephasing environments in terms of identical independent noise sources while preserving the power spectrum. Given a calibrated single-source model, the largest EP order extracted from the measured dephasing response determines the number of sources and predicts the higher-order statistics of the combined noise. We illustrate an atomtronic readout that maps distinct EP signatures onto an evolving ensemble-averaged atomic interference pattern. Our results connect non-Hermitian dynamics to environmental noise statistics, providing a route to characterize noise in quantum devices used in computing, metrology, and precision measurement.

</details>


### [99] [Static dipolar impurity in a Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40460)
*Eugen Dizer, Xin Chen, Richard Schmidt, Arthur Christianen*

<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 作者希望理解偶极相互作用如何同时塑造少体散射性质以及由静态偶极杂质形成的极化子物理，并寻找可在超冷极性分子中检验的效应。

方法： 采用Fumi定理把各向异性相移直接关联到基态能量；使用泛函行列式方法计算精确的杂质谱；并将结果与仅包含一个粒子-空穴激发的变分拟设进行比较。

结果： 杂质谱表现为独立偶波和奇波贡献的卷积，基态同时占据偶波与奇波束缚态，其间距可通过偶极长度调节；仅含一个粒子-空穴激发的变分拟设无法描述这种双重占据基态。长程偶极相互作用会削弱Anderson正交灾难，使准粒子权重的幂律衰减减弱。摘要未提供具体定量参数或材料细节。

结论： 偶极相互作用可导致偶波与奇波束缚态同时占据的基态，并减弱Anderson正交灾难；这些效应有望在超冷极性分子中通过微波屏蔽和相互作用的独立可调性进行实验检验。

原始摘要： We consider a static dipolar impurity immersed in a Fermi gas and show how dipolar interactions shape both the few-body scattering properties and the resulting polaron physics. Using Fumi's theorem, we connect the anisotropic phase shifts directly to the ground-state energy, and compute the exact impurity spectrum via the functional determinant approach. The resulting spectrum is a convolution of independent even- and odd-wave contributions, giving rise to a ground state with simultaneously occupied even- and odd-wave bound states whose separation is tunable via the dipole length. A variational ansatz limited to one particle-hole excitation fails to capture this doubly occupied ground state. We further find that long-range dipolar interactions weaken Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe, reducing the power-law decay of the quasiparticle weight. These effects can be tested with ultracold polar molecules, where microwave shielding and independent tunability of interactions enable experimental access to the predicted regimes.

</details>


### [100] [Single-atom billiards in an ultracold bath with energy-tunable collisions and threshold-sensitive detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40098)
*Silvia Hiebel, Sabrina Burgardt, Julian Feß, Artur Widera*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 利用速度和深度可调的晶格，将单个铯原子以受控速度射入超冷铷浴，区分碰撞次数与碰撞破坏输运的概率；在少量碰撞区观察到单次碰撞逐出概率随碰撞能超过晶格束缚尺度而由小升至接近1。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 微观尺度输运在无序热介质中可能显著不同于宏观情形；超冷量子气体具有高可控性和可直接观测的动力学，适合在精确可控条件下研究此类输运，并建立单次散射事件与涌现耗散输运之间的联系。

方法： 实现能量可调的单原子对撞器：让单个铯原子以受控速度穿过超冷铷浴。恒定晶格速度决定碰撞能分布中的定向贡献，晶格深度提供碰撞诱导输运破坏的可调能量阈值；独立改变晶格速度、晶格深度、浴密度和温度，以分离碰撞事件数目与其破坏输运的概率，并在每次通过仅预期少数碰撞的区域进行推断。

结果： 推断的单次碰撞逐出概率随碰撞能超过晶格束缚尺度而从较小值上升至接近1；实验将微观碰撞能量转移转化为二值单原子观测量。摘要未提供具体数值参数。

结论： 该工作提供了从单个散射事件到涌现耗散输运的可控联系，并展示超冷量子气体可作为研究无序热介质中微观输运的平台。

原始摘要： Transport processes at the microscopic scale can differ strongly from their macroscopic counterparts, particularly when the environment is not a well-defined surface but an unordered thermal medium. Ultracold quantum gases offer a high degree of controllability and directly observable dynamics, thereby providing a versatile platform to study such transport processes under precisely controllable conditions. Here, we realize an energy-tunable single-atom collider by transporting individual cesium atoms at controlled velocity through an ultracold rubidium bath. The constant lattice velocity sets the directed contribution to the collision-energy distribution, while the lattice depth provides a tunable energy threshold for collision-induced transport disruption. By independently varying these parameters together with bath density and temperature, we separate the number of collision events from their probability to disrupt transport. Across a regime with only a few expected collisions per passage, the inferred single-collision ejection probability rises from small values to near unity as the collision energy exceeds the lattice confinement scale. The experiment thus converts microscopic collisional energy transfer into a binary single-atom observable and provides a controlled link between individual scattering events and emergent dissipative transport.

</details>


### [101] [Resolving Photochemical Reaction Pathways Using Field-Induced Surface Hopping Simulation of fs-EUV-TRPES: The Case of Furan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40011)
*Xincheng Miao, Toshinori Suzuki, Roland Mitrić*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种将场诱导表面跳跃与显式脉冲激发、RMS-CASPT2 及时间分辨光电子谱模拟相结合的方法，用于解析光化学反应路径，并以 200 nm、10 fs 脉冲激发的呋喃为对象进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 将 fs-EUV 光电子谱的光谱特征归属到具体光化学反应路径，需要能够把非绝热动力学与实验可观测谱联系起来的理论预测。

方法： 采用场诱导表面跳跃并结合显式脉冲激发制备实验条件下的激发态系综，用 RMS-CASPT2 多参考方法描述激发态、锥形交叉和断键坐标，将轨迹映射为光电子信号，并系统分析光电离近似以模拟时间分辨光电子谱；随后应用于 200 nm、10 fs 脉冲激发的呋喃。

结果： 返回基态的轨迹中约 87% 在环褶皱锥形交叉附近跳跃，13% 在开环锥形交叉附近跳跃；随后基态振动能量再分配仍使体系布居开环结构，并识别出五种基态产物。模拟谱再现了随时间变化的电子结合能位移、特征拐折及含预测光产物贡献的谱带；与基于 Dyson 范数的强度相比，平面波和库仑波连续态函数改善了最低结合能信号早期形状，并表明光电子各向异性比角度积分谱更敏感于连续态近似。

结论： 研究证明，显式脉冲非绝热动力学与动态相关的多参考电子结构相结合，可通过模拟 fs-EUV-TRPES 解析竞争性光化学反应路径，并强调连续态近似对光电子谱尤其是各向异性的影响。

原始摘要： Recent advances in femtosecond extreme ultraviolet photoelectron spectroscopy have enabled insight into photochemistry in gases, condensed phases, and at interfaces. Assigning spectral features to reaction pathways requires theoretical predictions connecting nonadiabatic dynamics to measured observables. Here, we present a general approach for resolving photochemical reaction pathways by combining field-induced surface hopping with explicit pulse excitation, RMS-CASPT2, and simulations of time-resolved photoelectron spectra. The pump field prepares the excited-state ensemble under experimental conditions, while the multireference treatment describes excited states, conical intersections, and bond-breaking coordinates. Mapping trajectories onto photoelectron signals, supported by a systematic analysis of photoionization approximations, enables comparison with experiment. We apply this approach to furan excited by a 200 nm, 10 fs pulse. Among trajectories returning to the ground state, approximately 87 % hop near the ring-puckering conical intersection and 13 % near the ring-opening intersection. Subsequent ground-state vibrational energy redistribution nevertheless populates ring-opening structures. Five ground-state products are identified. Simulated spectra reproduce time-dependent electron-binding-energy shifts, characteristic kinks, and bands with predicted photoproduct contributions. Compared with Dyson-norm-based intensities, plane- and Coulomb-wave continuum functions improve the early-time shape of the lowest binding energy signal and show that photoelectron anisotropy is more sensitive to the continuum approximation than the angle-integrated spectrum. More generally, these findings demonstrate how explicit-pulse nonadiabatic dynamics and dynamically correlated multireference electronic structure can resolve competing photochemical reaction pathways through simulated fs-EUV-TRPES.

</details>


### [102] [Kelvin-Wave Multiplication by Sound Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39909)
*Weican Yang*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 亚声速 Kelvin 量子可发射声子并分裂为两个更软的量子，导致 Kelvin 粒子数增加而能量降低；在反射腔中，该过程表现为 Kelvin 增益、均方位移增大和均方斜率减小，且初始声相位平移 π 会反转这些变化。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 摘要未明确给出研究动机；其关注点是由 Kelvin 量子发射声子并分裂为两个软量子所导致的 Kelvin 粒子数增长与能量损失，以及该过程在反射腔声场中的可观测效应。

方法： 在软声子极限下利用旋转不变性，将奇宇称于声子轴向动量反转的领先声学剪切振幅与释放能量联系起来；采用微观计算支持色散依赖；并在含相干声与 Kelvin 种子的反射腔中进行 Gross--Pitaevskii 演化。

结果： 微观计算支持所得到的色散依赖；Gross--Pitaevskii 演化显示 Kelvin 增益、更大的均方位移和更小的均方斜率；初始声相位平移 π 会反转这些变化。

结论： Kelvin 量子的声子发射与分裂可增加 Kelvin 粒子数并改变腔内声场响应，且初始声相位可调控这些效应；摘要未提供更多定量结论或实验验证信息。

原始摘要： A subsonic Kelvin quantum can emit a phonon and split into two softer quanta, increasing Kelvin population while losing energy. In the soft-phonon limit, rotational invariance relates the leading acoustic shear amplitude, odd under reversal of phonon axial momentum, to the energy released. Microscopic calculations support this dispersion dependence. In a reflecting cavity with coherent sound and Kelvin seeds, Gross--Pitaevskii evolution shows Kelvin gain, larger mean-square displacement and smaller mean-square slope; a $π$ shift of the initial sound phase reverses these changes.

</details>


### [103] [Optical clock network tracks tidal variations of the gravity potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39635)
*Kilian Stahl, Ingo Nosske, Chetan Vishwakarma, Tim Lücke, Joshua Klose, Erik Benkler, Alexander Kuhl, Shambo Mukherjee, Thomas Legero, Jochen Kronjäger, Uwe Sterr, Christian Lisdat, Sören Dörscher, Chen-Hao Feng, Matthew Y. H. Johnson, Jacques-Olivier Gaudron, Wei Huang, Jacob Tunesi, Xi Zhang, Marco Schioppo, Adam L. Parke, Ian R. Hill, Heiner Denker, Biplab Dutta, Etienne Cantin, Olivier Lopez, Anne Amy-Klein, Christian Chardonnet, Benjamin Pointard, Rodolphe Le Targat, Jérôme Lodewyck, Paul-Eric Pottie, Ichiro Ushijima, Masao Takamoto, Hidetoshi Katori, Yogeshwar B. Kale, Stefano Condio, Irene Goti, Tommaso Petrucciani, Cecilia Clivati, Simone Donadello, Filippo Levi, Davide Calonico, Marco Pizzocaro*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用欧洲的光学钟网络和相位稳定光纤链路，观测由潮汐引起的动态重力势差所导致的时间膨胀变化，并在约800 km基线上于1天测量内分辨出约10^-17量级的预测信号，结果与模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 已有实验验证的计时测高主要针对静态重力势差；月球、太阳等天体及地球潮汐响应会使钟速变化约10^-17，这为检验光学钟网络能否分辨微小相对论时间膨胀变化提供了可预测信号，同时频率计量需考虑时间相关的时间膨胀。

方法： 在歐洲部署光学钟网络并以相位稳定光纤链路连接；采用两种不同的地理构型，基线各约800 km，从而获得不同的全日和半日潮汐分量组合；其中一次比较使用可搬运光学钟作为远程传感器，测量钟速变化并与潮汐模型预测比较。

结果： 测得的钟速变化跟踪潮汐变化，在1天测量时间内分辨出预测信号，并与模型预测一致；实验将计时测高从静态势差扩展到动态重力势差观测。

结论： 研究证明光学钟网络可观测动态重力势差及相对论时间膨胀的时间依赖性，提示频率计量需计入该效应，并为提升稳定性与可用性的量子钟网络监测地表形变、质量重分布等其他重力势变化提供前景。

原始摘要： Gravity affects the passage of time, as described by General Relativity. On Earth, the rate of a clock as seen by a fixed observer changes by about one part in $10^{16}$ per metre in height. Chronometric levelling exploits this effect to directly measure differences in gravity potential by comparing distant clocks. It has been proposed as a complementary approach to established geodetic techniques for linking height reference systems and monitoring the terrestrial gravity potential. Experimental demonstrations have to date focused primarily on static potential differences. Yet, the potentials of the Moon, the Sun and other celestial bodies as well as the Earth's response to their tidal forcing change clock rates by several $10^{-17}$, providing a predictable test signal to demonstrate optical clock networks resolving small variations in relativistic time dilation. Here, we report chronometric observations of time-varying gravity potential differences using a network of optical clocks and phase-stabilized fibre links in Europe. Two distinct geographical configurations, with baselines of about 800 km each, result in different combinations of diurnal and semidiurnal tidal components. One comparison uses a transportable optical clock as a remote sensor. The measured rate shifts track the tidal variations, resolving the predicted signal within 1 d of measurement and agreeing with model predictions. These results extend experimental chronometric levelling from static to dynamic potential differences and highlight the need to account for the time dependence of relativistic time dilation in frequency metrology. They also open the prospect for quantum clock networks with improved stability and availability to move beyond known tides and monitor other gravity potential variations, such as those arising from surface deformation and mass redistribution in geologically or volcanically active regions.

</details>


### [104] [Reverse Quantum Annealing for Ground-State Energy Calculation in Molecule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39129)
*Pradyot Pritam Sahoo*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文报道了用逆向量子退火计算碱土金属单氟化物分子基态能量的结果：将Dirac-Coulomb电子哈密顿量划分为多个sub-QUBO，并用优化的逆向退火调度改进正向退火解，以系统性降低残余构型误差；采用Quantum Annealing Eigensolver获得电子基态波函数与能量，目标是指向亚毫哈特里化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 推动量子退火范式下的分子电子结构计算，并探索达到亚毫哈特里化学精度的途径；摘要未提供更具体的应用背景或材料体系动机。

方法： 将Dirac-Coulomb电子哈密顿量分解为sub-QUBO；利用优化的逆向退火调度对正向退火解进行精化，从而系统性最小化残余构型误差；使用Quantum Annealing Eigensolver算法获得电子基态波函数和能量。

结果： 完成了碱土金属单氟化物分子的逆向量子退火基态能量计算，获得了电子基态波函数与能量，并系统性降低了残余构型误差；摘要未给出具体分子、能量数值或已达到的精度指标。

结论： 该工作为在量子退火范式中实现亚毫哈特里化学精度、推进分子电子结构计算提供了一条路径；摘要未报告已实现的亚毫哈特里精度或具体验证细节。

原始摘要： We present the results of our reverse quantum annealing computations of the ground-state energies of alkaline-earth monofluoride molecules. By partitioning the Dirac--Coulomb electronic Hamiltonian into sub-QUBOs and refining forward-annealing solutions through optimized reverse-annealing schedules, residual configuration errors are systematically minimized. The electronic ground-state wave functions and energies are obtained using the Quantum Annealing Eigensolver algorithm. This work provides a pathway for achieving sub-milliHartree chemical accuracy and advancing molecular electronic structure calculations in the quantum annealing paradigm.

</details>


### [105] [Gravitational Interactions in Nuclear Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38832)
*Konstantin Beyer, Niklas E. Önne, Shimon Kolkowitz, Dolev Bluvstein, Mikhail Lukin, Jun Ye, Igor Pikovski*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究大型核钟核系综能否用于观测核间相互引力作用，分析邻近原子引起的引力红移及核能叠加态作为引力源产生的量子引力相互作用，并讨论在固态钍钟等体系中探测相关频移的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 原子钟精度随原子数增加而提升，核钟将时钟态编码于核跃迁而非电子跃迁，为精密测量提供新前沿；作者希望探索这类核系综能否用于观测彼此间的引力相互作用，并走向短距离引力探测及检验超越牛顿极限的引力量子源质量。

方法： 通过理论研究邻近原子造成的引力红移，纳入核能叠加态作为引力源，分析成对核之间的量子引力相互作用；提出利用空间对称性和高精度光谱学抑制电磁场效应，并讨论用外部引力场传感器直接探测能量诱导的质量叠加态。

结果： 研究发现，对于宏观数量的核，例如固态钍钟，相互引力导致的时钟频率移动可能变得不可忽略；而观测涉及时钟的引力诱导纠缠仍因自发辐射退相干而需要显著更大的实验进展。摘要未给出具体频移数值或实验实现结果。

结论： 核钟单独使用或与光机械系统等互补传感器结合，可能成为未来探测短距离引力、检验超越牛顿极限的引力量子源质量的途径。

原始摘要： Atomic clocks achieve extraordinary precision through quantum control over large ensembles of atoms, with precision increasing and atom number advancing steadily. Emerging nuclear clock technology encodes clock states in nuclear rather than electronic transitions, offering a new frontier for precision measurement. Here we study whether a large ensemble of such clock nuclei could be used to observe their mutual gravitational interaction. We investigate the gravitational redshift induced by neighboring atoms, including nuclear energy superpositions as sources of gravity, giving rise to a genuinely quantum gravitational interaction between pairs of nuclei. We find that the resulting clock frequency shift due to mutual gravity may become non-negligible for a macroscopic number of nuclei, for instance in a solid-state thorium clock, and we propose approaches to mitigate electromagnetic field effects by exploiting spatial symmetry and high-precision spectroscopy. We further discuss how energy-induced mass superpositions could be directly probed using external gravitational field sensors. By contrast, observing gravity-induced entanglement involving clocks would require substantially greater experimental advances due to decoherence from spontaneous emission. Taken together, our results suggest that nuclear clocks, either alone or in combination with complementary sensors such as optomechanical systems, may offer a future route toward probing gravity at short distances and testing gravitating quantum source masses beyond the Newtonian limit.

</details>


### [106] [Tan's Contact for Trapped Spin-Orbit-Coupled Fermi Gases at Finite Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38512)
*Tianhao Wu, Yuanbang Wei, Rui Tian, Dongdong Pu, Beizhi Chen, Qihang Ying, Hongyan Shi, Yue Zhang, Shuai Li, Bo Liu*

分类： physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用路径积分蒙特卡洛研究有限温度下自旋轨道耦合费米气体的Tan接触，发现热效应会显著削弱零温下Tan接触随自旋轨道耦合强度的线性标度，并使其变为非单调依赖，提出由异自旋费米子对平均占据数与能量重加权占据数竞争驱动的机制。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： Tan接触连接多体量子系统的微观特征与两体接触相互作用，对量子气体描述至关重要；但此前自旋轨道耦合费米气体的Tan接触研究仅限于零温，而多数实验条件下不可忽略的热效应尚未被探索。

方法： 采用路径积分蒙特卡洛（PIMC）方法研究自旋轨道耦合费米气体中Tan接触的热效应，并分析相互作用范围内异自旋费米子对的平均占据数与能量重加权占据数之间的竞争。

结果： 热效应从根本上改变自旋轨道耦合与Tan接触之间的标度关系：零温下Tan接触随自旋轨道耦合强度呈线性标度，该线性关系在热效应下被显著削弱，并因上述占据数竞争机制转变为非单调依赖。摘要未给出具体数值参数或定量结果。

结论： 热效应不可忽略且会深刻改变自旋轨道耦合费米气体的Tan接触标度行为；所揭示的机制将线性标度转为非单调依赖，研究成果有望用于未来实验探索强相互作用自旋轨道耦合费米气体中Tan接触的热效应。

原始摘要： Tan's contact, bridging microscopic features of many-body quantum systems to two-body contact interactions, is pivotal in the description of quantum gases. However, past studies of Tan's contact of the spin-orbit coupled Fermi gases have been exclusively discussed at zero temperature. The thermal effects on that, which cannot be neglected in most experimental conditions, has not been explored so far. Here, through a path integral Monte Carlo (PIMC) study, we show that thermal effects fundamentally alter the scaling relation between spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and Tan's contact. The linear scaling of Tan's contact with respect to the strength of SOC found at zero temperature is drastically undermined by the thermal effects. And a new mechanism based on the competition between the mean occupancy and the energy-reweighted occupancy of the opposite spin fermion pairs within the interaction range has been unveiled, which radically changes the above linear scaling to a non-monotonic dependence. Our work should be useful for future experiments to explore the preternatural thermal effects on Tan's contact of the strongly interacting spin-orbit-coupled Fermi gas.

</details>
