### [107] [Predicting Weight Windows for Duct-Streaming Shielding Geometries with a Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40457)
*Jilang Miao, Ansel Kneier, Jonathan Porrazzo*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用U-Net卷积神经网络从二维管道几何和源位置快速预测OpenMC中MAGIC前向通量权重窗的下界，以避免每个新几何都进行迭代蒙特卡罗计算；在250个混凝土管道样本上训练后，模型可在毫秒级给出预测，并在未重新训练的矩形Protvino迷宫上取得与MAGIC约0.96的相关性及显著方差缩减效果。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 前向通量权重窗可为管道流屏蔽问题提供全局方差缩减，但每换一个几何都需要重新进行迭代蒙特卡罗计算来构造权重窗，计算成本高；作者希望学习几何与源位置到下界窗的映射，从而快速生成权重窗。

方法： 在Protvino迷宫包围盒上构造250个随机混凝土管道，涵盖三类弯曲和矩形族，使用Cf-252点源在OpenMC中运行MAGIC生成权重窗；训练一个1.9百万参数的U-Net，以MAGIC下界窗的对数值为学习目标，输入为二维管道几何和源位置。

结果： 在留出管道上，携带通量网格的误差为0.14至0.51个数量级；重复MAGIC运行在该部分的可复现性为百分之几个数量级，因此残差主要为模型误差，而低于10^-6的区域参考值本身是噪声。未重新训练应用于矩形Protvino迷宫时，预测与MAGIC相关系数为0.96；作为权重窗并在部署时钳制最深网格后，出口探测器品质因数为模拟值的54倍、同几何MAGIC生成运行的1.7倍、复用MAGIC权重窗的0.80倍，而后者不计构造成本。任意权重窗集的运行成本由最深网格决定，并可由布居代理量预测。

结论： 初步结果表明，CNN可在毫秒级学习并预测MAGIC权重窗下界，在未见几何上具有较高相关性和显著方差缩减潜力；但预测仍存在模型误差，性能尚低于不计构造成本的复用MAGIC权重窗，作者将其定位为初步可行性结果。

原始摘要： Forward-flux-based weight windows give global variance reduction for duct-streaming shielding problems, but each new geometry needs an iterative Monte Carlo calculation to build them. We report preliminary results on a convolutional neural network (CNN) that learns the map from a two-dimensional duct geometry and source position to the lower window bound of such an iteration, here the MAGIC generator in OpenMC, in milliseconds. A set of 250 random concrete ducts of three curved and rectangular families on the Protvino labyrinth bounding box was run in OpenMC with a Cf-252 point source and MAGIC generation, and a 1.9-million-parameter U-Net was trained on the logarithm of the lower bound. On held-out ducts the error in the cells that carry the flux is 0.14 to 0.51 decades; replicate MAGIC runs show this part of the map to be reproducible to a few hundredths of a decade, so the residual is model error, while below $10^{-6}$ the reference itself is noise. Applied without retraining to the rectangular Protvino labyrinth, the prediction correlates with MAGIC at 0.96. Used as weight windows there, with its deepest cells clamped at deployment, it gives an exit-detector figure of merit 54 times the analog value, 1.7 times that of the MAGIC generation run for the same geometry, and 0.80 of that of reused MAGIC windows whose construction it does not pay for. The run cost of any window set is shown to be set by its deepest cells and predictable from a population proxy.

</details>


### [108] [ML-COHP: predicting bond descriptors using latent space of a universal interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40445)
*Daniil S. Alekseev, Ilya V. Chepkasov, Alexander G. Kvashnin*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作用 MACE 基础模型的冻结描述符预测 ICOHP，以低成本替代逐个结构进行 DFT 单点加 LOBSTER 投影；在高熵二硼化物、负载 Pt 团簇/纳米颗粒等体系上，强键误差为 1.6–4.1%，并能在约 1 秒内重现晶界 ICOHP。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： ICOHP 是 DFT 中衡量键强的标准量，但每个结构都需要单独的 DFT 单点和 LOBSTER 投影；对于百原子级晶界或催化纳米颗粒，这一流程需要数天，计算成本过高。

方法： 从 MACE 基础模型的冻结描述符出发预测 ICOHP；采用对称配对头读取两个原子的标量通道和键投影矢量通道，并对由同一模型元素嵌入构建的配对基线进行修正。模型在高熵二硼化物 (Hf,Zr,Ti,Nb,Ta)B2、缺陷石墨烯上的 Pt 团簇以及吸附 O2 的 Pt/Pd 纳米颗粒上测试。

结果： 对强于 0.5 eV 的键，误差为 1.6–4.1%，约为 Ferreira 等人在相同数据上训练的 Gaussian-process 模型误差的一半。仅用高温 HEB2 快照训练时，模型可在约 1 秒内将弛豫晶界胞的 LOBSTER ICOHP 重现到 0.06 eV，而 LOBSTER 需 5–51 小时。矢量通道使误差相对标量通道减半，基线使模型仅用两个训练结构仍可使用。结合微调 MACE 势给出的几何结构，模型可给出 Pt/Pd 颗粒在连续 O2 覆盖范围内的成键情况；对这些颗粒的 DFT 计算表明，O2 使 Pt-rich 壳层损失的金属—金属键合比 Pd 壳层多数倍。

结论： MACE 描述符加对称配对头可作为快速、低训练数据需求的 ICOHP 代理模型，在多种催化与晶界体系上保持较高精度，并能显著缩短相对 LOBSTER 的计算时间，从数小时降至约秒级。

原始摘要： The integrated crystal orbital Hamilton population (ICOHP) is the standard measure of bond strength in a DFT calculation, but every structure needs its own DFT single point and LOBSTER projection, which takes days for a cell of a hundred atoms, such as a grain boundary or a catalytic nanoparticle. We predict ICOHP from the frozen descriptors of a MACE foundation model. A symmetric pair head reads the scalar and bond-projected vector channels of the two atoms and corrects a pair baseline built from the element embeddings of the same model. We tested the model on the high-entropy diboride (Hf,Zr,Ti,Nb,Ta)B$_2$, Pt clusters on defective graphene and Pt/Pd nanoparticles with adsorbed O$_2$. The error on bonds stronger than 0.5 eV is 1.6-4.1%, about half that of the Gaussian-process model of Ferreira et al. trained on the same data. Trained only on high-temperature HEB$_2$ snapshots, the model reproduces the LOBSTER ICOHP of relaxed grain boundary cells to 0.06 eV in about a second, against 5-51 hours of LOBSTER. The vector channels halve the error compared to the scalar ones, and the baseline keeps the model usable with two training structures. With geometries from a fine-tuned MACE potential, the model gives the bonding of Pt/Pd particles over a continuous O$_2$ coverage, and DFT on these particles shows that O$_2$ costs a Pt-rich shell several times more metal--metal bonding than a Pd shell.

</details>


### [109] [A Gaussian-Process Surrogate for Control Rod Worth with Calibrated Uncertainty: Application to VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40389)
*Sean Gohlke, Jilang Miao*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文在VERA基准题第4题上构建反应性随控制棒位置变化的高斯过程代理模型，用核函数导数闭式给出微分棒价值及其方差，并在独立测试中验证其精度、不确定度校准和积分棒价值一致性。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 传统微分和积分控制棒价值通常通过对离散棒位处的特征值计算作差分获得，这种方法对导数的分辨率较差，而且不能给出导数的不确定度。

方法： 针对由3×3组件构成的VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4（含混合AIC/B4C棒束控制组件，用OpenMC和ENDF/B-VIII.0建模），构建反应性随棒位变化的高斯过程代理。模型用参数化S型均值函数承载动态范围，用Matern-5/2高斯过程建模残差，并把每次计算的蒙特卡洛不确定度作为已知观测噪声。由于微分是线性运算，微分棒价值及其方差可由核函数导数闭式得到。训练集为43次计算、覆盖三种粒子数，测试集为11次独立计算。

结果： 代理模型在测试运行噪声之上满足固定的10 pcm精度目标；区间已校准，129次独立运行的标准化留一残差方差为1.07，95%区间为[0.85,1.39]。总棒价值与OpenMC直接端点差分在合成不确定度内一致，微分棒价值峰值定位误差不超过一个步长。三种拟合质量差异很大的均值函数给出的微分棒价值彼此相差不超过0.1 pcm/step，而其置信带宽度随核平滑度变化可达四倍。摘要称已报告所需运行数和剩余近似，但未给出具体数值。

结论： 高斯过程代理能够为微分和积分控制棒价值提供带校准不确定度的连续估计，并避免离散差分对导数分辨不足的问题；其总价值与直接端点差分一致，微分峰值定位准确，且对均值函数形式不敏感而对核平滑度较敏感。摘要还概述了将该方法扩展到棒位置与可溶硼的设想。

原始摘要： Differential and integral control rod worth are usually obtained by differencing eigenvalue calculations at discrete rod positions, which resolves the derivative poorly and gives it no uncertainty. We build a Gaussian-process (GP) surrogate for reactivity as a function of rod position on VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4, a $3\times3$ assembly cluster with a hybrid AIC/B$_4$C rod cluster control assembly, modelled in OpenMC with ENDF/B-VIII.0. A parametric sigmoidal mean carries the dynamic range, a Matern-5/2 GP carries the residual, and the reported Monte Carlo uncertainty of each run enters as known observation noise. Because differentiation is linear, the differential rod worth and its variance follow in closed form from the kernel derivatives. Trained on 43 runs at three particle counts and tested on 11 independent runs, the surrogate meets a fixed 10 pcm accuracy target above the test-run noise, and its intervals are calibrated: the standardized leave-one-out residuals over 129 independent runs have variance 1.07 with 95% interval [0.85, 1.39]. The total worth agrees with the direct OpenMC end-point difference within combined uncertainty, and the differential worth peak is located to within one step. Three mean functions of very different fit quality give the same differential worth to within 0.1 pcm/step, while the width of its band depends on the kernel smoothness by a factor of four. The number of runs required and the remaining approximations are reported, and the extension to rod position and soluble boron is outlined.

</details>


### [110] [PhysicsNeMo-Mesh: GPU-Native Meshes and Simulation Cases for Physics AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39072)
*Peter D. Sharpe, Corey Adams, Negin Sobhani, Sanjay Choudhry*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文介绍 PhysicsNeMo-Mesh：一个在 GPU 上批量执行、可嵌入 PyTorch 自动微分并保留网格语义的开源网格处理模块，用于把物理仿真机器学习中的预处理从一次性数据整理移到使用点，以缓解 I/O 瓶颈并支持多模型复用。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 动机是解决物理仿真机器学习的三类瓶颈：样本相对单次前向计算过大，GPU 利用率受 I/O 限制；传统预处理昂贵，只能在数据整理阶段一次性完成，难以针对每个模型和工作流在使用点采用不同预处理；等变与网格原生架构需要数据整理通常会丢弃的信息。

方法： PhysicsNeMo-Mesh 提供类似 PyVista 的 GPU 批量网格处理，并置于 PyTorch 自动微分中；为点、单元和整体网格上的字段附加名称与形状等语义，并以 simulation case 保存完整 PDE 问题规格，包括边界条件及其规定值。快速 GPU 网格处理使按使用点预处理可行，并可与训练重叠；摘要未提供具体算子、数据格式和实现细节。

结果： 在单块 GB200 GPU 上，网格操作比 72 线程 VTK 快 6.2–1,068×；加载完整 case 比读取其 VTK 文件快 8.0–88×，双方读取相同字段时为 8.0×。与训练重叠时，该预处理使 127 ms 的 Transolver 步吞吐下降 2.1%。在 PhysicsNeMo 公开 recipe 中，一份存储的 DrivAerML 数据集可供给 Transolver、GeoTransolver 和 GLOBE。形状与壁面条件均变化时，逐边界条件将热传导误差从 87% 降至 6–9%；保留字段秩和命名边界可保留下一代架构所需的语义结构。

结论： PhysicsNeMo-Mesh 兼有中间数组存储的速度和 VTK 等归档格式的灵活性，使一份数据集服务多个模型与工作流，并保留字段秩、命名边界等语义结构，以满足先进等变和网格原生架构需求。摘要未给出更广泛的基准或部署结论。

原始摘要： PhysicsNeMo-Mesh is the mesh module of the open-source PhysicsNeMo library. It provides mesh processing similar to PyVista, but on the GPU, in batches, and inside PyTorch's automatic differentiation. Like xarray for unstructured meshes, it attaches semantics to data: fields on points, cells, and whole meshes have names and shapes, and a simulation case keeps the complete PDE problem specification, including boundary conditions and their prescribed values. It addresses three problems that plague machine learning for physical simulation. First, GPU utilization is bottlenecked by I/O, because samples are very large relative to the compute per forward pass. Second, because traditional preprocessing is expensive, it is necessarily done once, during curation, rather than differently for each model and workflow at the point of use. Finally, advanced equivariant and mesh-native architectures require information that curation ordinarily discards. Fast GPU mesh processing makes it practical to preprocess at the point of use: on one GB200 GPU, mesh operations run $6.2\text{--}1{,}068\times$ faster than VTK with 72 threads, and loading a whole case is $8.0\text{--}88\times$ faster than reading its VTK files ($8.0\times$ when both read the same fields). Overlapped with training, this preprocessing costs $2.1\%$ of throughput for a $127\,\text{ms}$ Transolver step. So one dataset serves many models and workflows: in PhysicsNeMo's public recipe, one stored DrivAerML dataset feeds Transolver, GeoTransolver, and GLOBE. PhysicsNeMo-Mesh combines the speed of an intermediate array store with the flexibility of an archival format such as VTK. When shapes and wall conditions both vary, per-boundary conditions cut a heat-conduction error from $87\%$ to $6\text{--}9\%$. And by keeping field ranks and named boundaries, it retains the semantic structure that next-generation architectures will need.

</details>


### [111] [Exploring Fractional Quantum Hall States with Deep Learning: From Abelian to Non-Abelian Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38533)
*Xiangyu Ma, Kehuang Chen, Yuntian Gu, Weiluo Ren, Ji Chen, Di Luo, Fengcheng Wu, Xiang Li*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种对称性适配的神经网络波函数，可同时描述阿贝尔与非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔态并纳入所有朗道能级；计算复现了复合费米液体及低填充下的维格纳晶化，并在第一朗道能级部分填充时给出与 ν=4/3、7/5 的 \bar{2}^{k}1^{k+1} 型 parton 相一致的重叠与纠缠谱证据，以及 ν=3/2 的反 Pfaffian 型纠缠结构。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 分数量子霍尔系统具有强关联拓扑相及拓扑量子计算潜力，但其微观基态难以识别，原因是竞争相常近简并，且朗道能级混合难以处理。

方法： 引入一种对称性适配的神经网络波函数，能够描述阿贝尔和非阿贝尔态，并纳入所有朗道能级；通过有限尺寸重叠和纠缠谱分析与其他候选拓扑序比较。

结果： 方法准确捕捉复合费米液体以及低填充下维格纳晶化的出现；当第一朗道能级部分填充时，有限尺寸重叠和纠缠谱提供与 ν=4/3 和 7/5 的 \bar{2}^{k}1^{k+1} 型 parton 相一致的证据，而 ν=3/2 态表现出反 Pfaffian 型纠缠结构。

结论： 神经网络波函数为研究分数量子霍尔系统中竞争拓扑序提供了灵活框架。

原始摘要： Fractional quantum Hall systems host strongly correlated topological phases with potential applications in topological quantum computation, yet identifying their microscopic ground states remains challenging because competing phases are often nearly degenerate and Landau-level mixing is difficult to treat. Here, we introduce a symmetry-adapted neural-network wavefunction that describes Abelian and non-Abelian states while incorporating all Landau levels. The method accurately captures composite-fermion liquids and the emergence of Wigner crystallization at low filling. When the first Landau level is partially filled, finite-size overlaps and entanglement spectra provide evidence consistent with $\bar{2}^{k}1^{k+1}$-type parton phases at $ν=4/3$ and $7/5$, while the $ν=3/2$ state exhibits anti-Pfaffian-like entanglement structure. These results demonstrate that neural-network wavefunctions offer a flexible framework for studying competing topological orders in fractional quantum Hall systems.

</details>


### [112] [Generalizing Density Functional Theory with a Particle-Hole Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40621)
*Shurui Zhao, Madhubani Mukherjee, David A. Mazziotti*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种系统的粒子-空穴展开，将DFT推广为单电子约化密度矩阵的显式泛函，并以半正定规划求解；二阶修正可显著缓解自然轨道占据数过度分数化，在并苯和铁卟啉体系中改善占据数与基本能隙。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 传统密度泛函近似难以描述强电子关联，尤其是自然轨道占据数强烈分数化的情况，因此需要一种可系统改进、同时保持DFT级计算可行性的理论框架。

方法： 作者引入系统的粒子-空穴展开，通过单电子约化密度矩阵的显式泛函推广DFT；已推导的基于累积量的修正成为首阶项，热力学（熵）修正作为无穷阶成员且其系数由单一参数确定；该展开可表述为由半正定规划求解的矩问题，计算标度与DFT相当；一阶和二阶修正均给出闭式自然轨道占据数。

结果： 在并苯和铁卟啉上的应用表明，二阶修正大幅降低过度的轨道分数化，使自然轨道占据数和基本能隙与关联计算结果更一致；摘要未给出定量误差、测试集规模或与其他方法的系统比较。

结论： 该工作提供了一种计算上接近DFT、可系统加入粒子-空穴/累积量与熵修正的1-RDM泛函路线；二阶修正在所展示的强关联体系中优于未修正描述，但其广泛适用性仍需更多基准验证，摘要未提供相关细节。

原始摘要： Strong electron correlation remains difficult to describe within conventional density functional approximations, particularly when natural-orbital occupations become strongly fractional. We introduce a systematic particle-hole expansion that generalizes density functional theory (DFT) through an explicit functional of the one-electron reduced density matrix. The previously derived cumulant-based correction emerges as the leading term and the thermal (entropic) correction as an infinite-order member whose coefficients are fixed by a single parameter. The expansion can be formulated as a moment problem solved by semidefinite programming with computational scaling comparable to DFT. We show that both the first- and second-order corrections yield closed-form natural-orbital occupations. Applications to periacenes and Fe-porphyrin demonstrate that the second-order correction substantially reduces excessive orbital fractionalization, yielding natural-orbital occupations and fundamental gaps in improved agreement with correlated calculations.

</details>


### [113] [Single-Temperature Ranked Greedy Cluster Exchange for Max-Cut on Two-Dimensional Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40515)
*Hao Geng, Nan Su, Gabriele Cavallaro*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出单温度RGCX方法：按割值对副本排序并成对确定性地交换所有可提升较优副本割值的分歧簇，同时结合固定温度Metropolis扫描，以加速二维Ising自旋玻璃/零场环面Max-Cut的基态搜索；在Gset环面图上取得快速且高成功率的认证最优或最佳已知割。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 大尺寸二维环面Ising自旋玻璃的基态搜索仍很困难，粗糙能垒和亚稳态会阻碍局部更新启发式充分松弛；这类实例等价于零场环面Max-Cut，是优化启发式的标准基准。现有许多Monte Carlo方法将单自旋翻转或簇移动与温度阶梯或退火 schedule 结合，但即使调参，也很少能在规模上快速、可靠地收敛到基态，因此需要借助副本间合作实现更快的基态搜索。

方法： 提出排名贪婪簇交换（RGCX），并与固定温度Metropolis扫描结合形成单温度RGCX。方法按割值对副本排序，以确定副本对的处理顺序；对每一对副本，确定性地交换所有能够提高较优副本割值的分歧簇，同时保留补集，并保持该副本对割值之和不变。摘要未给出具体种群规模、交换间隔和温度的数值。

结果： 在20,000自旋的Gset实例G81上，达到认证最优且成功概率为99%的估计运行时间TTS99为8核桌面计算机上的12.1 s。在相同种群规模、交换间隔和温度下，该方法在全部17个环面Gset图上达到最佳已知割，其中包括9个认证最优。

结论： 研究推进了大型二维环面上的基态搜索，表明单温度RGCX是一种高效的基于种群的方法，将排名贪婪簇交换与固定温度Metropolis动力学结合起来。

原始摘要： Ground-state search in two-dimensional Ising spin glasses on large toroidal lattices remains challenging for local-update heuristics because rugged energy barriers impede relaxation across metastable states. Equivalent to zero-field toroidal Max-Cut problems, these spin-glass instances also serve as standard benchmarks for optimization heuristics. Many Monte Carlo approaches combine single-spin flips or cluster moves with temperature ladders or annealing schedules, yet even with tuned parameters, rarely achieve rapid, reliable ground-state convergence at scale. Here, we address the need for faster ground-state search through cooperation between replicas. We introduce ranked greedy cluster exchange (RGCX) and combine it with fixed-temperature Metropolis sweeps to form single-temperature RGCX. Replicas are ranked by cut to determine the pair-processing order. Each pair deterministically exchanges every disagreement cluster that raises the better replica's cut, retaining the complement and conserving the pair's summed cut. On the 20,000-spin Gset instance G81, the estimated runtime to reach the certified optimum with 99% success probability (TTS99) was 12.1 s on an 8-core desktop computer. With the same population size, exchange interval and temperature, the method reached the best known cuts on all 17 toroidal Gset graphs, including 9 certified optima. These findings advance ground-state search on large two-dimensional tori by establishing single-temperature RGCX as an efficient population-based method that combines ranked greedy cluster exchange with fixed-temperature Metropolis dynamics.

</details>


### [114] [Electric field control of anisotropic spin traffic between spin-filtering and magnon-scattering in a layered antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40423)
*Mohamed Soliman, Jaime Garrido Aldea, Davy Borowski, Dorye L. Esteras, Neeraj K. Rajak, Niels Chapuis, Abdelkarim Ouerghi, Arnaud Gloppe, Jérôme Robert, Bernard Doudin, Takashi Taniguchi, Kenji Watanabe, José H. García, Stephan Roche, Jean-François Dayen*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于层状A型反铁磁半导体CrSBr构建双栅场效应晶体管，实现载流子密度与位移场的独立调控；系统输运测量表明位移场而非掺杂主导磁阻比（MRR）的符号和大小，负/正MRR分量分别关联层间与层内自旋相关输运，并结合第一性原理计算与有效自旋电路模型解释各向异性磁输运。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 层状A型反铁磁半导体兼具稳健磁性与可栅控半导体特性，是自旋电子器件的潜在平台；CrSBr具有显著输运各向异性和复杂自旋动力学，需要明确如何用电场有效调控其自旋输运。

方法： 制备基于CrSBr的双栅场效应晶体管，独立控制载流子密度和位移场；开展系统输运测量以分析MRR；区分负和正MRR分量的来源并关联层间与层内自旋相关输运机制；采用第一性原理计算和有效自旋电路模型解释实验观测的各向异性磁输运。

结果： 实验发现位移场而非掺杂主要决定MRR的符号和大小；识别出负MRR和正MRR分量，并将其分别与层间和层内自旋相关输运机制联系起来；第一性原理计算和有效自旋电路模型能够重现各向异性磁输运。摘要未提供MRR数值、调控范围或器件性能的定量数据。

结论： 位移场是CrSBr中自旋输运的有效电学控制参数；多栅架构有望在其他层状反铁磁半导体中实现类似调控。

原始摘要： Layered A-type antiferromagnetic (AFM) semiconductors offer a unique platform for spintronic devices by combining robust magnetism with gate-tunable semiconducting properties. We present a dual-gate field-effect transistor based on CrSBr, a layered A-type AFM with pronounced transport anisotropy and complex spin dynamics. Our device provides independent control over carrier density and displacement field. Systematic transport measurements reveal that the displacement field, rather than doping, predominantly governs the sign and magnitude of magnetoresistance ratio (MRR). We identify the origins of both negative and positive MRR components and connect them to interlayer and intralayer spin-dependent transport mechanisms. We interpret these findings with ab initio calculations and an effective spin-circuit model that reproduces the anisotropic magnetotransport. Our results identify the displacement field as an efficient electrical control parameter of spin transport in CrSBr and suggest that multi-gate architectures could provide similar control in other layered antiferromagnetic semiconductors.

</details>


### [115] [Adiabatic Variational Principle: Born-Oppenheimer Theory for Emergent Electronic-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40117)
*Yang Yang, Ali Rayat, Ho Jang, Yunhao Fan, Sankha Subhra Bakshi, Gia-Wei Chern*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出绝热变分原理（AVP），从相互作用多体哈密顿量直接导出序参量动力学，将微观多体描述与相场类非平衡模拟连接起来，并用于电荷密度波和自旋密度波动力学。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 现有微观多体方法对平衡态量子态的描写日益精确，但涌现序动力学的大规模模拟通常依赖唯象方程和预设的自由能泛函，缺乏直接从微观哈密顿量出发的、与求解器无关的基础。

方法： AVP假设微观弛豫快于序参量演化，使多体态随集体构型绝热跟随，类似Born-Oppenheimer分离；每个瞬时序参量构型定义受约束的准稳态多体态，约束的拉格朗日乘子给出驱动集体演化的热力学力；非线性Mori-Zwanzig投影理论将可逆动力学与集体变量的微观代数联系起来，并把耗散和涨落归因于被消除的自由度。

结果： 将AVP应用于电荷密度波和自旋密度波动力学，可恢复Allen-Cahn畴生长，解析演化中的畴网络及其微观电子结构，并为巡游磁性得到广义Landau-Lifshitz动力学。

结论： AVP为涌现电子序的非平衡动力学相场类模拟提供了不依赖特定求解器的微观基础；摘要未提供定量误差、计算成本或实验对比等具体验证结果。

原始摘要： Recent advances in microscopic many-body methods have enabled increasingly accurate descriptions of equilibrium quantum states, yet large-scale simulations of emergent-order dynamics typically rely on phenomenological equations with prescribed free-energy functionals. We introduce the Adiabatic Variational Principle (AVP), a framework for deriving order-parameter dynamics directly from an interacting many-body Hamiltonian. When microscopic relaxation is fast compared with order-parameter evolution, the many-body state follows the collective configuration adiabatically, in analogy with the Born--Oppenheimer separation of electronic and nuclear motion. This assumption makes explicit the adiabatic closure implicit in conventional phase-field descriptions. Each instantaneous order-parameter configuration then defines a constrained quasi-stationary many-body state, and the Lagrange multipliers enforcing these constraints directly provide the thermodynamic forces driving the collective evolution. Nonlinear Mori--Zwanzig projection theory further connects the reversible dynamics to the microscopic algebra of the collective variables and relates dissipation and fluctuations to the eliminated degrees of freedom. Applications to charge- and spin-density-wave dynamics recover Allen--Cahn domain growth, resolve evolving domain networks together with their microscopic electronic structure, and yield generalized Landau--Lifshitz dynamics for itinerant magnetism. The AVP thus provides a solver-independent microscopic foundation for phase-field-like simulations of the nonequilibrium dynamics of emergent electronic order.

</details>


### [116] [Static Response of the Strongly Coupled Electron Gas from Diffusion Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40093)
*Muhammed H. Güneş, Yubo Yang, Vitaly Gorelov, Miguel A. Morales, Matteo Gatti, Lucia Reining, Shiwei Zhang*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文用扩散蒙特卡洛计算均匀电子气在2≤rs≤100范围内的静态密度响应χ(q)和局域场因子G(q)，给出扰动势优化等实用方法，并发现低密度下G(q)在中间q呈非单调结构，且可用统一参数化描述液相数据。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 零温G(q)的精确数据此前主要限于金属密度（rs≤10），而G(q)对介电屏蔽、有效电子-电子相互作用及TDDFT交换关联核很重要，因此需要扩展到低密度并厘清可靠的计算方法。

方法： 采用扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）计算2≤rs≤100的χ(q)与G(q)；分析试用波函数中扰动势的优化，给出最优值的闭式表达式及线性响应窗口的密度依赖；检验backflow关联和系统尺寸的影响；使用Slater-Jastrow波函数与适中系统；最后用参数化G(q)拟合整个液相的数据。

结果： 在金属密度下，q>kF时与先前DMC数据一致，并扩展到q<kF；在rs=2时与图解QMC一致。低密度下G(q)在中间q出现非单调结构，响应趋近非相互作用气体；与有限温度PIMC定量一致，提示其有限温度结构继承自基态。扰动势优化很重要，差的选择可反转响应符号；backflow不改变χ(q)，G(q)对系统尺寸仅弱依赖。

结论： 研究为电子气及真实材料响应性质的未来QMC计算提供实用指南；所给G(q)参数化可重现液相数据，并将中间q的非单调结构同对关联函数和静态结构因子中的短程有序联系起来。

原始摘要： The local field factor $G(q)$ of the HEG describes how exchange and correlation modify the response to a static perturbation, and enters dielectric screening, effective electron--electron interactions, and the xc kernel of TDDFT. Accurate zero-temperature data for $G(q)$, however, remain limited to metallic densities, $r_s\leq10$. In this work, we present diffusion Monte Carlo calculations of the static density response function $χ(q)$ and of $G(q)$ for $2\leq r_s\leq100$, and describe and discuss the methodological steps that enabled these calculations. We show how optimization of the perturbing potential in the trial wave function has an important effect, as a poor choice can reverse the sign of the response. A closed-form expression is provided for its optimal value, together with the density dependence of the linear-response window. We further show that backflow correlations leave $χ(q)$ unchanged and that $G(q)$ depends only weakly on system size even at very low density, so that Slater--Jastrow wave functions and modest system sizes suffice. Together, these results provide a practical guide for future QMC calculations of response properties of the electron gas and real materials. Our results agree with previous DMC data at metallic densities for $q>k_F$, and extend them below $k_F$. They also agree with diagrammatic QMC at $r_s=2$. At lower densities, our results show that $G(q)$ develops a nonmonotonic structure at intermediate-$q$, where the response approaches that of the noninteracting gas. Quantitative agreement is seen with finite-temperature path integral Monte Carlo, suggesting that the previously reported finite-temperature structure is inherited from the ground state. Using a parametrization of $G(q)$ that reproduces our data throughout the liquid phase, we relate this structure to short-range order in the pair correlation function and the static structure factor.

</details>


### [117] [Excitons in solid molecular hydrogen from quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39894)
*Edgar Josué Landinez Borda, Ravindra Shinde, Claudia Filippi, Markus Holzmann, Saverio Moroni*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文用量子蒙特卡罗研究压缩下phase-I固态分子氢的最低中性激发，比较单轨道替换与包含全部单粒子-空穴跃迁的多行列式QMC波函数，并对照BSE和TDDFT；多行列式对激发能影响很小，却对激子局域化及相关响应量至关重要，且方法差异随压缩减小。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 旨在研究phase-I固态分子氢在持续压缩下最低中性激发的结构与响应性质，并评估激发态波函数形式、多行列式展开以及代表热涨落和量子涨落的核构型对结果的影响；摘要未进一步说明更宏观的应用动机。

方法： 采用量子蒙特卡罗（QMC）方法计算激发态，使用仅含单轨道替换的波函数，以及包含所有单粒子-空穴跃迁的多行列式展开，并与Bethe-Salpeter方程（BSE）和含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）比较；考察多个代表热涨落和量子涨落的核构型，同时涉及完全优化和虚时间投影。

结果： 多行列式展开对QMC激发能影响很小，但对激子局域化及相关量很关键，且不受完全优化和虚时间投影影响。低密度强绝缘区，多行列式QMC的局域化程度与BSE相当，部分情形更强；跃迁偶极矩和激发诱导力呈类似趋势，QMC与BSE总体一致，但在局域化显著不同的核构型上差异明显。TDDFT通常给出更扩展的激子和更弱的跃迁偶极矩。随着压缩接近半导体区，这些差异减小，多粒子-空穴跃迁的重要性降低。

结论： 多行列式展开对于正确描述激子局域化及相关响应性质至关重要，而其对激发能并非关键；BSE在局域化程度较高的情形与QMC吻合较好，TDDFT倾向于给出更离域的激子；压缩使系统接近半导体区时，多粒子-空穴跃迁重要性下降，QMC、BSE与TDDFT之间的差异随之减小。摘要未提供具体数值结果。

原始摘要： We use quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods to investigate the lowest neutral excitation of solid molecular hydrogen in phase-I under increasing compression, with emphasis on structural and response properties. We employ QMC wave functions for the excited state that feature only a single orbital substitution, or a multideterminant expansion including every single particle-hole transition, and compare the results with the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) and time-dependent density-functional theory (TDDFT). We examine a number of nuclear configurations representing thermal and quantum fluctuations. The effect of the multideterminant expansion is marginal on the QMC excitation energies but crucial on the exciton localization and related quantities, irrespective of full optimization and imaginary time projection. In the strong insulating regime at low density, multideterminant QMC results match and in some cases surpass the degree of localization afforded by BSE. A similar trend is observed for transition dipole moments and excitation-induced forces, with generally good agreement between QMC and BSE but marked discrepancies for nuclear configurations where localization differs significantly. TDDFT generally gives a more extended exciton with correspondingly weaker transition dipole moments. These differences decrease upon compression, as the system approaches a semiconducting regime where multiple particle-hole transitions are less important.

</details>


### [118] [RAKI: High-Order Alternative Finite Differences for General Relativistic Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39792)
*Rahime Matur, Beyhan Karakas, Ian Hawke, Nils Andersson*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了 RAKI：首个采用高阶 AFD-WENO-AO 重构和完全非交错、节点中心矢量势公式来模拟双中子星并合的 GRMHD 代码；它在平滑测试中最高达到九阶收敛，在磁化 TOV 测试中显著减少磁场损失，并在并合模拟中保持无单极约束到机器精度。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 双中子星并合及黑洞—中子星并合是重要的多信使源，可揭示并合期间和之后磁场放大效率、磁星与喷流形成等未解现象；准确理解这些现象需要精确模拟磁场演化，而这对当前 GRMHD 模拟仍是挑战。

方法： 作者开发了 RAKI（Relativistic Astrophysics Code Implementation of the Alternative Finite Difference Approach for GRMHD），使用高阶替代有限差分方法、自适应阶 WENO（AFD-WENO-AO）重构，以及完全非交错、节点中心的矢量势公式，并用于磁化 TOV 星测试和双中子星并合模拟。

结果： RAKI 在平滑测试问题中最高达到九阶收敛，并能准确捕捉间断；在磁化 TOV 星测试中，约 2 ms 内保留初始最大磁场强度的 99.2%，相同分辨率下磁场损失比参考 GRMHD 代码低一个数量级；在双中子星并合模拟中，五阶 AFD-WENO-AO 重构使磁化和非磁化模型的引力波相位均达到约三阶收敛；离散无单极约束在无交错网格下保持到机器精度，即使并合模拟包含移动细化区域亦然。

结论： 结果表明，RAKI 是一个高阶、非交错的 GRMHD 框架，可精确模拟中子星并合中的磁场演化，并服务于多信使天体物理相关研究。

原始摘要： Binary neutron star (BNS) and black hole--neutron star mergers are among the most important multimessenger sources. Observations of these events could provide valuable insights into several unresolved phenomena, ranging from the efficiency of magnetic field amplification during and after mergers to the formation of magnetars and jets. Understanding these phenomena requires accurate modelling of magnetic field evolution, which remains a challenge for state-of-the-art general relativistic magnetohydrodynamics (GRMHD) simulations. In this work, we present \texttt{RAKI} (Relativistic Astrophysics Code Implementation of the Alternative Finite Difference Approach for GRMHD), the first GRMHD code to simulate BNS mergers using high-order alternative finite-difference methods with adaptive-order WENO (AFD-WENO-AO) reconstruction and a fully non-staggered, node-centred vector-potential formulation. The code achieves up to ninth-order convergence in smooth test problems while accurately capturing discontinuities. In magnetised Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff (TOV) star tests, \texttt{RAKI} retains $99.2$ per cent of the initial maximum magnetic field strength over ${\sim}2$ ms, exhibiting an order of magnitude less magnetic field loss than a reference GRMHD code at the same resolution. In BNS merger simulations, fifth-order AFD-WENO-AO reconstruction yields approximately third-order convergence of the gravitational-wave phase in both magnetised and unmagnetised models. The discrete no-monopole constraint is preserved to machine precision without staggered grids, even during BNS mergers with moving refinement regions. These results establish \texttt{RAKI} as a high-order, non-staggered GRMHD framework for accurate modelling of magnetic field evolution in neutron star mergers and its implications for multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [119] [Transformer-inspired architectures make machine-learned electron densities accurate and transferable enough to bypass self-consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39687)
*Jeheon Woo*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于 Transformer 架构的 SwinUNETR-V2 与无注意力 NeXtUNETR，用于高精度预测 DFT 的电子密度；在 QM9 上取得领先精度，单次 Kohn-Sham 步骤即可复现自洽电子结构，并能支持几何优化、从头算分子动力学及小数据微调迁移。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 电子密度是密度泛函理论的核心变量；研究旨在用机器学习高精度预测电子密度，从而绕过昂贵的自洽迭代，并借助每参数计算量小、易扩展的架构降低成本。

方法： 构建受 Transformer 启发的 SwinUNETR-V2 及其无注意力对应模型 NeXtUNETR；模型参数量扩展成本低。预测密度用于单次 Kohn-Sham 步骤，并评估能量、力、HOMO-LUMO 间隙、几何优化和从头算分子动力学，以及在小数据集微调后的迁移能力。

结果： 在 QM9 上，SwinUNETR-V2 和 NeXtUNETR 的密度归一化平均绝对误差分别为 0.075% 和 0.046%，达到当时最佳精度；单次 Kohn-Sham 步骤可复现自洽电子结构。NeXtUNETR 的总能量、力和 HOMO-LUMO 间隙中位误差分别为 0.036 meV、0.65 meV/angstrom 和 1.8 meV；力可恢复自洽几何优化极小值并驱动从头算分子动力学；经小数据集微调后，两模型可迁移至含 QM9 之外元素或比其分子更大的体系。

结论： 所提网络能高精度预测电子密度，足以替代自洽迭代中的密度求解，并在能量、力、电子隙、几何优化和分子动力学中保持可靠性；同时具备对小数据集和分布外体系的迁移能力。

原始摘要： We introduce machine learning models that accurately predict the electron density, the central variable of density functional theory. They are built on Transformer-inspired architectures, which require little computation per parameter and can therefore be enlarged at low cost. The proposed models, SwinUNETR-V2 and its attention-free counterpart NeXtUNETR, achieve state-of-the-art accuracy on QM9, with normalized mean absolute errors in the density averaging 0.075% and 0.046%, respectively. The predicted densities are accurate enough to bypass the self-consistent iteration: a single Kohn-Sham step reproduces the self-consistent electronic structure. For NeXtUNETR, the median errors are 0.036 meV in the total energy, 0.65 meV/angstrom in the forces and 1.8 meV in the HOMO-LUMO gap. The forces not only recover the self-consistent minima in geometry optimization but also successfully drive ab initio molecular dynamics. Fine-tuned on small datasets, both networks also transfer accurately to systems containing elements absent from QM9 or larger than its molecules.

</details>


### [120] [Hidden Phase Transition in Single-Polymer Stretching under Elongational Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39575)
*Aiqing Zhu, Fushuai Wang, Eleonore Vissol-Gaudin, Qianxiao Li, Beatrice W. Soh*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文利用条件扩展的 UIDD 从微观轨迹中联合学习不同流强下的约化随机动力学及非平衡自由能景观，发现平面拉伸流中除线圈-伸展转变外还存在第二个动力学转变，并用简化链模型和大偏差分析及单分子 DNA 实验提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 研究动机是识别聚合物链在平面拉伸流中除已知线圈-伸展转变之外是否还存在新的转变，并建立能从微观轨迹学习流强相关约化动力学与非平衡自由能景观的方法。

方法： 作者提出 Universal and Identifiable Dissipative Dynamics（UIDD）的条件扩展，联合学习跨流强的约化随机动力学，并获得相应的稳态非平衡自由能景观；同时用简化解析链模型进行大偏差分析，并参考单分子 DNA 实验。

结果： 随着流强增加，演化中的自由能景观先呈现预期的线圈-伸展转变，随后出现此前未识别的动力学转变：伸展态仍持续存在，但在小端到端距离条件下，沿折叠坐标的分布变为双峰。简化解析链模型的大偏差分析复现了两个转变，并表明它们在长链极限下是相变；单分子 DNA 实验通过预测的折叠响应反转提供定性支持。

结论： 论文识别出平面拉伸流驱动聚合物链 stretching 的第二个转变，并表明条件 UIDD 可学习流强相关的约化随机动力学与非平衡自由能景观；该转变在简化模型中具有长链极限相变特征，并获得单分子实验的定性支持。

原始摘要： Using machine learning, we identify a second transition in polymer chain stretching driven by planar elongational flow, beyond the well-known coil-stretch transition. We achieve this by developing a conditional extension of Universal and Identifiable Dissipative Dynamics (UIDD) that jointly learns reduced stochastic dynamics from microscopic trajectories across flow strengths and yields the corresponding stationary nonequilibrium free energy landscapes. As the flow strength increases, the evolving landscape exhibits the expected coil-stretch transition, and then a previously unrecognized dynamical transition. In the latter, the stretched states persist, but the distribution along a folding coordinate becomes bimodal when conditioned on small end-to-end separation. Large-deviation analysis of a simplified analytical chain model reproduces both transitions and shows that they are phase transitions in the long-chain limit. Single-molecule DNA experiments provide qualitative support for the dynamical transition through the predicted reversal of the folding response.

</details>


### [121] [Distilling Large-Scale Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials to Predict Long-Timescale Electrolyte Transport Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39513)
*Ishan Amin, Yue Jian, Nitesh Kumar, Christine Zhang, Jianwei Lai, Casey S. Mezerkor, Aaron M. Melemed, Jonathan R. Thurston, Neil P. Dasgupta, J. David Bazak, Ethan J. Crumlin, Samuel Blau, Aditi S. Krishnapriyan*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种知识蒸馏方法，将大型预训练机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的精度迁移到快速、体系特异的“学生”MLIP中，用于液体电解质输运性质模拟；学生模型约快40倍，可在数天内生成稳定的20 ns轨迹，其预测的自扩散系数、离子电导率和剪切粘度与实验趋势相符，并优于经典力场预测。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 准确预测液体电解质输运性质对下一代电池设计至关重要，但所需力场既要达到量子力学精度，又要足够快以支持长时标模拟。大型预训练MLIP虽接近密度泛函理论精度，但仍过慢，难以在输运性质收敛所需的时标上模拟；同时希望避免额外从头算数据。

方法： 采用知识蒸馏：以大型“教师”MLIP为监督，将精度迁移到快速、体系特异的“学生”MLIP，且不增加从头算数据；在多种钠离子和锂离子电解质上进行评估。文中未提供蒸馏损失函数、模型架构等具体细节。

结果： 蒸馏模型运行速度约提升40倍，复现教师模型预测的结构观测量，并能在数天内产生稳定的20 ns轨迹，而教师模型需要数月。在多种钠离子和锂离子电解质中，学生模型预测的自扩散系数、离子电导率和剪切粘度跟随实验趋势，并优于经典力场预测；具体误差数值未提供。

结论： 知识蒸馏是将大型MLIP的精度转化为快速、长时标模拟能力的实用路线，可满足高通量电解质筛选对准确输运性质预测的需求。

原始摘要： Accurately predicting the transport properties of liquid electrolytes is critical for next-generation battery design and requires force fields that are both quantum-mechanically accurate and fast enough for long-timescale simulation. Recent large-scale pretrained machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) approach density-functional-theory accuracy, but remain too slow to access the necessary timescales to converge transport properties. We provide a knowledge distillation procedure to transfer the accuracy of a large-scale "teacher" MLIP into fast, system-specific "students", without any additional ab initio data. Our distilled models run ~40x faster, reproduce teacher-predicted structural observables, and produce stable 20 ns trajectories in days, rather than the months the teacher would require. Across a diverse set of sodium- and lithium-ion electrolytes, student-predicted self-diffusivities, ionic conductivities, and shear viscosities track experiment and outperform classical force-field predictions, establishing distillation as a practical route to accurate, long-timescale simulations necessary for high-throughput electrolyte screening.

</details>


### [122] [Ten-loop fixed-dimension renormalization-group functions of the three-dimensional O(N) model from distributed GPU-accelerated parametric integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39307)
*Paolo Ribeca*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文将三维 O(N) 模型的固定维度（大质量零动量方案）重整化群函数 β、η、ν 从已知八阶扩展到十阶，β 扩展到十一阶，适用于一般 N；采用 GPU 热带 Monte Carlo 计算大量参数积分，并用 Callan-Symanzik 方程逐阶求系数。结果复现低阶已知值，给出更高精度系数和重求和结果，并指出 N=1 时与共形 bootstrap 的偏差及 N=3 时重求和误差占主导。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 此前三维 O(N) 模型的 β、η、ν 在固定维度大质量零动量方案中已知到八阶。作者希望扩展到十阶（β 到十一阶）并适用于一般 N，同时重新评估所有低阶系数、提高 η 和 ν 的八阶系数精度，并检验微扰展开重求和与共形 bootstrap 结果的一致性及误差来源。

方法： 在固定维度大质量零动量方案中，利用 Callan-Symanzik 方程，使每阶系数由逐阶求和给出；这些求和是有限、零动量 \bar R 减除参数积分的线性组合，每个图对应一个积分。计算需将二点和四点函数算到十圈，最后一阶有 1,593,254 个图。作者在 GPU 上用热带 Monte Carlo 抽样，抽样密度按 forest terms 适配，并重新计算所有低阶。

结果： 计算在 3 个标准差内复现二至七圈的已知求和，并在较低精度下于九阶和十阶与通过中间方案的独立路线一致。新系数的相对不确定度为几个 10^{-5} 或更好；η 和 ν 的八阶系数精度分别提高 120 倍和 4 倍。从七圈起，将一阶积分到固定相对精度所需时间每圈仅增长 2.4–2.9 倍，但 integrand 构造仍按阶乘增长，且在十阶时其成本超过积分。重求和后的十阶级数给出 N=1 时 ν=0.62997(51)、η=0.0333(18)、ω=0.803(14)，对照共形 bootstrap 的 0.62997097(12)、0.036297612(48)、0.82968(23)；N=3 时偏差增大，不确定度现在由重求和而非系数主导。

结论： 作者实现了三维 O(N) 模型重整化群函数到十阶、β 到十一阶的高阶微扰计算，并获得高精度新系数；在 N=1 时与共形 bootstrap 的对比显示，当前误差主要来自重求和，N=3 时这一现象更明显。所有结果均由公开的独立、精确汇总运行账本自动生成或校验，该账本托管在 GitHub 上，欢迎任何人贡献计算时间。

原始摘要： The renormalization-group functions $β$, $η$ and $ν$ of the three-dimensional $O(N)$ model in fixed dimension (massive zero-momentum scheme) were known to eighth order in the coupling. We extend them to tenth order, and $β$ to eleventh, for general $N$, which requires the two- and four-point functions to ten loops (1,593,254 graphs at the last order), and re-evaluate all lower orders. Through the Callan-Symanzik equation every coefficient follows from per-order sums that are linear in finite, zero-momentum $\bar R$-subtracted parametric integrals, one per graph, which we sample on GPUs by tropical Monte Carlo with densities adapted to the forest terms. The computation reproduces the known sums from two to seven loops within three standard deviations and agrees at ninth and tenth order, at lower precision, with an independent route through an intermediate scheme. The new coefficients have relative uncertainties of a few $10^{-5}$ or better, and the eighth-order coefficients of $η$ and $ν$ improve by factors of 120 and 4. The time needed to integrate an order to fixed relative precision grows by only 2.4-2.9 per loop from seven loops on, while the construction of the integrands still grows factorially and, at tenth order, costs more than the integration. Resummed, the tenth-order series give $ν=0.62997(51)$, $η=0.0333(18)$ and $ω=0.803(14)$ for $N=1$, against $0.62997097(12)$, $0.036297612(48)$ and $0.82968(23)$ from the conformal bootstrap; at $N=3$ the deviations grow, so that the uncertainty is now dominated by the resummation rather than by the coefficients. Every result is generated automatically from, or checked against, a public ledger of independent, exactly pooled runs, hosted on GitHub, to which anyone is welcome to contribute computing time.

</details>


### [123] [virasoro_blocks: Software for the 2d bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39106)
*David Poland*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 介绍 virasoro_blocks 软件：它可在任意中心电荷下计算适合数值 bootstrap 的 Virasoro 共形块，利用最优插值减少 Kac 极点，并给出标量 gap 与平均交换维数的数值界。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： Zamolodchikov 递推在椭圆 nome 的每个有限阶将共形块表示为正前因子乘以交换权重的有理函数，但大量 Kac 极点不利于半定规划式 bootstrap；因此需要高精度、支持任意精度并处理奇异中心电荷的计算工具。

方法： 在固定椭圆 nome 阶数下基于 Zamolodchikov 递推，采用最优插值在保持高精度的同时减少极点数量；软件支持任意精度并处理奇异中心电荷，包括最小模型和 c=1，还计算 global blocks、N=1 超共形块、torus characters 与 torus one-point blocks。

结果： 软件可计算任意中心电荷的 Virasoro 共形块；在仅假设标量 Virasoro 主场的条件下，标量 gap 的界逼近 (c-1)/16 与 (c-1)/12 之间的 Liouville 解窗口；在若干中心电荷下给出相同标量四点函数中平均交换维数的界，这些界具有丰富结构，许多已知二维 CFT 位于其边界上或附近。摘要未提供具体数值、精度指标或性能数据。

结论： virasoro_blocks 通过减少 Kac 极点并支持任意精度与奇异中心电荷，为数值 bootstrap 提供实用计算工具；所得标量 gap 和平均交换维数界呈现丰富结构，并与许多已知二维 CFT 的边界行为相符。

原始摘要： We introduce virasoro_blocks, software that computes Virasoro conformal blocks at any central charge in a form suitable for the numerical bootstrap. At each finite order in the elliptic nome, Zamolodchikov's recursion gives the block as a positive prefactor times a rational function of the exchanged weight. However, its many Kac poles make it difficult to use in semidefinite programming approaches to the bootstrap. In this software we use optimal interpolation to reduce the number of poles while keeping high accuracy. The software works in arbitrary precision and handles singular central charges, including the minimal models and $c=1$. It also computes global blocks, $N=1$ superconformal blocks, torus characters, and torus one-point blocks. We illustrate it with two applications. Assuming only scalar Virasoro primaries, we compute bounds on the scalar gap that approach the window of Liouville solutions between $(c-1)/16$ and $(c-1)/12$. We also bound the average exchanged dimension in four-point functions of identical scalars at several central charges. These bounds have a rich structure, and many known 2d CFTs lie on or near their boundary.

</details>


### [124] [Data Driven Modeling of a Magnetic Newton's Cradle using the Optimization of a Discrete Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39099)
*Dimitris Koumoutsakos, Sergey Litvinov*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究磁性牛顿摆的动力学，结合实验、正向模拟和逆向数据驱动建模，比较点偶极与有限电流环两种磁相互作用模型，并用ODIL从稀疏摆 trajectories 推断磁强度、阻尼等参数及量化模型形式误差。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 经典牛顿摆依赖瞬时弹性碰撞，而磁性牛顿摆以连续磁力替代碰撞，可能呈现更丰富的动力学；作者希望将其建立为可严格量化的计算逆建模测试平台，并评估稀疏、含噪观测下的参数推断与模型形式误差。

方法： 开展实验与正、逆向数据驱动模拟；比较点偶极模型和有限电流环模型，参数既由直接测量确定，也通过ODIL从摆轨迹反演；用合成三摆“孪生”系统验证，并测试仅观测一个摆的稀疏数据情形；通过残差和参数偏差进行模型比较。

结果： 在合成三摆系统中，ODIL可将离散轨迹复现到舍入误差精度，并能从稀疏数据准确推断磁强度和阻尼，即使只观测一个摆。点偶极模型通过低估磁矩补偿近场误差，并在每次动量传递处产生结构化残差；电流环模型消除该偏差，并在全精度记录下恢复磁体半径。连续磁耦合复现经典牛顿摆的关键行为，同时支持N个简正模式和静态排斥偏移。摘要未给出具体数值结果。

结论： ODIL能够从稀疏、含噪观测中推断物理参数并量化模型形式误差；磁性牛顿摆不仅是物理趣例，更是可严格量化的计算逆建模测试平台。

原始摘要： We investigate the dynamics of a magnetic Newton's cradle (MNC) using experiments, simulations through forward and inverse data driven models. In the MNC magnetic forces replace the instantaneous elastic collisions of the classical design. We compare point-dipole and finite-current-loop interaction models, identifying their parameters from direct measurements as well as through pendulum trajectories using the method of Optimizing a Discrete Loss (ODIL). For a synthetic three-pendulum twin, ODIL reproduces discretized trajectories to round-off accuracy and infers accurately magnet strength and damping from sparse data even when only one pendulum is observed. Model comparison shows that the point-dipole model compensates for near-field errors by underestimating the magnetic moment and produces structured residuals at each momentum transfer. The current-loop model removes this bias and, on full-precision recordings, also recovers the magnet radius. The continuous magnetic coupling reproduces the classical cradle's key behavior while enabling richer dynamics, including $N$ normal modes and a static repulsion offset. These results demonstrate that ODIL can infer physical parameters, and quantify model-form errors from sparse, noisy observations. Ultimately, this work establishes the magnetic Newton's cradle not merely as a physical curiosity, but as a rigorously quantifiable testbed for computational inverse modeling.

</details>


### [125] [Molecular Electrodynamic Relaxation in Dielectric and Plasmonic Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38992)
*Gang Meng, Tao E. Li*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对自洽Maxwell–Schrödinger模拟分子辐射时辐射自相互作用引起的数值不稳定，提出去除辐射自反作用纵向分量的方案，在经典Maxwell方程与实时密度泛函电子动力学及其紧束缚近似耦合的框架中实现稳定传播，并定量重现真空原子/分子辐射寿命及弱、强耦合腔修饰发射。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 分子辐射发射是决定激发态寿命并显著影响光物理动力学的基本弛豫过程；但直接开展自洽Maxwell–Schrödinger分子辐射模拟时，辐射自相互作用的计算存在数值不稳定，限制了对辐射寿命和腔修饰发射的第一性原理预测。

方法： 在经典Maxwell方程与实时密度泛函电子动力学及其紧束缚近似的自洽耦合传播中，仔细去除辐射自反作用的纵向分量，以消除相关数值不稳定；该框架可超越微扰论处理真空及光学腔中的辐射发射。

结果： 该框架定量重现真空中原子和分子发射体的辐射寿命，并捕获弱耦合与强耦合区域的腔修饰发射；对于弱耦合光学微腔，核热运动使Purcell效应在适度红失谐腔中达到最大；对于单个亚甲基蓝分子与真实纳米粒子-镜面等离激元腔强耦合，预测分子电动力学弛豫加速至约11飞秒，与非绝热核动力学直接竞争。

结论： 该工作建立了在多种介电环境中第一性原理设计材料辐射性质的途径；摘要未提供具体实验验证细节。

原始摘要： Molecular radiative emission is a fundamental relaxation process that governs excited-state lifetimes and significantly influences photophysical dynamics, but direct self-consistent Maxwell--Schrödinger simulations of molecular radiation suffer from numerical instabilities in evaluating radiative self-interaction. Herein, this issue is resolved within the self-consistent propagation of classical Maxwell's equations coupled to real-time density-functional electronic dynamics and their tight-binding approximation. By carefully removing the longitudinal components of radiative self-reaction, this framework quantitatively reproduces radiative lifetimes of atomic and molecular emitters in vacuum and further captures cavity-modified emission at weak and strong coupling regimes beyond perturbation theory. For molecules weakly coupled to an optical microcavity, the cavity Purcell effect is shown to reach maximum using a modestly red-detuned cavity due to nuclear thermal motion. Moreover, for a single methylene blue molecule strongly coupled to a realistic nanoparticle-on-mirror plasmonic cavity, the predicted molecular electrodynamical relaxation is accelerated to approximately 11 femtoseconds, directly competing with nonadiabatic nuclear dynamics. This work establishes a route to first-principles design of material radiative properties in a wide range of dielectric environments.

</details>


### [126] [A Differentiable, GPU-Accelerated Finite-Element Formulation in JAX for Large-Deformation Shells with Contact: Application to Patient-Specific Aortic Valve Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38075)
*Mohsen Nakhaei, Alison M. Pouch, Matthew A. Jolley, Wensi Wu*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文在 JAX 的 tatva 能量型有限元框架中构建可微、GPU 加速的主动脉瓣正向求解器，融合有限应变可伸长指向壳、超弹性本构与有限范围排斥接触能，并用正则化矩阵-free Newton-Krylov 方法和两级预处理求解平衡。三例患者特异性瓣膜模型（两例三叶、一例二叶）最终相对平衡残差均低于 10^-4，GPU 中位运行约 15–25 分钟，相对 CPU 加速 5.3–6.5 倍；摘要未给出与临床或体内数据的对比。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 患者特异性主动脉瓣模型可用于评估组织属性与几何如何影响瓣叶变形和对合，并有望辅助干预规划；但大变形、近不可压缩性和持续演化的接触使此类仿真计算代价高，且非线性收敛困难。可微、GPU 加速方法虽能高效求导并并行计算，但其在带接触的软组织壳问题中的矩阵-free 应用仍不成熟，因此需要建立并验证此类正向求解框架。

方法： 基于 tatva 的能量型有限元框架，在 JAX 中实现正向求解器：采用有限应变可伸长指向（extensible-director）壳单元、超弹性本构模型和有限作用范围的排斥接触能；非线性平衡由正则化的矩阵-free Newton-Krylov 方法求解，并配以两级预处理；利用自动微分计算切向量积，不显式形成或存储主切矩阵；GPU 并行化单元与积分点计算。验证包括：将本构、壳、接触与自接触响应同解析解、半解析解及已发表基准对比；在无接触板平衡算例中，将收敛顶点位移对本构参数、厚度和压力的隐式敏度与独立非线性仿真的有限差分结果对比；并运行三例患者特异性主动脉瓣模型（两例三叶瓣、一例二叶瓣）。

结果： 本构、壳、接触与自接触响应通过与解析、半解析和已发表基准的对比得到验证（摘要未给出具体误差数值）；无接触板平衡下，收敛顶点位移对本构参数、厚度和压力的隐式敏度与独立非线性仿真的有限差分结果一致；三例患者特异性瓣膜模型的最终相对平衡残差均低于 10^-4；GPU 中位运行时间约 15–25 分钟，在测试硬件上相对对应 CPU 运行获得 5.3–6.5 倍加速。

结论： 结果提供了部件级验证，并在所测试案例中演示了 GPU 加速的主动脉瓣正向仿真，为未来基于梯度的逆向研究提供支撑。摘要未报告整体临床验证、体内数据或逆向反演结果，其更广泛适用性仍需后续研究确认。

原始摘要： Patient-specific aortic-valve models can help assess how tissue properties and geometry influence leaflet deformation and coaptation, with potential to inform intervention planning. Large deformations, near-incompressibility, and evolving contact make these simulations computationally demanding and can challenge nonlinear convergence. Differentiable, GPU-accelerated methods offer efficient derivative evaluation and parallel computation, but their matrix-free application to soft-tissue shells with contact remains less established. We developed a forward solver using tatva's energy-based finite-element framework in JAX. The solver combines finite-strain extensible-director shells, hyperelastic constitutive models, and a finite-range repulsive contact energy. Nonlinear equilibrium is solved using a regularized, matrix-free Newton-Krylov method with two-level preconditioning. Automatic differentiation evaluates tangent-vector products without forming or storing the primary tangent matrix, while GPU execution parallelizes element and quadrature-point computations. Constitutive, shell, contact, and self-contact responses were assessed against analytical, semi-analytical, and published benchmarks. At a contact-free plate equilibrium, implicit sensitivities of the converged apex displacement to constitutive parameters, thickness, and pressure agreed with finite differences from independent nonlinear simulations. Simulations of three patient-specific aortic-valve models (two tricuspid and one bicuspid) achieved final relative equilibrium residuals below 10^-4. Median GPU runtimes were approximately 15-25 minutes, with 5.3-6.5-fold speedups relative to corresponding CPU runs on the tested hardware. These results provide component-level verification and demonstrate GPU-accelerated forward valve simulation in the tested cases, supporting future gradient-based inverse studies.

</details>


### [127] [MoireKP: An Automated Workflow for Symmetry-Constrained Moiré Continuum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37760)
*Yan Zhang, Hanqi Pi, Wangqian Miao, Zhong Fang, Hongming Weng, Xi Dai, B. Andrei Bernevig, Quansheng Wu*

分类： physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: MoireKP 将局域轨道第一性原理哈密顿量自动构建为谷分辨的紧凑 TAPW 连续模型，并在 AA 堆叠扭转双层 MoTe2、AB 堆叠扭转双层 ZrS2 和 A–AB 扭转三层 MoTe2 的 K、M、Γ 谷中，以不超过 3.0 meV 的误差复现 TAPW 能带。


<details>
  <summary>详细分析</summary>
研究动机： 第一性原理哈密顿量包含研究莫尔材料所需的电子信息，但其规模使后续计算代价高昂，因此需要一种自动化方法，从局域轨道从头算哈密顿量构建紧凑的连续模型。

方法： 提出 MoireKP 自动化工作流：构建谷分辨的截断原子平面波（TAPW）哈密顿量，选取低能基，并将晶体对称操作投影到该基中；由这些表示确定对称性允许的哈密顿量项，再通过将低能有效哈密顿量投影到相应算符基上获得系数。模型维度、局域参考态和諧波截断可由用户指定或自动选择；该基于哈密顿量的构造保留所选低能子空间中的波函数信息。工作流应用于 AA 堆叠扭转双层 MoTe2、AB 堆叠扭转双层 ZrS2 和 A–AB 扭转三层 MoTe2，覆盖 K、M 和 Γ 谷。

结果： 所有构建的模型在高对称 k 路径上的目标窗口内，均以 3.0 meV 以内的误差复现 TAPW 能带能量。

结论： MoireKP 可从局域轨道第一性原理哈密顿量自动生成紧凑、对称性约束的连续模型，并保留选定低能子空间中的波函数信息；在所测试的三种莫尔体系和 K、M、Γ 谷中，模型均高精度复现 TAPW 能带。摘要未提供计算效率或更大规模体系的定量结果。

原始摘要： First-principles Hamiltonians contain the electronic information needed to study moiré materials, but their size makes subsequent calculations costly. We present MoireKP, an automated workflow for constructing compact continuum models from localized-orbital ab initio Hamiltonians. The workflow constructs a valley-resolved truncated atomic-plane-wave (TAPW) Hamiltonian, selects a low-energy basis, and projects the crystal-symmetry operations into this basis. These representations determine the symmetry-allowed Hamiltonian terms, whose coefficients are obtained by projecting the low-energy effective Hamiltonian onto the corresponding operator basis. The model dimension, localized reference states, and harmonic cutoff can be selected automatically or specified by the user. This Hamiltonian-based construction retains wave-function information within the selected low-energy subspace. We apply the workflow to AA-stacked twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, AB-stacked twisted bilayer ZrS$_2$, and A--AB twisted trilayer MoTe$_2$, covering K, M, and $Γ$ valleys. All models reproduce TAPW band energies within 3.0 meV in the target windows along high-symmetry $k$ paths.

</details>
